- `a28895a` P0P.G2: Automation layer — scheduled unattended jobs + reconcile alarm. Six commands registered in `routes/console.php`, all withoutOverlapping()+onOneServer(), all active-tenants-only: credentials:refresh-status (02:10), nursing:materialize-visits (02:20), clinical:evaluate-recalls (02:30, NEW command — none existed), billing:dunning-run (06:00), billing:reconcile (06:30 = launch-blocker monitor), appointments:dispatch-reminders (*/15, enqueue only). Hardening: credentials:refresh-status was iterating ALL tenants incl. suspended (fixed); billing:dunning-run/billing:reconcile took REQUIRED tenantId+actorId so could not run unattended — args now optional, absent = sweep active tenants via new `SystemActorResolver` (lowest-id tenant-wide permission holder; never super-admin, never branch-scoped, SKIP not escalate). Reconcile alarm (`ReconciliationAlarm`): failed run leaves the append-only reconciliation_runs row + an error-level log + the `billing.reconciliation.alarm` tenant setting (period/run id/failed invariants); clears only when the SAME period later passes; a failing tenant never aborts the sweep and the command exits non-zero. Added `SettingsService::forget()` (set(null) is not equivalent — 'array' coerces null back to []). Prod runner documented: cron `schedule:run` every minute + Horizon under supervisor; local Windows cannot keep Horizon alive (no pcntl) — use schedule:work + queue:work. No page props/routes/payloads touched. 431 tests / 2820 assertions.

- `0c644a0` P0P.G6: Generic CSV patient import (column mapping, mandatory dry-run, dedup, audited commit). NEW `Modules\Import` (chosen over `Migration` to avoid DB-migration confusion; D-072). Tables `import_batches` (uploaded/mapped/validated/committed/failed; mapping+summary JSON; private tenant-prefixed storage_path; date_format; duplicate_policy) + `import_rows` (pending/valid/invalid/duplicate/imported/skipped; raw/errors/match JSON; matched_patient_id; created_entity_id; idx tenant/batch/status). Flow: ImportBatchService.upload (league/csv parse — BOM/quoting/delimiter-sniff/encoding-repair, NOT hand-rolled; private disk `tenants/{t}/imports/{ulid}.csv`, no public URL) → setMapping (column→field + explicit date format; required first_name/last_name/date_of_birth) → ImportValidator.validate (DRY-RUN: parse/validate per row, run existing DuplicateDetector, per-row status + batch summary, WRITES NOTHING) → ImportCommitter.commit (only on validated batch; creates via REAL PatientService incl contacts/identifiers/coverages — MRN/tenancy/validation/audit normal; duplicate policy default SKIP, else import_as_new / merge-via-audited-PatientMergeService; idempotent on batch+row status; one `patient.import.committed` audit event; chain verifies). PatientFieldMap catalog shapes rows → PatientService inputs + dedup demographics. RBAC `data.import` added to RbacProvisioner PERMISSIONS + org_admin template; every controller action Gate::authorize('data.import'). Net-new admin pages Import/Index.vue + Import/Upload.vue (upload→map→dry-run→review→policy→commit) on current shell/tokens (NOT the design pass; presentational, server-enforced); nav link + i18n keys added; no existing page contract changed. Arch test: Import may use Patients + Audit services, not Audit\Models/AiCore/Scheduling/Clinical/Nursing/Billing/Comms. Added league/csv ^9.28. tests/Feature/Import/ImportTest.php 9 tests: clean CSV → N patients via PatientService (MRNs/tenant/audited); missing required → invalid, not imported; existing match → duplicate+matched_patient_id+reasons, skipped; dry-run writes nothing; commit-twice idempotent; tenant isolation (no cross-tenant match/visibility); RBAC 403 without data.import; malformed/mis-encoded CSV graceful; private disk no public URL. composer check green; npm run build green.
- `dad3ac9` P0P.G9: Waitlist auto-fill workflow (offer, notify, accept-through-booking). Extends Modules\Scheduling. NEW `waitlist_offers` table (BelongsToTenant: waitlist_entry_id, source_appointment_id, patient_id, service_id, branch_id, slot_starts/ends_at, resource_ids JSON, status offered/accepted/declined/expired, offered_by, offered_at, expires_at, responded_at, booked_appointment_id) + `WaitlistOffer` model. `WaitlistOfferService`: candidates() reuses WaitlistService::matchingForSlot (service/branch/window, tenant-scoped); offer() creates a time-boxed hold (TTL = scheduling.waitlist.offer_ttl_minutes default 30; one open offer per entry) + fires WaitlistOfferLifecycleChanged; accept() books via EXISTING BookingService::book (no-double-book resource lock) then marks entry booked + offer accepted (expiry checked/recorded OUTSIDE the booking txn so the throw can't roll it back); decline()/expire()/expireDue() release the hold, entry stays waiting for the next candidate. Notify path composed in APP LAYER (listener on WaitlistOfferLifecycleChanged audits waitlist_offer.* and, on creation only, sends the new `waitlist.offer` built-in template — TRANSACTIONAL, consent-gated on comms.email, skips fail-closed without consent) via Comms NotificationService — Scheduling never imports Comms (arch test unchanged; D-073). `ExpireWaitlistOffersCommand` (scheduling:expire-waitlist-offers, every 5 min, withoutOverlapping+onOneServer, active tenants). Reception UI additive on the day-board (net-new): waitlistOffers prop + offer/candidates/accept/decline action URLs + a "Waitlist auto-fill" panel (find candidates → one-click offer → accept/decline; status visible); appointment summary gains service_id; no existing prop removed; i18n keys; RBAC appointment.manage on every endpoint. Tests: tests/Feature/Scheduling/WaitlistOfferTest.php (8) — cancel surfaces matching entries (service/branch/window, tenant-scoped); offer notifies consent-gated (skipped no_consent / sent); accept books via BookingService + entry booked + chain verifies; decline frees slot for next candidate; expired offer does not book; expire sweep; RBAC (billing role refused); tenant isolation + audited — plus WaitlistOfferHammerTest (1) AttemptOfferAcceptCommand × 6 processes on one freed slot → exactly one ACCEPTED, 5 CONFLICT, 1 appointment. composer check green; npm run build green.

- `e9aeb66` P0P.G15: MySQL 8 full-parity verification + portability sweep (D-081; verified top commit 364c4fa P0P.G14 before starting). CONFIRMATORY + hardening; found TWO real problems. (1) CI HAD BEEN RED SINCE P0P.G7 — 8 commits, unnoticed (gates ran local checks only). Triage via the GitHub API (job log of run 29591093078): 2 failures, both 429s in CheckInTest — NOT an engine bug. Root cause: CI exports CACHE_STORE=redis at the OS level and phpunit <env> cannot override OS env (same class as the P0G.G2 queue incident) → throttle counters persist in REAL Redis across tests → the kiosk rate-limit test exhausts the 10/min bucket for later kiosk tests. REPRODUCED LOCALLY on MariaDB with CACHE_STORE=redis (identical 2 failures → engine-independent). Fix: Cache::flush() per test in CheckInTest beforeEach (config-pinning the store is NOT enough — Fortify named limiters resolve the RateLimiter singleton at boot, freezing its store; documented in the file + DB-PARITY). Proven: green under CACHE_STORE=redis twice back-to-back + normal path. (2) REAL ENGINE DIVERGENCE found by the sweep: information_schema on MariaDB shows implicit ON UPDATE CURRENT_TIMESTAMP on 9 first-non-nullable-TIMESTAMP columns (MariaDB explicit_defaults_for_timestamp=OFF vs MySQL 8 ON). Six harmless (append-only, UPDATE trigger-blocked: ai_interactions/integrity_checks/messages/payment_allocations/reconciliation_runs/refunds). THREE reachable: patient_consents.granted_at (consent WITHDRAWAL rewrote the grant moment to now() on MariaDB, preserved on MySQL 8 — real data bug), portal_login_tokens.expires_at (rewritten on consumption), thread_reads.read_at (masked; every update sets it explicitly — a trap). Fix: 2 migrations (Patients 2026_07_17_000010: granted_at + expires_at → DATETIME; Comms 2026_07_17_300010: read_at → DATETIME) per the standing D-E4 rule. NEW tests/Feature/Platform/MutableMomentParityTest.php (3): withdrawal preserves granted_at + consumption preserves expires_at (both FAILED on MariaDB pre-fix, pass post-fix) + engine-independent information_schema guard (no non-append-only table may carry an on-update column — fails on whichever engine regresses). Sweep results (each verified): FULLTEXT ngram → try-WITH PARSER ngram/fallback in patients migration ✓; spatial → nullable location + NOT NULL location_index mirror for the SPATIAL index + ST_Distance_Sphere/ST_GeomFromText both engines (MariaDB ≥10.4.19) ✓; CHECK constraints (5) enforced identically both engines ✓; triggers all SIGNAL 45000, ImmutabilitySweepTest PASS on MySQL 8 in the CI log (all 15 protected tables + positive sides) ✓; audit_events RANGE partitioning builds in CI ✓; NO generated columns, NO enum columns ✓; charset/collation pinned utf8mb4/utf8mb4_unicode_ci in config ✓; sql_mode strict=true → identical explicit mode list both engines ✓. Fresh-MySQL8 migrate: CI "Run migrations" step SUCCESS on the clean mysql:8 service; NEW ci.yml step asserts ZERO PENDING via migrate:status (fails on "Pending"). NEW composer script test:mysql = migrate:fresh --force + migrate:status + full pest (manual re-verification against a THROWAWAY MySQL 8; documented). docs/DB-PARITY.md: engines, all 10 divergence classes + handling, one-step verification commands, CI-env-divergence playbook. MySQL 8 suite numbers: pre-fix CI run (P0P.G14 tree) = 2 failed, 575 passed (4090 assertions) — the two cache-env failures only, everything else incl. hammers/airplane/simulated-month/immutability green; post-fix G15 CI run checked after push (expected to match local). Local MariaDB composer check green (Pint, PHPStan L5 OK, Pest full suite incl. the 3 new parity tests).

PAYMENTS: record→PaymentService::record (methods bank_transfer/card/cash/other; NO PSP — records a receipt), allocate→::allocate, reverse→::reverseAllocation — every move APPEND-ONLY; the service's over-allocation / reversal guards are caught as InvalidArgumentException and surfaced as `withErrors`, NEVER reimplemented; remainder=::unallocated, open=::openBalance. DUNNING: worklist (open>0, series=INV, due<today) + persisted dunning_events, "send reminders"→DunningService::evaluate(currentTenant, now()) — idempotent, legal-comms (not consent-gated), fee=new charge, original untouched. REPORTING: facts-only dashboard over ReportingService::summary (operational+throughput+financial-only-with-billing.view), neutral styling, NO judgment/target/trend fields. HARD RULE held + adversarially verified by grep: NO financial math in any controller/view — every `_minor` is a service call or a model-attribute passthrough; the only view arithmetic is money-format `/100`, rate-format `*100` on a service-returned ratio, and major→minor INPUT normalisation. Prototype omissions FLAGGED not faked: TWINT/QR-bill→backend methods; PSP/card-capture/receipt-email, camt.053 auto-match, AI-drafted reminders + approval-escalation ladder, per-patient running-balance ledger, and every Practice-Reporting-Hub judgment metric (DSO/collection-rate/case-acceptance/recall-compliance/targets/trends/provider-ranking/sparklines) all OMITTED. NEW tests only: `tests/Feature/Billing/BillingUiPart2Test.php` (9 tests — RBAC matrix incl. coordinator + view-only, tenant 404, record→allocation appended + invoice frozen, allocation-cannot-exceed-remainder surfaced on BOTH the store+allocate paths, reversal appended + reason-required + manager-only, new-invoice→gapless number, dunning idempotent, reporting facts-only with a recursive no-judgment scan); the frozen payment/dunning/reconciliation/hammer/metrics suite is UNCHANGED. An adversarial 5-dimension review→skeptic-verify workflow found + FIXED 3 real defects (medium: store()'s rejected immediate-allocation error surfaced nowhere → now a `page.props.errors` banner on the payment detail + a regression test; low: new-invoice preview currency derived from SettingsService, not hardcoded EUR; low: Record-payment / New-invoice buttons now `can_manage`-gated), 0 remaining. - `425357c` FIX.1: resolve C-1 (QA-audit delivery-blocker) — billing + import DETAIL and WRITE routes 500'd in the real browser (`TenantContextMissingException` at route-model-binding time). ROOT: `IdentifyTenantFromUser` is `append`-ed to the web group in `bootstrap/app.php`, so it runs AFTER Laravel's `SubstituteBindings`; a controller that IMPLICITLY binds a `BelongsToTenant` model (`show(Invoice $invoice)`) resolves it during `SubstituteBindings` — before the tenant context exists — and the fail-closed global scope throws. FIX: converted 12 actions to `string $id` + in-controller `Model::query()->whereKey($id)->firstOrFail()` (the app-wide convention, cf. `PatientShowController`/`ClinicalChartController`): InvoiceController show/issue/creditNote/download, CreditNoteController show, PaymentController show/allocate/reverse, ImportBatchController show/mapping/validateBatch/commit (import was PRE-EXISTING). Missing/cross-tenant id → 404 (fail-closed preserved); routes/URLs + all downstream service calls byte-identical; NO billing/payment/dunning LOGIC changed. App-wide grep confirmed billing + import were the ONLY implicit-bound tenant models (every other detail controller already uses string ids). NEW `tests/Feature/RouteBindingTenantContextTest.php` (3 tests / 16 assertions) hits the routes with `TenantContext::forget()` after seeding (before the request) so `IdentifyTenantFromUser` establishes context the way a real request does — CONFIRMED it FAILS (500 at TenantScope.php:38) on the old code and PASSES on the fix, and asserts 404 for missing/cross-tenant. TEST-GAP LESSON: the W6/W7 feature tests pre-set the `TenantContext` singleton in fixtures, masking the request-time ordering (green CI, broken browser). Existing behavior tests UNCHANGED. VERIFIED: composer check FULLY green (Pint passed · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · Pest **601 passed / 4519 assertions**, 0 failed). (D-090)
- `95a16ac` FIX.2: wire the staff landing (`/app`) to real MetricsService figures (M-1, from the QA audit) — the landing was an unwired placeholder ("—" / "Awaiting live data") despite real data, the first screen a customer sees. NEW `App\Http\Controllers\AppLandingController` (`GET /app`, replacing the prop-less closure) assembles a TODAY, tenant-wide view-model from the EXISTING `MetricsService` (P0P.G14): appointments (+ `by_status`), waiting (ARRIVED count), no-shows, active patients — all only with `reporting.view`; outstanding balance — only with `billing.view`. Each metric is called CONDITIONALLY on `Gate::allows` (MetricsService throws without the perm), so a role with neither (e.g. reception) gets the shell with both props `null` — NEVER a 500. NO new metric or query was invented — only existing service output, scoped to today + current tenant; money stays integer minor units. `App/Landing.vue` rebound: the glance tile + 4 KPI `StatCard`s (operational) + an Outstanding card (financial) + a today's-schedule strip (real count + status chips, or a genuine "Nothing scheduled today yet" at zero — distinct from the old "awaiting data" stub) + quick links. The landing date is parsed as LOCAL midnight (`T00:00:00`) so it never shifts a day. i18n keys only. NEW `tests/Feature/AppLandingTest.php` (3 tests / 71 assertions): real figures for a manager with data (assertInertia values), genuine zeros for an empty tenant (operational present, counts 0 — not the stub), and RBAC gating (coordinator → operational-only, billing role → financial-only, reception → both null, page still renders). Existing behavior tests UNCHANGED (AppShellTest's component-only + suspended-tenant-403 assertions still hold). VERIFIED: npm build green; composer check FULLY green (Pint passed · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · Pest **604 passed / 4590 assertions**, 0 failed). Wires the P.14 service; see [[Reporting]].
- `2fa85e7` FIX.3: fix M-2 (date-only timezone shift, from the QA audit) + BROWSER-VERIFY the C-1 billing fix end-to-end. **PART A — M-2:** date-only values (DOB, invoice issue/due dates, AR "as of") were rendered with `new Date("1954-03-12")`, which parses as **UTC** midnight → a viewer behind UTC sees the day BEFORE (Erika's DOB `03/11/1954` on the patients index vs stored `1954-03-12`; invisible for Swiss UTC+1, wrong for any behind-UTC viewer). Same class as the W6 `isOverdue` fix. NEW shared helper `resources/js/lib/date.ts` — `formatDateOnly()` / `ageFromDateOnly()` — parses a `^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$` string as **LOCAL** midnight (`` `${value}T00:00:00` ``) so the calendar day never shifts; a value with a time component passes through unchanged. Wired into **only date-only renders**: `Patients/Index` (DOB + age), `Clinical/Chart` (age), and the six billing pages (`Invoices/Index`+`Show`, `Payments/Index`, `Dunning/Index`, `CreditNotes/Index`, `Aging`) — **datetime (timestamped) rendering deliberately NOT touched** (encounter/message/access-log instants must localise; the Chart encounter `started_at` grouping left as-is). NEW root `vitest.config.ts` (`@`→`resources/js`, TZ pinned `America/Los_Angeles`) + `npm run test:unit` script + `resources/js/lib/date.test.ts` (**7 tests green**) incl. a self-validating assertion that the naive parse yields `03/11` in that zone while the helper yields `03/12`. The nurse-PWA keeps its own `test:pwa` config (untouched). **PART B — browser-verified C-1** (Playwright MCP, Chromium in **America/Los_Angeles**, real TOTP 2FA login as org_admin Andrea, fresh `migrate:fresh`+DemoClinic+DemoSpitex seed) — every flow the QA audit found 500ing now PASSES: invoice **detail** (no 500, string-ULID route), **issue** draft→gapless **INV-7**, **credit note**→**CN-2** with original **INV-5 untouched** (number/dates/3 lines/subtotal 159.00/VAT 10.61/total 169.61 unchanged; only balance→0 / status→"Credit-noted"), **payment** 100.00 recorded + **allocated** to INV-1 → balance **313.00→213.00** (status "Partially paid"), **over-allocation** of 150 from a 100 payment **rejected** ("Cannot allocate more than the payment unallocated remainder"; unallocated stays 100), **PDF** download (`200 application/pdf`, `%PDF-`, `INV-1.pdf`), CSV **import dry-run** (map→validate→both rows valid, **writes nothing** — 0 `Testimport` patients after). M-2 spot-check LIVE: Erika's DOB renders **`03/12/1954`** where the buggy `new Date` path yields `03/11/1954` in the same browser. Findings: **no flow failed** — nothing fixed beyond M-2 scope. `docs/QA-AUDIT-REPORT.md` gains a "§7 FIX-verify" section (C-1 confirmed-in-browser table + M-2). `.playwright-mcp/` added to `.gitignore`. Existing behavior tests UNCHANGED. VERIFIED: npm build green; `npm run test:unit` **7 passed**; composer check FULLY green (Pint passed · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · Pest **604 passed / 4590 assertions**, 0 failed). Closes the audit's Critical (re-confirmed) + M-2; see [[Billing]], [[Patients]], [[Clinical]]. (D-091)

- `ed55004` FIX.5: add a route-reachability SMOKE that drives every major route through the REAL middleware stack in CI, so a request-time 500 (the C-1 class) can never ship green again — the systemic guard the QA audit recommended. TEST-INFRA + CI ONLY, no app logic touched, existing tests unchanged. NEW `tests/Feature/Smoke/RouteSmokeTest.php` (3 tests): (1) seeds the demo clinic and, as org_admin (superset role), hits EVERY major GET route — landings, patients index/show/register, day-board, dispatch, competencies, inbox, clinical chart/encounter/note/note-edit/orders/snippets, billing index + **detail** (invoice/CN/payment show — the C-1 surface) + aging/dunning/new-invoice/**PDF**, reporting, CSV import index/create/**show**, admin/kiosks, public booking — asserting each returns **200 (never a 500/419)**; (2) per-role RBAC smoke (doctor/nurse/coordinator/reception/billing each reach their pages 200 and are denied others 403 — incl. reception → **403 on `/billing/invoices` by URL**, org_admin → 403 on `/admin`); (3) a portal patient reaches all 7 portal pages (200). **The C-1-catching mechanism:** `TenantContext::forget()` BEFORE each request, so `IdentifyTenantFromUser` must re-establish context via the middleware exactly like an independent browser request — the exact condition C-1 exploited and the pre-seeded W6/W7 feature tests masked. It is the generalisation of the FIX.1 regression test (which 500'd on the old implicit-binding controllers), so a re-introduced request-time 500 fails this smoke. **Request-level Pest, NOT a headless browser:** chosen for reliability — runs in the existing MySQL-8 Pest job on every push, deterministic + fast (~46s), exercises the identical middleware pipeline C-1 broke, with none of the artisan-serve/browser-install/TOTP-timing flakiness browser-in-CI would add (the gate's sanctioned fallback; the C-1 class is a server-side 500 fully covered here). Wired into CI as a dedicated fast-fail step `Route smoke (real middleware stack…)` running `composer test:smoke` BEFORE `composer check` (fails in ~1 min if a route 500s), and it also runs inside the full suite. NEW scripts `composer test:smoke` + `npm run test:smoke` (local run); QA report gains a §8 documenting the guard. Maintainability: a single route list in the test — a new page is one line. VERIFIED: `composer test:smoke` green (3 tests); composer check FULLY green (Pint passed · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · Pest **610 passed / 4624 assertions**, 0 failed); ci.yml step is a clean mirror of the existing steps. See [[D-093]], [[Platform]].

- `48e385f` CLINIC.W8b: BUILD the settings BACKENDS a clinic needs day one (the W8 discovery found no write path) — editable practice profile, branch CRUD, per-branch opening hours, timezone. Genuine domain work; SCHEDULING SAFETY is the key risk (branches + hours feed the booking engine), guarded + tested. Mapped first with 3 parallel Explore agents (booking/slot engine, branch references, tenant/audit/migration). **Profile:** NEW nullable `tenants` columns (contact_email/phone, address_line1/2, city, postal_code, country) via a Platform migration; editable through `SettingsController::updateProfile` (name + contact/address → tenant columns; locale + timezone → SettingsService). slug/region/status/plan stay READ-ONLY (slug = public `/book/{slug}` key, region immutable, status/plan platform/billing). **Timezone/locale applied:** NEW `App\Http\Middleware\ApplyTenantLocaleTimezone` (web group, after IdentifyTenantFromUser) runs `date_default_timezone_set()` + `app()->setLocale()` from the tenant settings — it NEVER touches `config('app.timezone')`, so Eloquent keeps serialising UTC (stored data unchanged); surfaced on Inertia's `locale`/`timezone` as LAZY closures (Inertia evaluates `share()` before the middleware, so eager values missed it). A tenant that set nothing keeps platform defaults → existing behaviour untouched. Full per-widget datetime→tz display flagged as a follow-up. **Branch CRUD:** NEW `branch_hours` table + `BranchHours` model (per-weekday, validated in booted like ResourceAvailability) + `BranchHoursService`; NEW **app-layer** `App\Http\Controllers\BranchController` + `App\Services\BranchService` — app layer because the deactivation guard spans Platform's Branch + Scheduling's appointments/resources and `arch('Platform does not depend on Scheduling')` forbids it inside Platform. **SCHEDULING SAFETY (two tested guards):** (1) deactivation is SOFT (`active=false`, never a hard delete — appointments/encounters/charges/visits `restrictOnDelete` a branch) and BLOCKED when the branch has future active appointments (blockingStatuses, starts_at ≥ now); the day-board + portal branch lists now filter `active=true` (public booking already did) so a deactivated branch vanishes from every booking surface. (2) opening hours feed the engine: `AvailableSlotFinder` bounds its scan to the branch's [open, close] for the weekday (closed day → no slots), and `BookingService::createBooking` (the authoritative funnel for book/bookOnline/series/waitlist) + `checkAvailability` reject a start outside hours (NEW `BookingUnavailableException::outsideBranchHours`). BACKWARD-COMPATIBLE: a branch with NO configured hours keeps the default 07:00–19:00 and imposes no booking constraint, so every existing scheduling test (none set hours) stays green. All writes admin.manage-gated, tenant-scoped (cross-tenant → 404), audited via app-layer model hooks (branch.created/updated/activated/deactivated, branch.hours_changed, tenant.profile_updated — Platform never imports Audit). NEW Vue: editable `Admin/Settings.vue` (profile + identity + billing + branch summary) + `Admin/Branches.vue` (create + per-branch details/hours-grid/deactivate); `branchesAdmin.*` i18n. GAP flagged: adding resources (rooms/chairs) to a branch has no backend, so a new branch isn't bookable until resources are seeded. NEW tests: `tests/Feature/Platform/BranchAndProfileTest.php` (10 — profile edit+audit+gate+validation, branch create/edit/code-unique/gate, **deactivation BLOCKED with future appts**, empty-branch deactivate removes from day-board, cross-tenant 404, hours saved+audited+invalid-window, timezone applied+surfaced) + `tests/Feature/Scheduling/BranchHoursSlotTest.php` (4 — unconfigured keeps default window, configured hours bound slots, closed day = no slots, booking rejected outside/accepted inside). Route smoke gains `/admin/branches`. NO money/clinical/existing-behaviour changed; reconciliation/fence/hammer/audit suites green. VERIFIED: npm build green; composer check FULLY green (Pint passed · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · Pest **633 passed / 4753 assertions**, 0 failed). See [[D-095]], [[Platform]], [[Scheduling]].

- `f9e0da8` CLINIC.W9: BUILD the Governance dashboard + AI approval-queue — the two MOST safety-sensitive admin screens, as READ/ACT WINDOWS onto tested backends (no new autonomy, no audit-mutation, no fence bypass, no reimplementation of approval logic), P0D.GU. DISCOVERY first (read the real code): AiCore `ApprovalQueue::approve/reject` (re-authorizes the reviewer against the TOOL's own permission + `assertPending`; reject executes nothing; approve runs only `tool->execute()`; both fire `AgentActionLifecycleChanged` → app-layer glue audits `agent_action.*`/`ai_interaction.*`), `AutonomyPolicy` (off/suggest/approve/auto, clinical/financial hard-capped at approve, applied at PROPOSE time), `AgentAction`; Audit `AuditService::verifyChain(?tenantId)` (pure replay, writes nothing) + `AuditEvent` (read-only, **NO `BelongsToTenant`** — Audit may not depend on Platform); Platform `IntegrityCheck` (D-069) + `Setting`/`KillSwitch`; Billing `ReconciliationRun` (D-068) + `ReconciliationEngine::check` (pure read); permission catalog (`audit.view`/`ai.manage` exist, org_admin holds both); + the `Governance Dashboard`/`Admin Approval Queue` prototypes (extracted labels, not markup). Both controllers live in the APP LAYER (`App\Http\Controllers\GovernanceDashboardController` + `AiApprovalQueueController`) because they compose Audit+Platform+Billing+AiCore. **PART A — Governance (`/governance`, `audit.view`, STRICTLY READ-ONLY):** displays posture from EXISTING data — live `verifyChain()` + latest scheduled `IntegrityCheck`; latest `ReconciliationRun` + persisted `billing.reconciliation.alarm`; AI-usage outcome counts + integer-minor cost over the append-only `ai_interactions` ledger (30-day window); pending-`AgentAction` depth; kill-switch state (via `KillSwitch::enabled()`); recent + security-relevant audit events (DISPLAYED, never editable). NO mutation path (every source append-only at model+trigger; controller only reads); the one POST ("verify now") re-runs the existing verification and appends NOTHING (tested: audit-event count unchanged). **`AuditEvent` filtered by `tenant_id` EXPLICITLY** (no auto-scope) — the isolation guarantee (tested cross-tenant). **PART B — AI approval-queue (`/governance/approvals`, `ai.manage`, READ + ACT-THROUGH-EXISTING-PATH):** lists PENDING agent actions (agent/tool/category/autonomy/why/source-grounded proposed output, AI-draft badged); approve/reject go ONLY through `ApprovalQueue` — NO new execute path, NO create/propose route (a human can't inject an un-fenced action; the fence refuses clinical asks at propose time), NEVER sets autonomy (the request body can't raise it — tested). THE CAP BINDS: `ApprovalQueue` re-authorizes the reviewer against the tool's own permission, so a reviewer with `ai.manage` but lacking a tool's permission (e.g. `appointment.manage`) is DENIED (403 — the controller lets `AuthorizationException` propagate and catches only `AiCoreException`); reject executes nothing; every decision is audited by the EXISTING glue (controller adds no audit). Actions resolve by STRING id (FIX.1/D-090) → cross-tenant/missing = 404. NEW `Governance/Dashboard.vue` + `Governance/ApprovalQueue.vue` (Eucalyptus Glow — StatCards, glass cards, status badges, per-card approve/reject-with-reason), two nav entries (`audit.view`/`ai.manage`, added to `HandleInertiaRequests::NAV_PERMISSIONS`), `governance.*`+`aiQueue.*` i18n. NEW tests: `tests/Feature/Governance/GovernanceDashboardTest.php` (4 — read-only+real-status+gated, tenant-scoped activity isolation, verify-now writes-nothing + no mutation route + model immutable, non-audit-viewer denied) + `AiApprovalQueueTest.php` (4 — lists pending+gated+tenant-scoped+no-create-route, approve-through-existing-path+audited+autonomy-not-raisable, reject-does-nothing+reason-required+audited, cap-binds-via-UI + cross-tenant 404). Route smoke gains both GET routes (org_admin 200 / reception 403). Completeness fix in passing: `AgentAction` `@property` block gained the real review-timestamp/reason columns (approved_at/rejected_at/executed_at/rejection_reason — in fillable+casts but undocumented, so L5 flagged them) — same as W8's Branch fix, docblock only, no behavior. ONE existing test updated (tracking, not weakening — same category as W8's admin.manage add): `NavAndErrorPageTest`'s exact nav-permissions map gained `audit.view`+`ai.manage` (the two new nav links are gated on them). The P.4 eval harness + audit/integrity/immutability suites are UNCHANGED and green. VERIFIED: npm build green; `composer test:smoke` green; composer check FULLY green (Pint passed · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · Pest **649 passed / 4902 assertions**, 0 failed). Closes two founder-scope admin gaps (governance + AI approval-queue); remaining unwired: KB admin, staff-telehealth join. See [[D-097]], [[AiCore]], [[Platform]].

DISCOVERY first (mirrored the exact patterns): module registration (Reporting/Clinical providers + composer autoload + bootstrap/providers), the append-only clinical model (`OrderResult` — BelongsToTenant + LogsReads + DB SIGNAL-45000 triggers), the arch boundary tests, `RbacProvisioner` (catalog + role templates; `RbacTest` asserts permission COUNT relatively → adding a permission is safe). **Built (domain + service + tests only, NO UI — chart UI is G2):** registered `Modules\Dental` (plain internal module D-012; `DentalServiceProvider` boot=loadMigrationsFrom; composer psr-4 + bootstrap/providers; arch: `Dental may use care modules + Audit services but not Audit models, AiCore, Nursing, or Comms`). **Tooth notation = FDI/ISO 3950** two-digit (`Support\ToothNotation`, pure) — permanent 11–18/21–28/31–38/41–48 (32) + primary 51–55/61–65/71–75/81–85 (20, family dentist charts children); dentition DERIVED from the id (never stored); a patient's tooth set = whatever teeth have records (missing = a charted state, mixed dentition = both — no hardcoded 32-tooth assumption). **`tooth_records`** (BelongsToTenant, **APPEND-ONLY** at model `updating`/`deleting`-throw + DB triggers `tooth_records_no_update`/`_no_delete` SIGNAL-45000 portable): one immutable charting row per tooth-or-surface moment — `tooth` (FDI), `surface` (nullable, null=whole-tooth), `charted_condition` (a fact the dentist SELECTED from an allowed vocabulary per scope: WHOLE_TOOTH present/missing/unerupted/implant/pontic/crown/root_canal/bridge_retainer vs SURFACE sound/caries/restoration/fracture/sealant/veneer/erosion/abrasion), `note`, `reason` (a correction is a NEW row + reason, prior states never destroyed), `charted_by`, `charted_at`. **current odontogram = latest per (tooth, surface); history = every row.** **ELECTRIC FENCE (record-not-judge, same as vitals D-D3 / order results D-076):** DELIBERATELY no severity/score/risk/grade/abnormal/flag/priority/recommendation column — the system records what the dentist charts, never detects caries / grades decay / assesses risk / diagnoses (asserted by a schema + recursive-output fence test). `Services\ToothChartService` pure record+retrieve: `chart()` (Gate `dental.chart`, actor+patient same-tenant fail-closed, deterministic FDI/surface/condition validation, audited `dental.tooth_charted`), `currentChart()`/`history()` (Gate `patient.view`, patient-scoped `read` audit). RBAC: NEW `dental.chart` permission granted to org_admin + doctor (the treating-clinician role = the general dentist; dentist/hygienist/assistant split is a later gate; reception/nurse refused). NEW tests: `tests/Feature/Dental/ToothChartTest.php` (6 — record+scope+audit+read-log; state-change preserves prior + append-only at model AND raw-DB; FDI valid permanent+primary + invalid id/condition/surface rejected; schema+output NO interpretation field [fence]; cross-tenant isolation; RBAC reception/nurse refused, org_admin allowed) + `Dental` arch boundary. NO existing behavior changed; the P.4 eval / reconciliation / immutability / audit suites stay green UNCHANGED. - `1f4e653` DENTAL.G4: perform-a-procedure workflow (clinical record + charge + tooth-state, atomic). The dentist records a performed procedure on a tooth → the clinical record AND the charge (via G3) AND any tooth-state change (via G1) in ONE coherent action. Ties G1 (tooth model) + G2 (odontogram) + G3 (catalog+billing). Per `docs/DENTAL-DELIVERY-MAP.md`. The charge reconciles-to-the-unit like any other (tested). Append-only: a correction is a NEW performed record; the prior is preserved + the model is immutable (tested at model + raw-DB). **UI:** the G2 odontogram extended ADDITIVELY — a "Perform a procedure" side-panel form (procedure + branch + optional resulting tooth-state + note, shown only when `can_perform` = dental.chart && billing.manage) + a per-tooth performed-procedure history; `OdontogramController::perform` (POST `/dental/chart/{patient}/perform`, string-id FIX.1). PRESENTATIONAL (P0D.GU) — the service owns all logic. NEW `dental.perform.*`/`dental.performed.*` i18n. **NO G3 code touched** (the mapping is a perform-time input, so the catalog needed no change). NO new billing/charting logic — adversarial grep still finds zero pricing/charge/VAT math in `Modules\Dental`; G1 append-only preserved. The dentist builds a phased plan whose estimate REUSES the G3 fee schedule (snapshotted at proposal), estimates WITHOUT billing (G4 performing charges), and links performed procedures back to plan items to track completion. Ties G3 (pricing) + G4 (perform). Per `docs/DENTAL-DELIVERY-MAP.md`; critical path G1→G5. DISCOVERY first (confirmed the G3 `DentalProcedure->tariffItem->unit_price_minor` fee path, the `ServiceAgreementService` legal-transition state-machine pattern, and G4's `PerformProcedureService::perform` signature). **Built (domain + service + UI):** NEW `treatment_plans` (BelongsToTenant, LogsReads, NOT append-only — a lifecycle) → `treatment_plan_phases` → `treatment_plan_items` (a planned procedure = a G3 `dental_procedure` + tooth/surface + `estimated_fee_minor`); + additive nullable `performed_procedures.treatment_plan_item_id` (ALTER is DDL — the G4 append-only UPDATE trigger doesn't fire). `Services\TreatmentPlanService`: `create/addPhase/addItem` (draft-only, gate `dental.chart`, sequence=max+1); **`propose` SNAPSHOTS each item's `estimated_fee_minor` from the live tariff fee (READ through the existing store — no recompute) then transitions draft→proposed — so a later fee-schedule edit NEVER changes an accepted plan's agreed estimate (tested UP-edit → estimate unchanged)**; `accept`(stamps accepted_at)/`decline`/`start`/`complete` via a legal-only `transition`/`canTransition` match machine (illegal throws `DentalException`; completed/declined terminal — tested draft→accept + completed→start + declined→accept all throw); `itemEstimate` = snapshot ?? live fee; phase/plan totals = `->sum(itemEstimate)` (the ONLY arithmetic — adversarial grep finds NO pricing/charge/VAT/discount math in `Modules\Dental`, only pass-throughs + this sum). **NO DOUBLE-CHARGE (tested): the plan ESTIMATES; accept/start post ZERO charges** — a charge is created ONLY when the procedure is PERFORMED (G4). **Link to G4:** `PerformProcedureService::perform` gained an optional `?TreatmentPlanItem $planItem = null` (G4's atomic 3-write workflow otherwise UNCHANGED — all existing G4 calls/tests unaffected) that validates same-tenant + `planItem->treatmentPlan->patient_id === patient->id` and stamps the link; a plan item is "done" when a performed procedure references it (DERIVED, no stored flag). **ELECTRIC FENCE: dentist-authored** — no auto-suggested procedures, no severity prioritisation, no AI; the service records what the dentist adds + sums fees; the rendered payload carries no severity/score/grade/risk/suggested/ai field (recursive `tpAssertNoJudgment`). **RBAC:** manage = `dental.chart`; read = `patient.view`; perform a planned item = dental.chart + billing.manage (via G4). **UI:** `Http\Controllers\TreatmentPlanController` + `Dental/TreatmentPlans.vue` (`/dental/plans/{patient}`, string-id FIX.1) staff editor (build phases/items draft-only, lifecycle buttons mirroring the server machine, per-item perform-a-planned-item form) + `PortalTreatmentPlanController` + `Portal/TreatmentPlan.vue` (`/portal/treatment-plan`) read-only patient view (proposed-onward only, per-plan `auditRead`, no actions/PSP). PRESENTATIONAL (P0D.GU) — the service owns all logic. NEW `treatmentPlan.*`+`portalTreatmentPlan.*` i18n. **PHPStan L5 forced:** a `default => throw` arm on the validated-action `match` (mixed subject); `tariffItem->` not `tariffItem?->` (Larastan types the belongsTo non-null — `nullsafe.neverNull`). NEW `tests/Feature/Dental/TreatmentPlanTest.php` (5 — estimate snapshot-at-propose + fee-edit-doesn't-change-accepted; no-double-charge [accept/start = 0 charges] + perform links item + marks done; legal-only lifecycle; RBAC [reception can't author / billing can't read] + no-judgment fence; tenant-scoped cross-tenant 404). Route smoke gains `/dental/plans/{patient}` (doctor 200 / billing 403) + `/portal/treatment-plan`. NO existing behavior changed; the reconciliation/immutability/fence/eval + G1–G4 suites stay green UNCHANGED. VERIFIED: npm build green (new TreatmentPlans + Portal/TreatmentPlan chunks); composer test:smoke green; composer check FULLY green (Pint passed · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · Pest **681 passed / 5371 assertions**, 0 failed). **With G5 the CORE DENTAL SPINE (G1→G5) is COMPLETE — a general dentist can chart the mouth (G1/G2) → record + bill procedures (G3/G4) → build/present/track a phased fee-scheduled plan (G5). Remaining: G6 perio · G7 diagnosis record · G8 imaging (+ later sterilization/inventory, ortho/scan-compare, live imaging, licensed code sets).** See [[D-103]], [[Dental]], [[Billing]].
- `e80620e` DENTAL.G6: perio charting (per-site raw measurements, record-not-judge). Per-tooth, per-SITE periodontal measurements as RAW recorded facts — the vitals discipline applied to perio. Per `docs/DENTAL-DELIVERY-MAP.md`. DISCOVERY first (mirrored the exact G1 append-only clinical-model pattern: `tooth_records` migration [ULID pk, char tenant_id, denormalized patient_id, DB SIGNAL-45000 no-update/no-delete triggers] + `ToothRecord` [BelongsToTenant, HasUlids, LogsReads, `creating` validation, `updating`/`deleting`-throw] + `ToothChartService` [gate + assertActorTenant/assertPatientTenant fail-closed + audit + patient-scoped read-log] + `OdontogramController` [string-id `whereKey->firstOrFail`, Inertia render] + the recursive no-judgment fence test). **Built:** NEW `perio_exams` (BelongsToTenant, LogsReads, **APPEND-ONLY** at model + DB triggers `perio_exams_no_update`/`_no_delete`) — a point-in-time 6-point probing session (patient, examined_by, exam_date, note); groups NEW `perio_measurements` (BelongsToTenant, **APPEND-ONLY** model + triggers) — one row per tooth × SITE. **Six sites/tooth** (`PerioMeasurement::SITES` = mesio_buccal/buccal/disto_buccal/mesio_lingual/lingual/disto_lingual — the standard 6-point probing, DISTINCT from the odontogram's 5 anatomical `ToothNotation::SURFACES`). Per site the RAW probed values: `pocket_depth_mm` (unsignedTinyInt), `recession_mm` (smallInt — signed, negative = gingival overgrowth), `bleeding_on_probing` (bool), + optional per-tooth `mobility` (0–3) / `furcation` (0–4). Tooth = FDI (reuses G1 `ToothNotation::isValid`). **CRITICAL ELECTRIC FENCE (perio's core risk): RAW numbers ONLY — DELIBERATELY NO periodontal stage (I–IV), NO grade (A–C), NO severity, NO risk score, NO "disease detected", NO auto-flag of a deepening site, NO computed attachment-loss finding — in schema, service, OR UI.** `PerioMeasurement::assertValid` is PURE data-entry validation (valid FDI id, valid site, physically-plausible bounds: depth 0–15, recession -15–30, mobility 0–3, furcation 0–4) exactly like the odontogram rejecting an unknown surface — bounds reject impossible input, they NEVER grade. A per-site trend over time (`siteHistory`, oldest first) is RAW context — raw numbers in sequence, NO band/arrow/"worsening" label (same rule as unified vitals trends P.13). Proven by a recursive `pcAssertNoJudgment` (forbids stage/staging/grade/severity/risk/flag/classification/worsening/… keys) over BOTH the page props AND the siteHistory output. `Services\PerioChartService`: `recordExam` (gate `dental.chart`, actor+patient same-tenant fail-closed, `DB::transaction` of exam + its site rows — an invalid value throws and the WHOLE exam rolls back, audited `dental.perio_charted`); `examsFor`/`siteHistory` (gate `patient.view`, patient-scoped `read` audit). **UI:** `Http\Controllers\PerioChartController` (GET `/dental/perio/{patient}` = show, POST = store, string-id FIX.1) + `Dental/PerioChart.vue` — the classic perio grid (teeth × 6 sites entry with depth/recession/BOP + per-tooth mobility/furcation; prior exams as raw grids), PRESENTATIONAL (P0D.GU): NO severity colouring, NO flagged sites, NO stage/grade badge, NO auto-watch — a dot marks BOP (data entry), not severity. NEW `perio.*` i18n. Route smoke gains `/dental/perio/{patient}` (doctor 200 / billing 403). NEW `tests/Feature/Dental/PerioChartTest.php` (7 — records raw per-site measurements + tenant/patient scoped + audited + read-logged; append-only re-exam preserves prior [model AND raw-DB triggers on BOTH tables]; page payload carries NO stage/grade/severity/flag [the fence]; per-site history is raw over time + no judgment; rejects invalid FDI/site/out-of-range [deterministic, tx rolls back]; RBAC dental.chart-records/patient.view-reads; tenant-scoped cross-tenant fail-closed). NO existing behavior changed (no existing test modified; the helper prefix is `pc` to avoid a global collision with PerformProcedureTest's `pp` helpers); the reconciliation/immutability/fence/eval + G1–G5 suites stay green UNCHANGED. VERIFIED: npm build green (new PerioChart chunk); composer test:smoke green (3 passed); composer check FULLY green (Pint passed · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · Pest **688 passed / 5498 assertions**, 0 failed). Per `docs/DENTAL-DELIVERY-MAP.md`. DISCOVERY first (reused the G6 append-only clinical-model pattern: `tooth_records`/`perio_*` migration + model [BelongsToTenant, HasUlids, LogsReads, `creating` validation, `updating`/`deleting`-throw, DB SIGNAL-45000 triggers] + service [gate + fail-closed tenant asserts + audit + patient-scoped read-log] + controller [string-id `whereKey->firstOrFail`, Inertia] + the recursive no-judgment fence test; confirmed the delivery-map says build the prototype's AI-diagnosis WITHOUT the AI). **Built:** NEW `diagnoses` (BelongsToTenant, LogsReads, **APPEND-ONLY** model + DB triggers `diagnoses_no_update`/`_no_delete`) — stores what the DENTIST decided: `label` (text they wrote OR picked), optional `tooth`/`surface` (FDI, reuses `ToothNotation`), `findings`, `reason` (a change), and `status` ∈ `Diagnosis::STATUSES` {provisional, confirmed, ruled_out} the DENTIST sets; `diagnosis_term_id` = PROVENANCE only (null = free text). NEW `diagnosis_terms` (BelongsToTenant, plain catalog, NOT append-only) = the tenant's OWN pick-list `{label, is_active}` — TENANT-AUTHORED like the procedure catalog, **NO licensed diagnostic code set (ICD/SNODENT) bundled**. **CRITICAL ELECTRIC FENCE — THE SHARPEST (do not compromise): NO AI in the diagnosis path AT ALL this gate — NO suggested/proposed diagnosis, NO auto-ranked differential, NO computed likelihood/confidence, NOTHING auto-populates a diagnosis from the charting/perio/imaging.** The system ONLY records what the dentist entered; `status` is the DENTIST'S determination — recorded, never decided/suggested. The prototype's "agent's proposed diagnosis / auto-ranked differential" is built WITHOUT it (purely dentist-authored; a governed-AI diagnosis draft was DELIBERATELY not added — a diagnosis is the one place we want no AI in the loop for now). `Diagnosis::assertValid` is PURE data-entry validation (non-empty label, valid FDI/surface, known status) — never infers/ranks. The pick-list is a plain ALPHABETICAL list (`orderBy('label')`), NEVER sorted/filtered by a computed judgment. `Services\DiagnosisService`: `record` (gate `dental.chart`, actor+patient same-tenant fail-closed, term-id must be THIS tenant's [else DentalException], audited `dental.diagnosis_recorded`); `diagnosesFor` (gate `patient.view`, patient-scoped `read` audit, history = every row newest-first); `terms`/`addTerm` (the tenant's pick-list; addTerm audited `dental.diagnosis_term.created`). NO suggestion/ranking/inference method exists — adversarial grep finds zero suggest/propose/differential/likelihood/rank/AI logic in `Modules\Dental` diagnosis code. **UI:** `Http\Controllers\DiagnosisController` (GET `/dental/diagnoses/{patient}` = show, POST = store, POST `/dental/diagnosis-terms` = storeTerm, string-id FIX.1) + `Dental/Diagnoses.vue` — the dentist writes/picks a diagnosis, sets the status THEY determine, ties an optional tooth, references findings, manages their own term list; diagnosis history newest-first. PRESENTATIONAL (P0D.GU): NO "suggested diagnosis" UI, NO differential ranking, NO AI panel, NO auto-fill from charting. NEW `diagnosis.*` i18n. Route smoke gains `/dental/diagnoses/{patient}` (doctor 200 / billing 403). Helper prefix `dx` (no global collision). NO existing behavior changed (no existing test modified); the reconciliation/immutability/fence/eval + G1–G6 suites stay green UNCHANGED. - `a62be58` UI.F1: visual-fidelity — fix root token/shared-component drift to match Eucalyptus Glow prototype. PURELY VISUAL (P0D.GU) — no data/props/logic/route/fence/RBAC/billing change; NO omitted behaviour reintroduced (no graded vitals, AI panels, pay buttons, editable signed notes); NO .vue template/prop change, so every assertInertia/behaviour test passes UNCHANGED. **Opening:** top commit `dbbb6a3` DENTAL.G9 (the brief said `8e58acf` — flagged the hash drift per AGENTS.md; content is correct, DENTAL.G9 is on top); CI green. **DIAGNOSIS (rendered prototype `resources/prototype/*.html` [gitignored 52 MB bundles, served locally on :8001] vs the live app, comparing computed styles):** the drift is ROOT/shared, not per-page. **(1) THE root cause — no webfont delivered.** `resources/css/app.css` names `--font-sans: 'Inter'` and `<body class="font-sans">`, but nothing loaded Inter — no `@font-face`, no `<link>`, no font package (`grep` clean; zero woff2 in the repo). So the app rendered Inter ONLY on machines where Inter is system-installed and fell back to `ui-sans-serif, system-ui` (Segoe UI / SF / Roboto) everywhere else — shifting type metrics + vertical rhythm (hence "spacing/fonts feel off") on EVERY page, while the prototype always loads Inter via Google Fonts. (On the dev machine Inter happened to be system-installed, masking it — confirmed the app fetched zero font files.) **(2) `.glass-card` token drift:** shadow `0 14px 40px rgba(53,70,47,0.11)` vs the prototype's dominant `0 16px 44px …0.11` (18 uses); white hairline border opacity `0.6` vs the prototype's brighter `0.8`. **(3) `.euca-wash` glow:** top-right radial `rgba(198,218,191,0.5)` vs the prototype `0.55`. **Confirmed NON-drift (the colours are NOT wrong):** the euca ramp (`#f7faf5`→`#35462f`), ink (`#2a332a`), surfaces, radii (2xl = 20px), card blur (24px), and the shell/nav/pill structure all MATCH the prototype exactly — the "colours feel off" perception was downstream of the wrong font + the subtle card/wash drift. Root shared surfaces = the typography base (JS entry) + the shared `.glass-card` and `.euca-wash` classes. **FIX (root, shared — corrects every page + all 3 shells [app/auth/portal] at once, since all use `euca-wash`/`glass-card` and one JS entry):** (a) self-host Inter via `@fontsource/inter@^5` (weights 400/500/600/700 = every `font-*` utility the app uses), imported in `resources/js/app.ts` → Vite bundles 28 subset woff2 (`font-display: swap`) into the build — CSP-safe, NO external CDN, guaranteed Inter on every machine (verified: the live app now fetches its own `inter-latin-400-normal…woff2`); (b) `.glass-card` shadow → `0 16px 44px`, border opacity → `0.8` (verified computed 16px/44px + `0.8`, matching the prototype); (c) `.euca-wash` glow → `0.55`. **Re-comparison:** the shared fixes propagate to every page; on the dev machine (Inter pre-installed) the before/after is subtle, but the cross-machine win is decisive (Inter now always renders) and the glass cards now match the prototype's lift + hairline. **Remaining → F2 (page-specific):** per-page heading sizes (page h1 24px vs the prototype's 22px), the native `<input type=date>` styling, and the prototype's nav tenant-chip (that is CONTENT, not styling; the nav DENSITY difference is RBAC content — org_admin sees 13 items vs the prototype's curated 6 — not a visual-token fix). New dep `@fontsource/inter` in package.json + package-lock. NO test file changed. VERIFIED: npm run build green (28 woff2 emitted); composer check FULLY green (Pest 707/5741 unchanged); composer test:smoke green. See [[D-108]].

- `99bc7f7` HOSPITAL.G1: bed/ward/unit model + concurrency-safe bed-claim + inpatient RBAC (Phase 1 foundation). The FIRST hospital-vertical gate (inpatient/ADT), built per `docs/HOSPITAL-PHASE1-ADT-MAP.md` — the domain foundation everything else (ADT stay, ward board, charting, bed-billing, discharge) depends on. **Opening:** top commit `59ca4ae` (the ADT map), CI green. **NEW module `Modules\Hospital`** (PSR-4 + provider registered; arch rule mirrors Dental — may use care modules + Audit **services**, never Audit models/AiCore/Nursing/Comms; in G1 it depends only on the Platform foundation). **Bed is NET-NEW, NOT a Scheduling Resource** (map §2.1): a bed is occupied CONTINUOUSLY for a multi-day stay, so there is deliberately NO `starts_at`/`ends_at` — it reuses Resource's *pattern* (tenant + one branch + typed enum + `active`) and the `BookingService` lock idiom, not its table. **Ward is a Hospital-owned model** (documented choice — not Platform's unwired `Department` stub): clean bidirectional `Ward hasMany Bed`, inpatient-specific growth, the `Nursing\Visit → Branch` pattern; `Department` left for its generic purpose. **Housekeeping status** {free, occupied, cleaning, blocked} is a legal-only state machine (`Bed::TRANSITIONS`); **free→occupied is reached ONLY via the concurrency-safe `BedService::claim()`** (`DB::transaction` + `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` + assert-still-free), proven by `BedClaimParallelHammerTest` (8 OS processes race one free bed → exactly 1 CLAIMED / 7 CONFLICT — the `BookingParallelHammer` sibling). Each transition fires `BedStatusChanged` → an **app-layer listener** → one append-only `bed.status_changed` audit row; ward/bed CRUD audited via app-layer model hooks (Hospital stays free of Audit — the Branch/Resource + Appointment pattern). **Inpatient RBAC additive** (the `dental.chart` precedent; `Gate::before` unchanged): +4 permissions (`ward.manage`, `bed.manage`, `admission.manage`, `document.view`) + 6 role templates (ward_nurse, charge_nurse, hospitalist, bed_manager, admissions_clerk, him_records); `org_admin` gains all four; management gates on `bed`/`ward.manage`, the claim on `admission.manage`; ward-level scope is branch-level for Phase 1. **ELECTRIC FENCE (operational, not clinical):** a bed/ward has NO patient/acuity/severity/score/flag column — status is housekeeping, never a judgment (schema fence test); a NEWS2-style deterioration score is out (certified-partner/non-goal per the map §3). NO ADT workflow (G2), NO UI (G3). No existing behavior test modified; the relative permission-count assertion + the hardcoded RBAC negative-sweep stay green. Added tests: `WardBedManagementTest` (7 — CRUD+tenant/branch scoping+audit, legal status transitions, occupied→cleaning→free turnover, claim+conflict, RBAC gating, cross-tenant isolation, fence-no-judgment) + `BedClaimParallelHammerTest` (1, concurrency) + the Hospital arch rule (1). VERIFIED: `composer check` FULLY green (Pint `passed` · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · **Pest 717 passed / 2 skipped / 5841 assertions**, 0 failed — +9 tests/+70 assertions vs POLISH.3's 708/5771); no frontend this gate. See [[D-113]].
- `52586ea` HOSPITAL.G2: ADT stay + admit/transfer/discharge state machine (atomic, bed-safe). The CORE of the inpatient vertical — a patient is admitted to a bed, transferred between beds/wards, and discharged, over a multi-day `Stay`. Built per `docs/HOSPITAL-PHASE1-ADT-MAP.md`. **Opening:** top commit `99bc7f7` (HOSPITAL.G1), CI green. **`Stay` is a NET-NEW entity ABOVE an UNMODIFIED `Encounter`** (map §2.2 — the `VisitPlan→Visit` analogue; G2 does NOT touch Clinical, so Encounter's one-open-per-practitioner invariant holds for every vertical — bedside charting reuses Encounter per ward-round in G4). `stays` (BelongsToTenant, **LogsReads** patient-scoped read-log) is the MUTABLE current state (patient, admitting clinician [StaffProfile], current bed/ward, admitted_at/discharged_at, status, admission_type/reason, discharge_disposition); the immutable admit/transfer/discharge history is **append-only `stay_events`** (model + DB triggers `stay_events_no_update`/`_no_delete`, the tooth_records posture — the full bed journey is preserved). **State machine (legal-only):** {admitted → discharged}; a **transfer is a bed-move WITHIN admitted** (not a status change). `admission_type` ∈ {elective, emergency, transfer} = an operational ROUTE (validated in the model `creating` hook). **`AdmissionService` (gated `admission.manage`, tenant+branch fail-closed, rejects a cross-tenant bed UP FRONT):** admit / transfer / discharge are each **ATOMIC** (the dental-perform discipline) — the stay change + the bed claim/release (**via G1's proven concurrency-safe `BedService::claim`/`release` — NOT reimplemented**) + the append-only `StayEvent` all in ONE `DB::transaction`; a forced failure rolls back EVERYTHING (no orphan stay, no stuck bed — even the bed's audit row rolls back, proven by a test). admit `claim`s the bed (free→occupied); transfer `claim`s the new + `release`s the old (occupied→cleaning); discharge `release`s the bed + sets disposition/discharged_at. **One-active-stay guard** (patient row-lock + `lockForUpdate()->exists()` — the one-open-encounter analogue). Concurrency reuses G1's hammer-proven claim (admit-to-occupied-bed → conflict, tested). Every transition fires `StayTransitioned` → an **app-layer listener** → one append-only `admission.<eventType>` audit row (keyed by event type since a transfer keeps status=admitted; the AppointmentTransitioned pattern — Hospital stays free of Audit). `BedService` gained `release()` (occupied→cleaning, `admission.manage`, same lock idiom). **NO charge posted — bed-to-billing is G6.** Minimal action surface: `AdmissionController` (string-id FIX.1) — show (GET, `patient.view`, read-logged, renders a minimal `Hospital/Admission.vue`) + store/transfer/discharge (POST, `admission.manage`); FIX.5 route smoke extended (org_admin GET 200, reception POST admit 403). **ELECTRIC FENCE (operational, not clinical):** no acuity/severity/score/triage/deterioration column on stays/stay_events (schema fence test); the bed/ward/route/disposition are facts a human sets. NO ward-board/charting UI (G3+). No existing behavior test modified (the smoke was EXTENDED with the ADT fixture+routes, the ImportBatch precedent); Encounter untouched (asserted: an admit creates no Encounter). Added tests: `HospitalAdmissionTest` (12 — admit/transfer/discharge + audit, legal-only state machine, one-active-stay, three atomicity proofs incl. post-claim rollback, RBAC gating, cross-tenant isolation, append-only history, fence). VERIFIED: npm run build green; composer check FULLY green (Pint `passed` · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · **Pest 729 passed / 2 skipped / 5921 assertions**, 0 failed — +12 tests/+80 assertions vs G1's 717/5841); composer test:smoke green (3). See [[D-114]].
- `d011a5f` HOSPITAL.G3: ward board (live bed-occupancy cockpit) over the ADT domain. The first inpatient UI — the operational view of a ward's beds (free/occupied/cleaning/blocked) with the current patient per occupied bed and the ADT actions surfaced from it. **PRESENTATIONAL over G1/G2 (P0D.GU)** — the services own all logic. Built per `docs/HOSPITAL-PHASE1-ADT-MAP.md`. **Opening:** top commit `52586ea` (HOSPITAL.G2), CI green. **Read controller `WardBoardController::show`** (GET `/hospital/wards`, gate `patient.view`) renders `Hospital/WardBoard.vue`: each active ward (via the new `WardService::activeWards()`) → its beds (`BedService::forWard` — label, bed_type, **housekeeping status**), the current patient + `admitted_at` per OCCUPIED bed (the active `Stay` keyed by `current_bed_id`, one query), and a plain **occupancy count** (occupied/total). **Reuses the day-board TILE/STATUS idiom for layout, but the data is beds/stays (continuous occupancy) — it NEVER touches the scheduling slot engine.** **Actions surfaced from the board, all through the EXISTING G1/G2 services** (no new ADT logic, no bypass of the atomicity/concurrency guarantees): ADMIT a free bed / TRANSFER / DISCHARGE POST to the existing G2 admission routes (**admit-from-the-board uses the proven `AdmissionService::admit` → concurrency-safe `BedService::claim`** — tested: the bed becomes occupied atomically); set a bed's housekeeping status via NEW `setBedStatus` (POST `/hospital/beds/{bed}/status`, gate `bed.manage`, string-id FIX.1) → `BedService::setStatus` (legal-only). Read gate = `patient.view` (all inpatient clinical staff incl. ward nurses hold it; billing denied); the payload's `can_admit`/`can_manage_beds` reflect the actor + the pickers (patients/clinicians) surface only for `admission.manage` (server Gate authoritative). The board is NOT per-occupant read-logged (operational overview, the day-board posture). **ELECTRIC FENCE (operational only):** housekeeping status + occupant name + `admitted_at` (LOS-so-far = plain elapsed time the client renders) + a plain occupancy count. NO acuity/severity/risk/priority/deterioration field; the status COLOUR is the housekeeping state, never a clinical judgment — asserted by a recursive `wbAssertNoJudgment` over the payload. **No charge posted (billing is G6).** i18n keys + Eucalyptus Glow tokens (no hardcoded hex/strings). No existing behavior test modified (the FIX.5 smoke was EXTENDED with the board route — doctor 200 / billing 403). Added tests: `WardBoardTest` (5 — the board renders each ward's beds + status + the current patient per occupied bed + occupancy count [assertInertia]; admit-from-the-board goes through AdmissionService atomically; the fence [no judgment key in the payload]; RBAC [patient.view to view, bed-status = bed.manage, action affordances per role]; tenant scoped). **the one-open-per-practitioner invariant is enforced UNCHANGED — a second round for the same stay is refused, tested**) + a `WardRound` link + a sign-and-lock note DRAFT (`ClinicalNoteService::saveDraft`), atomically, then the controller redirects into the **EXISTING note editor** (`clinical.notes.edit` — the write→sign→lock→amend→version flow is REUSED, not rebuilt). `recordVital` reuses `ClinicalListService::recordVital` (note.write) tied to the stay's latest round Encounter (raw `Vital`). **The ONLY new affordance is `vitalsForStay`** — a stay-scoped READ filtering the existing `Vital` store to the stay's round Encounters + building the RAW per-metric series via the existing `VitalsSeries::build` (NO schema change, no bands/flags/scores). `placeOrder` reuses `OrderService::place` (order.manage) tied to the round; `ordersForStay` reads them. `BedsideChartController` (string-id FIX.1): `show` (GET `/hospital/admissions/{stay}/chart`, `patient.view`, **read-logged**) renders `Hospital/StayChart.vue` (rounds + note-editor links + raw vitals-over-stay + orders); `startRound`/`recordVital`/`placeOrder` (POST, the existing clinical gates). **RBAC = the EXISTING clinical permissions the inpatient roles already hold** (ward_nurse + hospitalist have encounter.manage/note.write/note.sign/order.manage; read = patient.view) — no new permission. **ELECTRIC FENCE carries through:** raw vitals (VitalsSeries), sign-and-lock notes unchanged, append-only order results — NO computed acuity/deterioration/early-warning score (NEWS2 = certified-partner/non-goal, NOT built); the stay-chart payload carries no judgment field (recursive scan). **No charge posted (billing is G6).** No existing behavior test modified (the FIX.5 smoke was EXTENDED with the stay-chart route — doctor 200 / billing 403); **Clinical's suite + Encounter's invariant tests stay green** (reuse, not modify). - `ab9fc26` HOSPITAL.G5: nursing shift handover (SBAR, nurse-authored, record-not-judge). The structured artifact that carries a stay's key information across nursing shifts. Built per `docs/HOSPITAL-PHASE1-ADT-MAP.md`. **Opening:** top commit `abe491a` (HOSPITAL.G4), CI green. **DISCOVERY (reported):** a handover is a NET-NEW structured SBAR artifact, NOT a reuse of `ClinicalNote` — SBAR (Situation/Background/Assessment/Recommendation) ≠ SOAP; it carries shift metadata a note lacks; it is **STAY-scoped** whereas `ClinicalNote` is Encounter-scoped (mandatory encounter_id, a round encounter — a handover isn't). The write is audited via an app-layer `Handover::created` hook (`handover.recorded`) — Hospital stays free of Audit. i18n keys + tokens. **ELECTRIC FENCE (record-not-judge):** the SBAR fields capture what the OUTGOING NURSE WRITES — `assessment` is the nurse's OWN written assessment (a SBAR section, like a note's SOAP assessment), NOT a computed score; there is DELIBERATELY no acuity/severity/score/risk/priority/flag column, and NOTHING auto-populates any field (proven: a fresh stay has 0 handovers, and a recorded handover's assessment is verbatim; the payload carries no judgment key — recursive scan). NO AI-drafted handover this gate. **No charge posted (billing is G6).** No existing behavior test modified (the FIX.5 smoke was EXTENDED with the handover route — doctor 200 / billing 403); G1–G4 + reconciliation/fence/immutability/eval suites stay green. Added tests: `HandoverTest` (6 — record SBAR [scoped + audited + verbatim], append-only [correction preserves prior; model + raw-DB throw], fence [no judgment column + nothing auto-populates the assessment + no judgment in the payload], shift-history newest-first, RBAC [note.write / patient.view], tenant-scoped 404). - `72a5fa8` HOSPITAL.G6: bed-to-billing — inpatient accrual + discharge invoice via the EXISTING billing engine (reconciles-to-the-unit). An inpatient stay accrues charges (bed-days + services) through the existing engine, and discharge produces an invoice that reconciles-to-the-unit. Built per `docs/HOSPITAL-PHASE1-ADT-MAP.md` §2.3/§5. **Opening:** top commit `ab9fc26` (HOSPITAL.G5), CI green. **KEY POSTURE — NET-NEW is STRICTLY ORCHESTRATION, zero new billing/pricing/VAT/line-total math:** the engine prices everything — a bed-day is a tenant-authored `TariffItem`, accrual is the existing `ChargeCaptureService::captureManual` (the engine resolves + **snapshots** the fee and computes `line_total`), and the discharge invoice is the existing `ChargeValidator::validateForPatientPeriod` → `IssueService::createDraftFromCharges` → `issue` flow (gapless number + PDF); reconciliation is the existing `ReconciliationEngine` (I4 "N charges → 1 invoice" natively). **`bed_day_accruals`** (BelongsToTenant) — the idempotency ledger `unique(tenant_id, stay_id, service_date)` (charge_id FK; NO money stored). **`BedBillingService`** (orchestration): `CATALOG_KEY='hospital'`; `STARTER` = 3 GENERIC bed-day items (BED-DAY-GENERAL 50000 / ICU 200000 / ISOLATION 80000 minor units — the tenant's OWN codes, **NO licensed code set**, placeholder rates it edits); `seedStarter` (gate `billing.manage`, idempotent by code, unit 'bed-day', vat 0); `accrueBedDays(actor, stay, ?upTo)` captures one bed-day Charge per occupied calendar day via the engine, idempotent via the ledger (fast-check then capture+ledger in one `DB::transaction`; a race rolls back on the unique key); `invoiceStay(actor, stay)` (gate `billing.manage`, cross-tenant fail-closed) accrues the final bed-days then runs the existing validate→draft→issue flow filtered to the stay window; the bed resolves via a typed `Bed::findOrFail` (fail-closed, no mis-price fallback). **`Console\AccrueBedDaysCommand`** (`hospital:accrue-bed-days`) — the unattended sweep, shaped exactly like `nursing:materialize-visits`: iterate ACTIVE tenants, resolve the tenant's `org_admin` as the automated billing actor (holds `billing.manage`; skip+warn if none), accrue every ADMITTED stay; registered in `HospitalServiceProvider`, scheduled `->dailyAt('05:30')->withoutOverlapping(30)->onOneServer()` (before the 06:00 dunning / 06:30 reconcile sweeps). **`BedBillingController::invoice`** (POST `/hospital/admissions/{stay}/invoice`, `billing.manage`, string-id FIX.1) → redirect to the EXISTING `billing.invoices.show`; light ADT wiring adds `actions.can_invoice`/`invoice_url` + an "Invoice stay" button on a discharged stay (`Admission.vue`, i18n key). **RECONCILES-TO-THE-UNIT (THE key proof):** a discharged stay's bed-day + service charges assemble into one gapless invoice and `ReconciliationEngine::check(period)` passes with **I4 `delta_minor === 0`**. **ELECTRIC FENCE / no-money-math (adversarial grep, tested):** `Modules\Hospital` contains none of `line_total_minor`/`vat_total_minor`/`subtotal_minor`/`vatMinor`/`intdiv(` — the only money it names is the authored per-diem RATE; all charge/VAT/line-total math lives in Billing. **One existing test updated by NECESSITY, not weakened:** `ScheduleRegistrationTest` (the canonical scheduled-command inventory — "guards against a future gate quietly scheduling something unattended") got the new sweep added to its expected cadence map + exact-set list, exactly as prior gates did for the nursing/billing sweeps; the FIX.5 smoke was EXTENDED with the invoice route (reception POST 403). Added tests: `BedBillingTest` (7 — per-diem tenant-authored [generic codes, integer minor units, zero VAT, no licensed set], accrue via the engine + snapshot, idempotent command [twice ≠ double-charge], **discharge invoice + reconcile δ=0** [6 bed-days × 50000 + 1 consult × 12000 = 312000; every invariant green], fee snapshot [editing the tariff never changes a past charge], no-money-math grep, RBAC + cross-tenant fail-closed). VERIFIED: npm run build green; composer check FULLY green (Pint `passed` · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · **Pest 754 passed / 2 skipped / 6340 assertions**, 0 failed — +7 tests vs G5's 747); composer test:smoke green (3). See [[D-118]].

Audited via app-layer `DischargeSummary::created`/`updated` hooks (`discharge_summary.drafted`/`.finalized`). **No change to G2's discharge state-change or G6's billing (additive); the G4 `WardRound` gained timestamp `@property` annotations (docblock only).** No existing behavior test modified (the FIX.5 smoke was EXTENDED — discharge-summary GET 200 + reception write 403). - `46cced0` PHARMACY.G1: pharmacy module + tenant-authored formulary + medication-safety seam (null-object) + RBAC — Phase 2 foundation. The foundation of the pharmacy / medication-management vertical (Phase 2 of the phased hospital build), per `docs/HOSPITAL-PHASE2-PHARMACY-MAP.md`. **Opening:** top commit `3718cb8` (the Phase 2 map), CI green. **(1) New peer `Modules\Pharmacy`** (composer psr-4 + `bootstrap/providers.php` + `PharmacyServiceProvider`) with its own arch rule (may use Platform + care modules Patients/Clinical/Billing + Audit SERVICES; NOT Audit models, AiCore, Comms, or the peer verticals Nursing/Dental/Hospital — the inpatient stay-link for G2 is app-layer, not a direct Hospital dep). **(2) Tenant-authored FORMULARY** — `formulary_items` (BelongsToTenant): `code` (the tenant's OWN code, NOT licensed), `name`, `form` ∈ {tablet/capsule/liquid/injection/topical/other}, `strength` (free text), `active`; `unique(tenant_id, code)`; `FormularyItem` model (a `saving` guard for non-empty code+name + valid form, throwing `FormularyException`). `FormularyService`: `seedStarter` (gate **`formulary.manage`**, idempotent by code, a SMALL GENERIC starter of 5 common meds with the tenant's own `MED-*` codes — the `DentalCatalogService`/`BedBillingService` discipline) + `create`/`update`/`deactivate` (gate `formulary.manage`, tenant fail-closed) + `forTenant`. **NO licensed drug data bundled** (no First Databank / Medi-Span / RxNorm / ATC / NDC) and **NO computed-safety column** (no interaction/dose/contraindication/severity/score/risk) — a schema fence asserts both. A licensed drug DB would later ENRICH a row at a partner seam (NOT attached now). **(3) THE MEDICATION-SAFETY SEAM, built EMPTY (the crux):** drug-interaction / allergy-class / dose-range / duplicate-therapy checking each COMPUTE a clinical-safety JUDGMENT (the fence refuses "dosing logic … Ever"; medical-device territory, DEFERRED.md D-P0D.G3) — so G1 builds the SEAM not the logic, EXACTLY mirroring `LabConnectivity → ManualLabConnectivity`: a `Contracts\MedicationSafetyProvider` interface (`checkOrder`/`checkAdministration`) bound in `PharmacyServiceProvider::register` to a **`NullMedicationSafetyProvider`** that returns `SafetyResult::none()` (+ plain value objects `SafetyResult`/`SafetyAlert`/`SafetyContext`/`MedicationReference`). **A homemade checker is a PERMANENT non-goal** (it would contradict the clinical-safety eval `ClinicalAgentsEvalTest`). `none()` = "CareOS asserts NOTHING about safety" (not "safe") — a human, and when licensed a certified partner bound in place of the null-object (WITHOUT touching consumers), owns that judgment; findings are ADVISORY + human-owned (surfaced, never auto-blocking). **(4) Pharmacy RBAC (additive):** `formulary.manage` + `dispense.manage` permissions + `pharmacist` (patient.view + formulary.manage + dispense.manage) + `pharmacy_technician` (patient.view + dispense.manage) role templates; `org_admin` gains both; RbacProvisioner consts + a `syncPermissionCatalog`/`provisionTenant`-all backfill migration (the `add_billing_manage_permission` pattern); new tenants via the Tenant `created` hook. A minimal formulary admin surface (`FormularyController` index/store/deactivate + `Pharmacy/Formulary.vue` + i18n, gated `formulary.manage`); formulary writes audited via app-layer `FormularyItem::created`/`updated` hooks (`formulary.item.*`) so Pharmacy stays free of Audit. NO orders/eMAR/dispensing this gate. No existing behavior test modified (the FIX.5 smoke was EXTENDED — formulary GET 200 + reception write 403); all vertical + arch + eval + fence suites stay green. Added tests: `FormularyTest` (4 — tenant-authored [own MED-* codes, no licensed/computed-safety column, tenant-isolated, idempotent starter], RBAC-gated + audited [chain intact], admin surface gated [pharmacist 200 / reception 403 / writes 403], cross-tenant fail-closed) + `MedicationSafetySeamTest` (3 — bound to the null-object + asserts no judgment [no alerts even for warfarin+aspirin], `SafetyResult::none()` empty, real + swappable [a certified-partner test double is resolvable in its place]) + the `Pharmacy` arch rule. - `3cb06a2` PHARMACY.G2: medication orders — net-new prescribing entity + safety-seam call-site + clinician-authored. The prescribing entity of the pharmacy vertical, per the map §2.2/§5. **Opening:** top commit `46cced0` (PHARMACY.G1), CI green. **(1) NET-NEW `MedicationOrder`, NOT the generic `Order`** (the clinical Order is a code+status orderable with ZERO dose/route/frequency/PRN — forcing meds onto it breaks the lab/imaging worklists; the Stay-above-Encounter reasoning). So a med order OWNS its tables while REUSING the `Stay`/`StayEvent` shape: `medication_orders` (BelongsToTenant, **LogsReads**) is the MUTABLE current state — patient, `prescribed_by` (User), `formulary_item_id` (the G1 formulary), `dose_amount`+`dose_unit`, `route` (plain enum PO/IV/IM/SC/topical/inhaled/other), `frequency` (descriptor QID/BID/PRN), starts/stops, prn+prn_reason, note, `status` ∈ {active, held, discontinued, completed} + status_reason, with a legal-only clinician-driven state machine (active→{held,discontinued,completed}, held→{active,discontinued,completed}; terminal after); `medication_order_events` is the **APPEND-ONLY** history (model guards + DB triggers `medication_order_events_no_update`/`_no_delete` — the `stay_events` recipe: placed/held/resumed/discontinued/completed + reason + performed_by + occurred_at). **`stay_id` is a SOFT nullable ref** to a Phase-1 inpatient stay (NO FK/relation — Pharmacy stays arch-independent of Hospital; null for outpatient). **(2) THE SAFETY SEAM THREADED (the crux):** `MedicationOrderService::prescribe` (gate **`medication.prescribe`**; tenant+patient fail-closed) **CALLS `MedicationSafetyProvider::checkOrder`** at placement, and `safetyReview` calls it for the display surface — today the G1 null-object returns none(), so no alerts and the order is NEVER blocked. ADVISORY + HUMAN-OWNED: a future certified partner's findings are SURFACED (an alerts area wired to `SafetyResult`, EMPTY today), never auto-blocking, never auto-acting. **NO homemade interaction/dose/contraindication/duplicate checking** — proven by a grep test (no `new SafetyAlert(` anywhere in `Modules\Pharmacy\src` — CareOS never manufactures a finding) + a spy test (the seam IS called at placement and the order proceeds despite a returned alert). **(3) RBAC:** a NEW `medication.prescribe` permission — prescribing is a physician act (doctor/hospitalist/org_admin, NOT nurse), distinct from lab/imaging `order.manage`; read = `patient.view`. **(4) FENCE (record-not-judge):** every field is the clinician's entry — the system computes no dose, suggests no med, ranks nothing; no computed-dose/suggested/recommended/verdict/severity/score column (schema fence); nothing auto-populates; the payload carries no judgment key + the alerts area is empty. `MedicationOrderController` (string-id FIX.1): `index` (GET `/pharmacy/patients/{patient}/medications`, `patient.view`, **read-logged**) renders `Pharmacy/MedicationOrders.vue` (place form + active + history + the empty alerts area), `store` + `transition` (POST, `medication.prescribe`) + i18n; events audited via an app-layer `MedicationOrderEvent::created` hook (`medication_order.<type>`) so Pharmacy stays free of Audit. **No charge (pharmacy billing is G5); no eMAR/dispensing.** *(The exact-match `AllergyGuard` hard-stop is NOT wired this gate — the gate's safety requirement was the MedicationSafetyProvider seam; AllergyGuard would need a formulary `substance_key`, a later step.)* No existing behavior test modified (the FIX.5 smoke was EXTENDED — med-order GET 200 + reception prescribe 403); all vertical + arch + eval + fence suites stay green. Added `MedicationOrderTest` (7 — place [net-new, dose/route/frequency/PRN, patient + soft stay, audited + chain intact]; the seam is called at placement + advisory + NEVER blocks [spy double returns an alert, order still created]; NO homemade finding [no `new SafetyAlert(` grep]; append-only [model + raw-DB] + legal-only transitions; fence [no computed column, nothing auto-populates, empty alerts payload]; RBAC + read-logged; tenant/patient fail-closed). - `54735a4` PHARMACY.G3: eMAR — medication administration record (append-only, safety-seam at administration, record-not-judge). The electronic MAR, per the map §2.4. **Opening:** top commit `3cb06a2` (PHARMACY.G2), CI green. **(1) NET-NEW APPEND-ONLY administration domain** (not a note/order reuse): `medication_administrations` (BelongsToTenant, **LogsReads**, **APPEND-ONLY** — model guards + DB triggers `medication_administrations_no_update`/`_no_delete`, the `medication_order_events` recipe) — one immutable row per administration against a G2 `medication_order`: `outcome` ∈ {given, held, refused} (the nurse's FACT), `administered_by` (User), `administered_at`, `scheduled_at` (nullable — the due time; null for PRN), `dose_amount`/`dose_unit` (the dose GIVEN — defaults from the order for 'given', null for held/refused), `reason` (held/refused), soft `stay_id`.
