The operational dispensing + stock domain, per the map §1. **Opening:** top commit `54735a4` (PHARMACY.G3), CI green. **(1) NET-NEW inventory:** `medication_stocks` (BelongsToTenant) is the MUTABLE current on-hand per formulary item (the Bed-status analogue) — `on_hand`, `unit`, `reorder_threshold` (a plain number); mutated ONLY under a FOR UPDATE row lock (`MedicationStock::lockOnHand` — the `BedService::lockBedStatus` idiom); `unique(tenant, formulary_item_id)`. `stock_movements` (BelongsToTenant, **APPEND-ONLY** — model guards + DB triggers `stock_movements_no_update`/`_no_delete`) is the immutable ledger (type received/dispensed/adjusted, signed `quantity_change`, `resulting_on_hand`, reason, soft `dispense_id`); the current on_hand stays consistent with the latest movement. `StockService` = `receive` (+qty, creates the stock row on first receipt) + `adjust` (stock-take to an absolute count, reason required) + `forTenant`/`recentMovements`, each locking then writing a movement (gate `dispense.manage`). **(2) DISPENSING:** `dispenses` (BelongsToTenant, **LogsReads**, **APPEND-ONLY**) records a pharmacist dispensing a quantity against a G2 order. `DispensingService::dispense` (gate **`dispense.manage`**; tenant + patient fail-closed) does a **factual state check** (the order must be active — can't dispense a discontinued order) then, in ONE transaction, **locks the stock FOR UPDATE → asserts on_hand ≥ qty (else `insufficientStock`) → creates the Dispense → decrements on_hand → appends the 'dispensed' movement** — ATOMIC + concurrency-safe. **(3) THE SAFE + CONCURRENCY-SAFE DECREMENT (the crux):** no oversell, no negative on-hand; proven by `DispenseParallelHammerTest` — 8 OS processes race to dispense the last unit (`pharmacy:attempt-dispense`, the `hospital:attempt-bed-claim` sibling), exactly ONE wins + 7 `INSUFFICIENT` + on_hand=0. **(4) FENCE (operational):** stock/dispensing are FACTS — "below stock" is a factual `on_hand <= reorder_threshold` comparison (`isBelowThreshold`), NEVER a graded/severity/alert judgment; **NO safety checking in dispensing** (the seam is orders/administration); no computed-judgment/safety column on any of the three tables (schema fence). **(5) RBAC:** dispensing/inventory **reuse `dispense.manage`** (the G1 pharmacist/pharmacy_technician permission — NO new permission); the per-patient dispensing view reads `patient.view`, read-logged. `InventoryController` (GET `/pharmacy/inventory`, `dispense.manage`) → `Inventory.vue` (stock + below-threshold factual + receive + adjust + movement log); `DispensingController` (GET `/pharmacy/patients/{patient}/dispensing`, `patient.view`, read-logged) → `Dispensing.vue` (active orders + on-hand + dispense + history) + i18n; audited via app-layer `Dispense::created` (`medication.dispensed`, patient) + `StockMovement::created` (`stock.<type>`, tenant) hooks so Pharmacy stays free of Audit. **No charge (pharmacy billing is G5).** No existing behavior test modified (the FIX.5 smoke was EXTENDED — inventory + dispensing GET 200 + reception dispense 403); all vertical + arch + eval + fence suites stay green. Added `DispensingTest` (6 — receive/adjust [append-only + on-hand consistent + audited]; dispense [decrements, atomic, tied to order, audited + read-logged]; the stock guard [no oversell + no negative]; discontinued-order refused [factual state check]; fence [below-threshold factual, no graded/safety column]; RBAC + tenant/patient fail-closed) + `DispenseParallelHammerTest` (1 — the concurrency proof: 1 of 8 wins the last unit, on_hand=0). VERIFIED: npm run build green; composer check FULLY green (Pint `passed` · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · **Pest 789 passed / 2 skipped / 6846 assertions**, 0 failed — +7 tests vs G3's 782); composer test:smoke green (3). **Next: G5 pharmacy billing** (a formulary item's TariffItem → captureManual → invoice → reconcile-to-the-unit, the bed-day precedent, no new math). See [[D-123]].
- `a49b803` PHARMACY.G5: pharmacy billing — a dispensed med accrues a charge through the EXISTING engine (reconciles-to-the-unit); **Phase 2 core COMPLETE**. The final buildable pharmacy gate, per `docs/HOSPITAL-PHASE2-PHARMACY-MAP.md` §2.1/§5. **Opening:** top commit `92f8c1a` (PHARMACY.G4), CI green. **KEY POSTURE — NET-NEW is STRICTLY ORCHESTRATION, zero new billing/pricing/VAT/line-total math** (the bed-day HOSPITAL.G6 pattern, mirrored). **(1) MEDICATION PRICING — a formulary item maps to a tenant-authored `TariffItem`:** a soft nullable `tariff_item_id` on `formulary_items` (no FK — Pharmacy stays decoupled, the `stay_id` shape); `PharmacyBillingService::priceItem` (gate `billing.manage`, tenant fail-closed) `updateOrCreate`s a `TariffItem` in a get-or-created `pharmacy` `TariffCatalog` (the tenant's OWN `MED-*` code, integer minor units, zero VAT, `requires_service_documentation=false`) and links it — pricing lives in the EXISTING billing/tariff store, **NOT duplicated in pharmacy**, and **NO licensed drug pricing bundled** (the tenant authors placeholder rates it edits, the `BedBillingService::seedStarter` discipline). **(2) CHARGE ON DISPENSE via the EXISTING engine:** `chargeForDispense(actor, Dispense)` → `ChargeCaptureService::captureManual($patient, $branch, $dispensed_at, $item->code, $quantity, $actor)` — the engine resolves the tariff by code, **SNAPSHOTS the fee**, and computes `line_total` (qty × price); Pharmacy does NO money math. IDEMPOTENT via a new `dispense_charges` link `unique(tenant, dispense_id)` (a dispense is charged once; re-charging returns the same Charge; NO money stored — the Charge is the money). An UNPRICED med returns null (no charge, no gate) so the G4 dispensing tests stay green. Wired **BEST-EFFORT + DECOUPLED** in `DispensingController` AFTER the dispense commits (`try { … } catch (Throwable)`) — a billing hiccup NEVER blocks the concurrency-critical dispense (reconcilable later). **(3) INVOICE + RECONCILE-TO-THE-UNIT (THE key proof):** `invoicePatient(actor, patient, from, to)` runs the EXISTING `validateForPatientPeriod` → `createDraftFromCharges(SELF_PAY)` → `issue` flow (gapless number + PDF) over the patient's validated, uninvoiced charges in the window; a patient invoice INCLUDING a dispensed-med charge assembles into one gapless invoice and `ReconciliationEngine::check(period)` passes with **I4 `delta_minor === 0`** (for INPATIENT, pharmacy charges join the stay's discharge invoice via the existing G6 `invoiceStay` — same gather-by-patient+period, no new invoice logic). **(4) RBAC:** the `pharmacist` role gains the EXISTING `billing.manage` — RbacProvisioner const + a `provisionTenant`-all backfill migration; **NO new permission** (billing.manage already exists), so `RbacTest`'s permission-count stays green; new tenants via the Tenant `created` hook. A `PricingController` (GET `/pharmacy/pricing` + POST `/pharmacy/pricing/{item}`, `billing.manage`, string-id FIX.1) → `Pharmacy/Pricing.vue` (set a med's price like any tariff item) + i18n. **(5) ELECTRIC FENCE / no-money-math (adversarial grep, tested):** a med price is a RATE (financial), never a safety/appropriateness/substitution verdict — no cost-based substitution suggestion (substitution/safety is the certified-partner seam, not billing); `Modules\Pharmacy` contains none of `line_total_minor`/`vat_total_minor`/`subtotal_minor`/`vatMinor`/`intdiv(` (the only money it names is the authored per-unit RATE; all charge/VAT/line-total math lives in Billing); no cost/clinical-judgment column on `formulary_items`. No existing behavior test modified (the FIX.5 smoke was EXTENDED — pricing GET 200 + reception price-set 403); all vertical + arch + eval + fence suites stay green. Added `PharmacyBillingTest` (6 — med priced as a tenant-authored TariffItem [own code, integer minor units, zero VAT, no licensed pricing]; dispense captures a Charge via the engine + snapshot + idempotent [twice ≠ double-charge]; **patient invoice WITH a med charge reconciles δ=0** [2 × 800 = 1600, I4 green]; no-money-math grep + no-judgment-column fence; fee snapshot [re-pricing never changes a past charge]; RBAC billing.manage + cross-tenant fail-closed). VERIFIED: npm run build green; composer check FULLY green (Pint `passed` · PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors` · **Pest 795 passed / 2 skipped / 7056 assertions**, 0 failed — +6 tests vs G4's 789); composer test:smoke green (3). **PHARMACY CORE COMPLETE: G1 formulary + safety-seam → G2 orders → G3 eMAR → G4 dispensing/inventory → G5 billing.** The ONE deliberate gap is the medication-safety JUDGMENT — an EMPTY `NullMedicationSafetyProvider` seam invoked-but-no-op throughout (G2 placement, G3 administration), the certified-partner binding point; a homemade checker is a PERMANENT non-goal (medical-device territory). Phases 3–7 (lab / radiology / OR / ED) remain. See [[D-124]].
- `b7a6925` PHARMACY.G5-FIX: stabilize a PRE-EXISTING `PortalUiTest` date/time flake surfaced by G5's CI run (NOT a G5 regression — the follow-up to `a49b803`). The G5 commit's `composer check` was FULLY green locally, but CI (MySQL 8, run at 21:46 UTC) went RED on `portal cancellation enforces the cancel window policy server-side` with `BookingUnavailableException::outsideAvailability` — thrown during the test's SETUP, not any pharmacy code (my diff touches nothing in Scheduling/Portal/Booking; the test last changed at `b91e2d3`). **Root cause:** the test books `$soon = now()->addHours(2)` with a 30-minute service against a resource available `00:00–23:59`; when real `now()` is ~21:30–21:59 the slot starts ~23:46 and its 30-min end crosses midnight past the 23:59 window → `outsideAvailability`. A ~30-min DAILY flake window that only bites when CI happens to run in it (local passed at a different time-of-day) — the canonical "local green ≠ CI green" (see [[verify-ci-directly-github-api]]). **Fix (test-only, deterministic):** `Carbon::setTestNow('2026-06-15 09:00:00')` at the top of the test so `now()+2h`/`now()+10d` are stable in-hours, + a file-level `afterEach(fn () => Carbon::setTestNow())` (a no-op for tests that never freeze) so a frozen instant can never leak forward even on a mid-test failure. No production code, no new test, no count change (the test already passed locally: still Pest 795 / 2-skip / 7056 local; on CI's Redis the 2 Horizon skips run → 797 passed). Verified: `PortalUiTest` 9/9 green (deterministic); Pint `passed`; full composer check re-run green; CI re-run green. See [[D-124]], [[verify-ci-directly-github-api]].

NOT Audit models, AiCore, Comms, or peer verticals Nursing/Dental/Hospital/Pharmacy — the inpatient stay-link is a soft app-layer id). **(2) Theatre + theatre-scheduling — the crux (map §2.1):** the Scheduling `Appointment` is a fixed service-derived slot with NO per-booking duration + NO planned-vs-actual occupancy (`ends_at` never updated; an overrun reads *free* — the gap that made a bed a net-new `Stay`), so a **theatre is a Surgery-OWNED entity** (`theatres`; NOT Scheduling's `Resource`) and a surgical block is a **NET-NEW `theatre_slots`** (BLOCK: `starts_at`+`ends_at`, status booked/in_progress/completed/cancelled, soft `surgical_case_id`) that REUSES the `BookingService::lockResource`→`assertNoOverlap` **INVARIANT** but not the day-board model. **`TheatreSchedulingService`**: `createTheatre` (gate `theatre.manage`) + `bookSlot` (gate `surgery.schedule`) — in ONE `DB::transaction`, `lockTheatre` (`select … for update` on the theatre row) → `assertNoOverlap` (blocking slots over `starts_at < ?end AND ends_at > ?start`, `for update`) → insert. **THE OVERLAP-LOCK PROOF:** `TheatreBookingParallelHammerTest` — 8 OS processes race for the same contested block; No case lifecycle/checklist/consumables/billing/UI this gate. the arch boundary keeps Modules\ED independent of Hospital; the AppServiceProvider/BranchController app-layer precedent). `admit(actor, visit, Bed, StaffProfile, ?reason)`: gate `admission.manage`, then in ONE `DB::transaction` → `AdmissionService::admit($patient, $bed, $clinician, Stay::TYPE_EMERGENCY, …)` [the map found `Stay` already models `emergency`] → `EdVisitService::transition(…, dispositioned, admit, $stay->id)`. **Admission is REUSED, NOT reimplemented or modified** — the proven concurrency-safe bed claim + one-active-stay guard + atomic admit all apply unchanged; STRICTLY ORCHESTRATION — NO new billing/pricing/VAT/line-total math (the surgery-G5 / bed-day-HOSPITAL.G6 shape, mirrored). **(1) TENANT-AUTHORED PRICING:** an `ed` `TariffCatalog` holds each billable as a `TariffItem` (integer minor units, `vat_rate_bp` 0, NO licensed pricing) — the ED attendance/facility fee (`priceAttendance`, fixed `ED-ATTENDANCE` code) + each ED service (`priceService`, own code); via `authorTariff` = `updateOrCreate(catalog,code)`. **(2) CHARGE CAPTURE via the EXISTING `ChargeCaptureService::captureManual`:** `chargeVisit(actor, visit, attendance, serviceCodes[])` (gate `billing.manage`, tenant fail-closed) pushes a capture per billable (attendance ×1 + each selected service ×1); the ENGINE resolves the tariff by code, SNAPSHOTS the fee, computes the line total — ED does NO money math. Service_date = `dispositioned_at ?? arrived_at`. IDEMPOTENT via `ed_visit_charges` (`unique(tenant,charge_id)`; stores NO money). **(3) RECONCILES-TO-THE-UNIT (THE key proofs):** (a) a DISCHARGED patient's `invoiceVisit` (`validateForPatientPeriod` → gather VALIDATED uninvoiced charges on the service day → `createDraftFromCharges(SELF_PAY)` → `issue`) → `ReconciliationEngine::check` passes + **I4 δ=0** with ED charges present; (b) THE COMPOSITE EPISODE — an ADMITTED patient's ED charges are patient charges with a service_date in the stay window, so Hospital's `BedBillingService::invoiceStay` sweeps them onto the stay's discharge invoice ALONGSIDE the bed-days (ED never imports Hospital — the shared engine) → **I4 δ=0** with ED-attendance + BED-DAY-GENERAL on ONE invoice (the whole emergency→inpatient episode bills as one reconciling invoice). Both proven (clock frozen for a deterministic period). **(4) RBAC:** pricing/charge/invoice reuse `billing.manage` (NO new permission — the billing office bills, NOT the ED team; an `ed_physician` [ed.manage, no billing.manage] + reception are REFUSED). **(5) FENCE (financial):** a price is a RATE, never a clinical/appropriateness verdict — the ED-attendance fee is a plain `TariffItem`, **NOT acuity-driven** (proven: two visits triaged ESI 1 vs ESI 5 get the SAME 2000 fee — the acuity is a clinical record, the fee is a tariff, kept separate); fees snapshotted (a later re-price never changes a past charge); the money-math grep over `Modules\ED\src` + the app-layer composer is CLEAN (`line_total_minor`/`vat_total_minor`/`subtotal_minor`/`vatMinor`/`intdiv(` — zero); `ed_visit_charges` carries no money/acuity/severity column. **(6) UI (P0D.GU):** `EdBillingController` (`/ed/visits/{visit}/billing`, string-id FIX.1 — pricing surface + capture + invoice; an admitted visit shows "joins the stay invoice" instead of a visit-level invoice) + `ED/Billing.vue`; the disposition page links to it (billing.manage-gated); `ed.billing.*` i18n. FIX.5 smoke EXTENDED (`/ed/visits/{id}/billing` GET 200; reception charge 403). No audit hook for the link table (Charge/Invoice audited by Billing — the `DispenseCharge`/`SurgicalCaseCharge` precedent). No existing behavior test modified; **the reconciliation/fence/immutability/clinical-safety/triage-eval + G1–G5 + Phase-1/2/5 suites stay green** (the engine is REUSED, not changed). Added `tests/Feature/ED/EdBillingTest.php` (7). VERIFIED locally: ED billing 7 + smoke 3 passed; npm build green; PHPStan L5 on ED + app-layer `[OK]`; Pint passed; money-math grep clean. composer check + CI verified after push. **PHASE 6 (EMERGENCY DEPARTMENT) COMPLETE** — G1→G6: an ED runs end-to-end (arrival/EdVisit → triage[nurse-assigned acuity] → tracking board → clinical documentation[reuse Clinical] → disposition + the ED→ADT handoff[admit reuses AdmissionService → inpatient Stay] → billing[reconciles-to-the-unit, incl. the composite emergency→inpatient episode on one invoice]). Deliberate seam: COMPUTED triage acuity = certified-partner / permanent non-goal (the empty `TriageAcuityProvider`). Remaining hospital phases: Lab (Phase 3) + Radiology (Phase 4) — mostly integration shells pending HL7/FHIR + PACS/DICOM partners. See [[D-135]].

Proven: the bound impl is `ManualLabConnectivity` (transmit no-op; ingest throws), and the grep over `Modules\Lab\src` finds no HL7/analyzer client (`implements LabConnectivity`/`FhirClient`/`Hl7Client`/etc — none). **(4) FENCE:** `reference_range` is RECORDED reference data (displayed beside a result later) — NO computed abnormal/high/low/critical flag; `lab_tests` carries no abnormal/critical/flag/grade/score column; the grep over `Modules\Lab\src` finds no grade/flag-a-result logic (enforced in G4, established here). **(5) RBAC (additive):** perms `lab.catalog` + `lab.result` (ordering reuses the existing `order.manage`); roles `lab_tech`/`pathologist`(lab lead — +lab.catalog)/`phlebotomist`; org_admin gains both; reprovision migration `add_lab_permissions` (the `add_ed_permissions` precedent); `RbacNegativeSweepTest` untouched. **(6) AUDIT (app-layer):** `LabTest.created`→`lab.test_authored` (tenant-level; the order/result are audited by Clinical). **(7) UI (P0D.GU):** `LabCatalogController` (`/lab/catalog`, string-id FIX.1 — author/deactivate/seed) + `Lab/Catalog.vue`; `lab.catalog.*` i18n. FIX.5 smoke EXTENDED (`/lab/catalog` GET 200; reception author 403). **NO order/specimen/result UI this gate** (G2–G6). - `48cacb2` LAB.G3: specimen tracking (net-new; accession + legal-only state machine; append-only). illegal moves throw. **`specimen_events`** (BelongsToTenant, LogsReads, **APPEND-ONLY** — model guards + DB triggers `specimen_events_no_update`/`_no_delete`, the `ed_visit_events` recipe): one immutable row for collection + one per transition. **(2) `SpecimenService`:** `collect` (gate `lab.result`, tenant fail-closed; generate the accession + create the specimen[collected] + the `collected` event, atomically) + `transition` (gate `lab.result`, legal-only, rejection needs a reason, append-only event) + `forOrder`/`forPatient`. **Accession generation** mirrors the `MrnGenerator` recipe — lock the tenant row so concurrent collections serialise, take max `ACC-%`, `sprintf('ACC-%06d')`, ensure unique (proven sequential ACC-000001/2, unique per tenant). **(3) THE CLINICAL ORDER IS REUSED + UNTOUCHED:** collection records the SPECIMEN; reception refused); viewing = `patient.view` (read-logged). **(6) AUDIT (app-layer):** `Specimen.created`→`specimen.accessioned`; `SpecimenEvent.created`→`specimen.<event_type>` (patient-scoped). **(7) UI (P0D.GU):** `SpecimenController` (`/lab/orders/{labOrder}/specimens` show + collect; `/lab/specimens/{specimen}/transition`, string-id FIX.1) + `Lab/Specimens.vue` (collect + track state + append-only history); `lab.specimens.*` i18n. Per `docs/HOSPITAL-PHASE3-LAB-MAP.md`. **Opening:** top commit `fb52684` (LAB.G4), CI green. **REUSE-first — closes the order → result → review loop by SURFACING the EXISTING resulted → reviewed step for lab orders; the review flow is NOT reinvented.** **(1) `LabResultService::reviewWorklist(actor)`** (gate `order.manage` — the review permission, the same gate the existing `OrderService::toReview` uses) returns the ORDERING clinician's OWN resulted-but-not-reviewed lab orders — `LabOrder` whose reused Clinical `Order` is at `status=resulted` AND `ordered_by`=the actor — eager-loading `order.orderableItem`+`order.results`, ordered by **resulted-time** (the latest `OrderResult.entered_at`, newest first — a FACT). **(2) THE REVIEW ACTION REUSES the existing endpoint:** `LabReviewController` (invokable, `/lab/results/review`, the Clinical `OrdersReviewController` analogue lab-scoped) renders each row (patient, test, the RAW result value + the DISPLAYED reference range [LAB.G4], the recorded STAT flag, resulted-time) with `reviewUrl = route('clinical.orders.review')` — the worklist POSTs `order_id` to the EXISTING `OrderController::review` → `OrderService::markReviewed` (resulted → reviewed, gated `order.manage`). **NO new review endpoint/service/model/migration** — pure surfacing. **(3) THE FENCE:** the worklist shows FACTS + the LAB.G2 recorded STAT flag — the server orders by resulted-time (a fact); it computes NO priority/urgency ranking, NO critical-result flag, NO review-first judgment. Staff MAY sort client-side by the recorded STAT flag OR time (a recorded fact — the ED-board precedent). PROVEN: a **later-resulted ROUTINE order outranks an earlier-resulted STAT order** (ordered by time, NOT by STAT — clock travelled for determinism); the Inertia payload key-sweep finds no computed-judgment key (priority_score/urgency/rank/score/critical/abnormal/flag/review_first/deterioration/…; the recorded `priority` flag key is allowed); no computePriority/rankByUrgency/priorityScore/flagCritical/isCritical/reviewFirst logic in `Modules\Lab\src`. The result stays raw value + displayed range (G4's fence carried — no computed abnormal flag). **(4) RBAC:** `order.manage` (the ordering clinician — the doctor reaches it; reception [no order.manage] refused, service + HTTP 403); tenant+patient scoped (a resulted order in one tenant is invisible in another's worklist — BelongsToTenant fail-closed). **(5) AUDIT:** no new hook — reviewing is audited by Clinical's `order.reviewed` (the reused `markReviewed`). **(6) UI (P0D.GU):** `Lab/Review.vue` (Eucalyptus Glow; client-side sort by the recorded flag/time; mark-reviewed via the reused endpoint + a link to the G4 results page); `lab.review.*` i18n. I4, the composite tie-out). 15 min`, never server-read). **THE BUFFER-LIKE DECISION = (a) read-only for BOTH timeout + idle-wipe:** they are platform config/constant not meant to be tenant-set; an editable server setting for the PWA wipe would be IGNORED by the already-built PWA (dishonest), so read-only is the honest render — no fake editable control, no setting nothing reads. **THE GATE (no path to disable 2FA):** proven by a test — no `admin.security.update` route, only GET/HEAD verbs under `admin/security`, and the middleware still redirects an un-enrolled user to enrollment; the card is a window, the middleware stays authoritative. **(2) UI (P0D.GU, P1 visual language):** `resources/js/pages/Admin/Security.vue` (glass Card + eucardIn, 2FA locked badge + read-only session/wipe chips + "Enforced platform-wide" note; NO form, NO save), a cross-link card on `Admin/Settings.vue` (→ `/admin/security`), `securitySettings.*` + `settings.security.*` i18n (en) — unique top-level block name (the P3 duplicate-key lesson). `admin.manage` (reception 403). **Nothing written → no audit, no tenant write.** **NO existing behavior test modified; the 2FA/fence + all vertical suites stay green; correctly-more-real items (deeper RBAC catalog, richer profile/billing, read-only identity, honesty card, last-admin guard, Branches/Kiosks) un-regressed.** Added `tests/Feature/Admin/SecuritySettingsTest.php` (5 tests / 22 assertions: renders 2FA mandatory-locked + session 120 + wipe 15; viewing writes no Setting; THE GATE no-disable-path [no update route, GET/HEAD only]; middleware still enforces on an un-enrolled user; RBAC 403). create pending + email the accept link; audit `staff_invite.created`), `resend`/`revoke` (pending-only; audited), `accept` (resolve the token UNSCOPED via `withoutTenantContext`+`system` [the `PortalAccessService` pattern — `TenantScope` constrains to the current tenant even in system mode, so a guest must FORGET context first], set the tenant FROM the invite, provision the User via the REAL path `User::create` + `RoleAssignment::create` [auto-audits `role.assigned`], consume the token, audit `staff_invite.accepted`). **(3)** `StaffInviteController` (admin.manage store/resend/revoke, string-id FIX.1) + public throttled `StaffInviteAcceptController` (`/invite/{token}` show+accept; `throttle:10,1`; on accept `Auth::login` → `/app` → the mandatory-2FA middleware forces enrollment — the existing onboarding). `UserRoleController::index` EXTENDED (additive) with the pending-invites list + `inviteUrl`. **THE FENCE (governance preserved, tested):** RBAC REFLECT-ONLY — a test asserts there is NO permission-edit route (the only `/admin/roles` POST is `assign`); the invite grants a TEMPLATE role, never per-permission; single-use (a consumed token can't provision again — service-authoritative; over HTTP the logged-in invitee is bounced to 2FA first); expiring + revoke invalidate; Practice/Scheduling/Online booking/Agents/Notifications/Team & roles/Security; active-item state; "Online booking" → `/settings` since it has no dedicated surface — no faked dead section) in a shared `SettingsLayout.vue` (AppLayout + the sub-nav column); the SIX Settings pages (Settings/Agents/Scheduling/Security/Notifications/Roles) switched AppLayout→SettingsLayout. **UI (P0D.GU, P1 visual):** `Admin/Roles.vue` (invite form + pending list w/ resend/revoke; catalog stays read-only), `Auth/AcceptInvite.vue` (GuestLayout; name+password; invalid-token state), `staffInvite.*` + `settingsNav.*` i18n (en, unique blocks; the email placeholder's literal `@` ESCAPED `{'@'}` — a vue-i18n message-syntax guard, caught in browser-verify as a compiler SyntaxError). **NO existing behavior test modified; the electric fence + RBAC/last-admin + all vertical suites stay green; correctly-more-real items (deeper 26-role/40-perm catalog, richer profile/billing, read-only identity, honesty card, last-admin guard, Branches/Kiosks) un-regressed.** Added `tests/Feature/Admin/StaffInviteTest.php` (9 tests / 42 assertions: create pending+role+audit+email+RBAC 403; - `19d39c4` APPROVAL.P4: Approval Queue edit-before-sending — wire the wireframe's [Edit before sending] over the EXISTING `editedPayload` approve support. Fourth fix part of the APPROVAL-QUEUE chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/APPROVAL-QUEUE-DIFF.md` §8 item 2 (now **RESOLVED**). **Purely additive over the existing support — NO second approve path, NO new backend judgment; an edit is NOT a bypass.** **Opening:** top commit `cb79b27` (docs reconciliation on `aac95c8` APPROVAL.P3), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder (2 real pending actions + a seeded demo.echo for the edit demo). **Step 1 (confirmed, read-only):** `ApprovalQueue::approve($action,$reviewer,?$editedPayload)` (`Modules/AiCore/src/Services/ApprovalQueue.php`) ALREADY accepted an edited payload: it re-authorises the reviewer against `$tool->definition()->permission`, `assertPending`, uses `$payload = $editedPayload ?? $action->input_payload`, re-runs `$tool->execute($payload)` (which re-grounds/re-derives from live state), and records the `edited_payload` column. The live controller was approving AS-IS (no edited payload) and the UI had no editor. **(1) Service provenance (extend, not judge):** `approve()` now stamps human-edited when `$editedPayload !== null` — `result['human_edited']=true` on the executed result (beside the tool's own provenance, e.g. DraftReplyTool's `ai_assisted`), and `metadata:['human_edited'=>true]` on the `approved` + `executed` `ai_interactions` rows. The `edited_payload` column already distinguished edited from unedited; this adds the marker to the posted result + the append-only ledger. Unedited approve = null editedPayload → NONE of these (unchanged). **(2) Controller** (`app/Http/Controllers/AiApprovalQueueController.php`): `approve()` reads an OPTIONAL `edited_payload` (`validate(['edited_payload'=>'sometimes|array'])` — still can't raise autonomy or swap the tool) and passes it to `ApprovalQueue::approve`; flash `approved_edited` when edited. `presentPending` surfaces a new `inputPayload` (the tool INPUT execute re-grounds — distinct from the read-only `proposed_output` preview). **(3) UI** (`resources/js/pages/Governance/ApprovalQueue.vue`): an **Edit before sending** pill (rendered only when `canReview`) opens an inline editor seeded with `inputPayload` (JSON); **Approve edited** parses it (invalid JSON refused client-side) and POSTs `{edited_payload}` to the SAME approve route; reuses the P1/P2 visual + the P3 approve-contract caption (applies to the edited approve too). **(4) i18n:** unique `aiQueue.edit.*` sub-block + `flash.approved_edited` (en). **THE GATE (unchanged — the edit changes CONTENT, not the gate):** the edited approve STILL re-authorises + re-grounds + asserts-pending — proven by 5 new tests. **RBAC** canReview-gated (server re-authorises regardless — no weaker path). **NO existing behavior test modified; the electric-fence eval + AiApprovalQueue/anatomy/caption/chrome suites stay green.** Added `tests/Feature/Governance/ApprovalEditBeforeSendingTest.php` (5 tests / ~35 assertions: an edited approve posts the EDITED payload [demo.echo `result.message` = the edit] + records human-edited [result + ledger metadata]; an UNEDITED approve carries no human_edited marker [distinguishable]; an edited approve STILL re-authorises [ai.manage-only reviewer on a scheduler.suggest_slots action → 403, nothing runs]; STILL asserts still-pending [editing an executed action → AiCoreException, result not overwritten]; an edited CLINICAL draft [clinical.summarize_since_last_visit / note.write] still re-authorised → a reviewer without note.write denied [edit does not lower the clinical bar]). BROWSER-VERIFIED (Praxis Lindenhof, org_admin): edit a demo.echo draft → "Approve edited" → flash "…posted through the same gate — recorded as human-edited."; DB confirms `result.message`='EDITED by the reviewer in the browser', `result.human_edited`=true, `edited_payload` recorded, ledger executed row `metadata.human_edited`=true; Reject still works ("Action rejected. Nothing was executed."). VERIFIED locally: `composer check` FULLY green — Pint `passed`, PHPStan L5 `[OK] No errors`, **Pest 1009 passed** (13948 assertions); `composer test:smoke` green (3); `npm run build` green (ApprovalQueue chunk). composer check + CI verified after push. Remaining APPROVAL parts: governance stat strip, fence-refused modelling, bulk-approve (excludes clinical/financial), resolved search/filters/reviewer/grouping. See [[AiCore]].
- `2b51150` APPROVAL.P5: Approval Queue — model fence-refused as a countable outcome + the governance stat strip from REAL data (no faked numbers). Fifth fix part of the APPROVAL-QUEUE chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/APPROVAL-QUEUE-DIFF.md` §8 items 4 (fence-refused modelling) + 3 (stat strip) — both now **RESOLVED**. **The fence is UNCHANGED — only the RECORDING is new; every stat is REAL or honestly absent.** **Opening:** top commit `19d39c4` (APPROVAL.P4), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder (2 real pending actions incl. a handoff draft). **Step 1 (confirmed):** the fence refuses at approve time in `DraftReplyTool::execute` — a handed-off/empty draft throws (`preview()` re-grounds → handoff:true) — but that refusal was NOT a countable outcome: the controller caught the `AiCoreException` and left the action PENDING; the only pre-draft fence (InboxAgent clinical refusal) writes an `ai_interactions` `refused` row but creates no AgentAction. So a fence refusal at the queue was not queryable/listable. **(1) The refusal, RECORDED (not the fence changed):** new `Modules\AiCore\Exceptions\FenceRefusalException` extends `AiCoreException` (so every existing `catch (AiCoreException)` still catches it); `DraftReplyTool::execute` now throws IT (same condition, same message, same moment). `ApprovalQueue::approve()` wraps `tool->execute()` in `catch (FenceRefusalException)` → `recordFenceRefusal()`: transitions the action to the new `AgentAction::STATUS_FENCE_REFUSED` (+ `fence_refused_at`; the fence's OWN reason kept in `rejection_reason`, distinguished from a human reject by the status), writes an **append-only `fence_refused` `ai_interactions` row** (metadata.reason), fires `AgentActionLifecycleChanged($action,'fence_refused')` → the EXISTING generic glue audits `agent_action.fence_refused` (no glue change) → re-throws. Nothing executes. Migration `2026_08_22_000001` adds ONLY `fence_refused_at` (the `status` column is a plain string — the new value needs no schema change). **CONFIRMED the fence's behavior/when-it-fires is UNCHANGED:** a non-handoff action still executes; the electric-fence eval (`tests/Evals/`, 37/398) is untouched + green; inbox/live agent suites green (the exception-type change is transparent — it's still an AiCoreException). **(2) The stat strip — REAL data only** (`AiApprovalQueueController::index` passes `stats`): **pending** = real pending count; **fenceRefused** = real `fence_refused` count (danger-tint per wireframe); **approvedPct (30d)** = executed ÷ resolved-in-window (executed+rejected+fence_refused) from real timestamps; **avgReviewMinutes (30d)** = avg(resolvedAt−createdAt) from real timestamps. **THE HONESTY RULE:** approvedPct + avgReviewMinutes are computed ONLY when there is a real denominator — with **no resolved action in the window they are `null` → the UI renders an honest "—"**, never 0/estimate. So pending + fence count are always real; approved-% + avg-review are real when resolved records exist, honestly absent otherwise. Controller: `approve()` catches `FenceRefusalException` FIRST (before AiCoreException, subclass ordering) → redirect with status `fence_refused` (honest flash, not the reviewer's error); resolved query + `presentResolved` now include `fence_refused` (a new `resolvedAt()` helper coalesces executed/rejected/fence timestamps). **(3) UI** (`resources/js/pages/Governance/ApprovalQueue.vue`): a 4-tile stat strip (Pending / Approved·30d / Avg review·30d / Refused-by-fence[danger]) with `orDash()` for null metrics; a warning-tint `fence_refused` flash; the Resolved badge gained the `fence_refused` (warning) variant via `resolvedStatusClass()`. **(4) i18n** unique `aiQueue.stats.*` sub-block + `flash.fence_refused` + `status.fence_refused` (en). **NO existing behavior test modified.** Added `tests/Feature/Governance/ApprovalFenceRefusedTest.php` (4 tests / 55 assertions: a real handoff draft [Comms Thread + non-groundable patient message] approved → recorded fence_refused [status + fence_refused_at + reason + append-only ledger + audit], nothing executes, no staff message; only the fence path records fence_refused [a clean demo.echo still executes — trigger unchanged]; the stat strip shows real pending + fence 0 + honest null approvedPct/avgReview when nothing resolved; the fence count=1 + approvedPct=50 + avgReview≠null computed from real resolved records via the controller approve). BROWSER-VERIFIED (Praxis Lindenhof, org_admin): approving the seeded handoff draft → flash "The electric fence refused this action…recorded as fence-refused", the Refused-by-fence tile → 1, it appears in Resolved as Fence-refused; pending/approved-%/avg-review show real values. **CAUGHT (the lying-exit-code memory):** the first `composer check` reported exit 0 but Pint had FAILED (FenceRefusalException empty-body + a test unused import) — re-ran Pint --test manually, auto-fixed, re-ran the full check. VERIFIED locally: `composer check` FULLY green — Pint `passed`, PHPStan L5 `[OK]`, **Pest 1013 passed**; `composer test:smoke` green; `npm run build` green. composer check + CI verified after push. Remaining APPROVAL parts: bulk-approve (excludes clinical/financial), resolved search/filters/reviewer/grouping. See [[AiCore]].
- `4afdd3e` APPROVAL.P6: Approval Queue — the Resolved view: search + status/date/reviewer filters + grouping, over REAL resolved data. Sixth fix part of the APPROVAL-QUEUE chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/APPROVAL-QUEUE-DIFF.md` §8 item 6 (now **RESOLVED**). **Every filter/search/count maps to a REAL field; no fabricated entry/attribute; tenant + RBAC scoped.** **Opening:** top commit `2b51150` (APPROVAL.P5), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder. **(1) Controller** (`app/Http/Controllers/AiApprovalQueueController.php` `index` + new helpers): the resolved section is now server-side searchable/filterable over the REAL resolved set (executed/rejected/P5 fence_refused). **`allowedToolKeys($tools,$actor)`** = the tool keys whose declared permission the actor holds (the SAME per-tool gate approve enforces) → the base resolved query `whereIn('tool_key', $allowed)` **RBAC-scopes** the list AND the counts (a reviewer never sees/counts actions they couldn't act on; unregistered key excluded fail-closed); tenant-scoped by `BelongsToTenant`. **`resolvedFilters($request)`** reads real query params (rstatus∈all/executed/rejected/fence_refused; rq; rreviewer; rfrom/rto — dates regex-guarded). **`applyResolvedFilters`** applies search+reviewer+date (NOT the status pill): the free-text search matches `tool_key`/`agent`/`feature`/`why`/`rejection_reason` (real text columns — NOT the nested payload, stated honestly); reviewer→`reviewed_by`; date→`COALESCE(executed_at,rejected_at,fence_refused_at)` (portable). **Real per-status counts** via `selectRaw('status,count(*)')->groupBy('status')` over the filtered base (before the status pill). List ordered by the COALESCE resolved timestamp DESC, limit 20. `presentResolved` enriched: `toolName` (real declared name), **`reviewerName`** (bulk-resolved from `users.name`), **`systemAttributed`** (true for fence_refused → the fence, NOT the human who clicked; reviewerName null), `rejectionReason` (reject reason OR fence reason). `resolvedReviewers` = distinct real human reviewers (executed/rejected, RBAC-allowed) for the dropdown. **(2) UI** (`resources/js/pages/Governance/ApprovalQueue.vue`): the Resolved view (behind the P1 toggle) gained a "Search resolved…" box (debounced 300ms), status filter pills with real counts (All/Approved/Rejected/Fence-refused[danger dot]), a reviewer `<select>` + from/to date inputs + a Clear; results **grouped by day** (client-side over the returned rows — presentational), each row: tool name·agent, attribution ("Resolved by {reviewer}·{time}" or "Refused by the electric fence (system)·{time}"), the real reason in quotes, and the outcome badge (`resolvedStatusClass`). Filters push via `router.get('/governance/approvals', params, {preserveState, only:['resolved','resolvedCounts','resolvedReviewers','resolvedFilters']})`. **(3) i18n** extended the unique `aiQueue.resolved.*` sub-block (search/filterAll/filterApproved/filterRejected/filterFenceRefused with {count}, anyReviewer, dateFrom/dateTo, clear, noneForFilters, byReviewer, bySystem). **THE HONESTY:** every row + count is a real record; the Fence-refused pill uses the P5 `fence_refused` status (system-attributed + fence reason); no fabricated attribute is filterable. **NO existing behavior test modified; the electric-fence eval + AiApprovalQueue + all suites stay green.** Added `tests/Feature/Governance/ApprovalResolvedViewTest.php` (4 tests / 110 assertions: real outcome badge + reviewer/system attribution + real reason + real counts; the Fence-refused filter returns only the system-attributed fence rows with the fence reason; search matches the real `why` column + the reviewer filter maps to `reviewed_by`; tenant + RBAC scoping — an ai.manage-only reviewer sees the demo.echo but NOT the comms.draft_reply fence refusal, and counts reflect only permitted, a second tenant sees none). BROWSER-VERIFIED (Praxis Lindenhof, org_admin): seeded an approved/executed + rejected-with-reason + fence_refused; the Resolved view groups by day with correct badges + reviewer/system attribution + reasons; search filters by a real field; the Approved/Rejected/Fence-refused pills filter by real status with real counts; the reviewer + date sub-filters work. **CAUGHT the lying-exit-code again earlier in the chain — always re-run Pint --test.** VERIFIED locally: `composer check` FULLY green — Pint `passed`, PHPStan L5 `[OK]`, **Pest 1017 passed**; `composer test:smoke` green; `npm run build` green (ApprovalQueue chunk). composer check + CI verified after push. Remaining APPROVAL part: bulk-approve (MUST exclude clinical/financial — a test to prove exclusion). See [[AiCore]].
- `ea0e9b3` APPROVAL.P7: Approval Queue — bulk-approve (LOW-RISK only; clinical+financial EXCLUDED server-side; per-item gate). FINAL part of the APPROVAL-QUEUE chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/APPROVAL-QUEUE-DIFF.md` §8 item 5 (now **RESOLVED**) — **the Approval Queue page is now at FULL wireframe parity**. **A LOOP over the real per-action gate, NOT a batch shortcut; the clinical/financial exclusion is ENFORCED SERVER-SIDE.** **Opening:** top commit `4afdd3e` (APPROVAL.P6), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder. **Step 1 (confirmed):** risk class = the tool's REAL `ToolDefinition::category` (operational/clinical/financial) + the existing `isClinicalOrFinancial()` helper — NO invented risk field. Low-risk/bulk-eligible = operational; clinical (note.write/clinical.*) + financial (billing.*) are excluded. **(1) Controller** `AiApprovalQueueController::bulkApprove` (new POST `governance.approvals.bulk_approve`, `ai.manage`): validates `ids[]`; per id — loads the action (missing/cross-tenant → skipped fail-closed); resolves the tool category via `ToolRegistry` (unregistered → skipped fail-closed); **THE SAFETY GATE** — `if isClinicalOrFinancial() → excluded` (refused for bulk BEFORE approve, so a FORGED clinical/financial id is never approved this way, even for a reviewer holding note.write/billing.manage); else calls `ApprovalQueue::approve($action,$actor)` in try/catch(FenceRefusalException→fence fired, recorded fence_refused P5, skipped / AuthorizationException→per-item RBAC, skipped / AiCoreException→non-pending, skipped). Redirects with `status='bulk'` + a `bulk`=[approved,excluded,skipped] summary. `presentPending` gained `bulkEligible` (=canReview && category∉{clinical,financial}) — a UX hint; the server re-enforces. **(2) HandleInertiaRequests** shares a new `bulk` flash key (one line, additive). **(3) UI** (`Governance/ApprovalQueue.vue`): per-card checkbox on bulkEligible cards only; clinical/financial show an "Individual review only" chip; a bulk-action bar (select-all-low-risk + "clinical/financial need individual review" note + selected count + Approve selected + Clear); [Approve selected] POSTs the ids; a bulk-summary flash. **(4) i18n** unique `aiQueue.bulk.*` sub-block. **THE FENCE/HONESTY:** bulk is a loop over the full gate (re-authorise + re-ground + assert-pending per item — no skip); clinical+financial NEVER bulk-approved (server-enforced, forged request refused for that item); fence still fires per item (P5); per-item RBAC; risk from the tool's real category; no new judgment. **NO existing behavior test modified; the electric-fence eval (37/398) + AiApprovalQueue + all suites stay green.** Added `tests/Feature/Governance/ApprovalBulkApproveTest.php` (4 tests / 23 assertions: low-risk bulk approves each through the full gate [executed + reviewed_by]; **THE EXCLUSION PROOF** — a bulk incl. a clinical AND a financial action refuses BOTH server-side [they stay pending] while the low-risk item approves [approved=1,excluded=2]; a fenced bulk item is recorded fence_refused not forced [handoff→fence_refused, result null]; per-item RBAC skips an unauthorized operational item [ai_only reviewer: demo.echo executed, scheduler.suggest_slots stays pending]). BROWSER-VERIFIED (Praxis Lindenhof, org_admin): low-risk actions bulk-selectable (checkbox) + [Approve selected] approves each; clinical + financial actions show "Individual review only" (no checkbox); the bulk summary flash reports approved/excluded/skipped. Two pages done in the wireframe-parity pass: Admin Settings + Approval Queue. Branches next.** See [[AiCore]].

Public + portal branch lists use `scopeOnlineBookable`; public + portal **slots endpoints return `[]`** for a suspended branch. **(3) Admin surface:** `BranchController::onlineBookings` (POST `/admin/branches/{branch}/online-bookings`, admin.manage) → `BranchService::setOnlineBookings`, validated boolean, redirect status onlineBookingsEnabled/Suspended; `phone` added to `validateBranch`; index payload adds `phone` + `accepts_online_bookings` + `onlineBookingsUrl`. **(4) Distinct audit:** the `Branch::updated` hook now derives `branch.online_bookings_enabled`/`_suspended` (when accepts_online_bookings changed) vs the existing activated/deactivated/updated. **(5) UI (minimal — full master-detail visual is P2+):** `Admin/Branches.vue` gained an online-booking status pill + Turn-on/Suspend button (posts to onlineBookingsUrl) + a phone input (create + per-branch details); `branchesAdmin.onlineBookings.*` + `fields.phone` + 2 flash keys (unique i18n sub-block). **THE GUARD DISTINCTION (the crux, verified):** soft-suspend (`accepts_online_bookings=false`, `active=true`) is a SEPARATE action from hard deactivate (`active=false`); soft-suspend is ALWAYS allowed (never strands care) + does NOT deactivate; the HARD deactivate guard is UNCHANGED — still blocked while future appointments exist (regression test). **FENCE/honesty:** accepts_online_bookings REALLY gates online slots + the online write path (proven), not a cosmetic toggle; the hard guard stays real + unweakened. **NO existing behavior test modified; W8b/W8c + booking/slot-finder + electric-fence eval + all suites stay green.** Added `tests/Feature/Scheduling/BranchOnlineBookingTest.php` (6 tests / 36 assertions: soft-suspend stops NEW online bookings [online write refused] but keeps existing appts + staff book() + the day-board slot finder; Second fix part of the BRANCHES chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/BRANCHES-DIFF.md` §6 (now **RESOLVED**) — a REAL invariant-guarded state, NOT a cosmetic label. **Opening:** top commit `2418db1` (BRANCH.P1), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder. **Study:** no per-tenant default existed; the implicit default was "first branch" (`Branch::query()->firstOrFail()` in some billing charge-attribution paths; `StaffProfile.primary_branch_id` is a DISTINCT per-staff concept). `is_primary` formalizes the tenant default; billing paths NOT rewired (existing behavior unchanged; documented follow-up). **(1) Migration** `2026_08_24_000001` (additive, `is_primary` bool default false) + **backfill**: every existing tenant → exactly one primary (earliest-created active branch, else earliest overall — aligns with the implicit first-branch default). **(2) THE INVARIANT (exactly one primary per tenant, ALWAYS):** seeded by the `Branch::booted` **`creating` hook** (the tenant's FIRST branch is primary — covers ALL paths incl. the demo seeders' direct `Branch::query()->create`, which bypass BranchService); `BranchService::setPrimary` is an ATOMIC swap (clear current + set target in one tx — never zero/two); `setActive(false)` on the primary REASSIGNS to the earliest other active branch (atomic) before deactivating, and the SOLE branch KEEPS primary even when deactivated (never zero); NO un-set action (the flag only moves); `ensurePrimary()` = idempotent runtime mirror of the backfill (safety net + tested). **(3) Interaction with deactivate — the P1 HARD guard UNCHANGED:** `BranchController::deactivate` runs the P1 hard guard (blocked while future appointments exist) FIRST; only after it passes does the reassign run in `setActive`. Set-primary requires the target be ACTIVE (an inactive branch can't be the default → `inactive_primary` error). **(4) Controller/route:** `setPrimary` action + `POST /admin/branches/{branch}/primary` (admin.manage), index payload adds `is_primary` + `setPrimaryUrl`; audit `branch.primary_set` derived in the `Branch::updated` hook. **(5) UI (minimal — full visual P4):** `Admin/Branches.vue` "Primary" badge on the primary + "Set as primary" button on active non-primary branches; `branchesAdmin.primary.*` + `primarySet` flash (unique i18n sub-block). **INVARIANT/honesty:** is_primary is REAL + enforced (exactly one, always) — proven across set-primary, deactivate-primary-with-successor, sole-branch-deactivate, and backfill. **NO existing behavior test modified; the P1 soft-suspend/hard-deactivate guards + Branches/booking/fence-eval + all suites stay green; correctly-more-real un-regressed.** Added `tests/Feature/Platform/BranchPrimaryTest.php` (8 tests / 45 assertions: first branch primary + later not [via service AND direct Branch::create — the seeder path]; set-primary atomic [exactly one before+after]; inactive branch can't be made primary [never orphaned]; deactivating the primary reassigns to another active branch [never zero]; sole branch always primary + keeps it when deactivated; ensurePrimary from zero-primary AND multi-primary → exactly one [backfill logic]; RBAC admin.manage [reception 403] + tenant-scoped [cross-tenant 404] + audited [branch.primary_set]). - `1e8986a` BRANCH.P3: Admin Branches — practitioner-resource TYPE read-only (correctness FIX — a practitioner can't be retyped as room/chair/vehicle). Third fix part of the BRANCHES chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/BRANCHES-DIFF.md` §6/item 8 (now **RESOLVED**). **A FIX (stop an invalid edit), NOT a trim (no functionality removed).** **Opening:** top commit `1a9ac1e` (BRANCH.P2), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder. **The model (what distinguishes a practitioner):** `Resource.type` ∈ practitioner/room/chair/vehicle; a **practitioner** is PERSON-BACKED — it carries a `staff_profile_id` (→ `StaffProfile`), and the model invariant `assertValidResource()` lets ONLY a practitioner link a staff profile. Facility resources (room/chair/vehicle) are free-standing. Practitioner resources are provisioned via People (staff), NOT admin-created here (`ResourceController::CREATABLE_TYPES` = room/chair/vehicle). **THE BUG:** `ResourceController::update` ran `validateResource` (facility-only `in:`) + passed `type` to update for ALL resources — so a practitioner's type would be forced toward a facility type (a room/chair/vehicle select can't even represent a practitioner). **THE FIX:** `update` now branches — for a **practitioner** it validates the NAME only and IGNORES any submitted `type` (the practitioner stays a practitioner; a forged `type=room` has no effect); for a **facility** it keeps name+type editable within the facility-only `in:room,chair,vehicle` rule (so a facility also can't become a practitioner → 422). UI (`Admin/Branches.vue`): a practitioner row renders a read-only "Practitioner" label (`resources.type.practitioner` i18n) instead of the room/chair/vehicle `<select>`; facility rows keep the editable select. **A FIX not a trim:** practitioner name + status (activate/deactivate, still guarded) stay editable; facility-resource CRUD is UNCHANGED; only the invalid type-edit is stopped. server-enforced + tenant-scoped + admin.manage-gated. **NO existing behavior test modified; W8c ResourceCrud + P1/P2 + electric-fence eval + all suites stay green; correctly-more-real (practitioner rows in the roster) un-regressed — kept, just shown with their true non-editable type.** Added `tests/Feature/Scheduling/BranchPractitionerResourceTest.php` (6 tests / 30 assertions: the payload exposes type=practitioner [so the UI renders read-only] + resourceTypes stays facility-only; a forged retype of a practitioner→room is IGNORED [stays practitioner, name still updates]; a facility keeps its editable type [room→chair works]; a facility→practitioner retype is refused [422]; practitioner status stays editable [deactivate/activate work]; RBAC admin.manage [reception 403] + tenant-scoped [cross-tenant 404]). NOTE: the test practitioner has staff_profile_id=null on purpose — it proves the CONTROLLER-level fix works on its own (not relying on the model's staff-profile guard). VERIFIED locally: `composer check` FULLY green — Pint `passed`, PHPStan L5 `[OK]`, **Pest 1041 passed**; `composer test:smoke` green; `npm run build` green. composer check + CI verified after push. Remaining BRANCHES part: the master-detail restructure + glass/eucardIn/terracotta + create modal wizard (P4 — the full visual). See [[Scheduling]] / [[Platform]].

- `76f5a70` AGENT.P1: AiCore — the Agent entity + the capped effective-level resolver (governed container; min(config, tool ceiling, role ceiling); config only narrows). First fix part of the AGENT-TOOL-CONFIG chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/AGENT-TOOL-CONFIG-DIFF.md` §6 (the modelling decision — RESOLVED as a real Agent entity built as a GOVERNED CONTAINER). **NO UI this gate — the entity + resolver + the clamp proof.** **Opening:** top commit `0590723` (the audit), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder. **The real model (confirmed):** per-tool autonomy (`ai.autonomy.<tool.key>` settings) via `AutonomyPolicy` (`cap()` = per-tool `autonomyCeiling` + clinical/financial→approve; `set()` clamps; `effectiveCeiling()`=cap(AUTO)); `AgentRuntime::runTool` resolves the per-tool `levelFor` at call time; agents were CODE CLASSES only (Inbox/FollowUp/ClinicalSummary/Dispatch/Billing + FrontDesk) — **no Agent DB entity**; role RBAC gates each tool's `permission` at approve. **(1) The Agent entity** (`Modules\AiCore\Models\Agent`, `agents` table, migration `2026_08_25_000001`, BelongsToTenant): per-tenant, `key` (→ code-class agent), `name`, CONFIGURED `autonomy_level` (default suggest), `status` (active/paused), `tool_keys` whitelist (JSON). `AgentRegistry::AGENTS` = the 6 canonical agents (inbox/scheduler/recall/clinical_summary/dispatch/billing) covering all 10 real governed tools. **Additive migration + backfill for existing tenants; new tenants seeded via a `Tenant::created` hook** (app-layer AppServiceProvider, alongside the Platform RBAC provisioner — Platform must not depend on AiCore; `AgentResolver::ensureForTenant($tenant)` runs in `TenantContext::system()` with explicit tenant_id, the RbacProvisioner pattern). **(2) THE CAPPED RESOLVER** (`Modules\AiCore\Services\AgentResolver::effectiveLevel(Agent,ToolDefinition,roleCeiling=AUTO)`): effective autonomy = **MIN(configured, tool ceiling [effectiveCeiling], role ceiling)**; a paused agent or non-whitelisted tool → OFF (not callable). Config can only NARROW — never raises past the tool/role ceiling. **Wired into `AgentRuntime`** (additive optional `?Agent $agentEntity` param — null = the existing per-tool path, 100% unchanged): with an entity, the runtime acts on the capped level; the role ceiling = OFF when the acting user lacks the tool's `permission` (Gate check). **(3) CLAMP-ON-WRITE + CLAMP-AT-RUNTIME (defense in depth):** `App\Services\AgentConfigService::configure` (app-layer, audited `agent.configured`) clamps on write (valid level enum, valid status, whitelist restricted to REAL registered tool keys — a forged/unknown key dropped); the resolver clamps at call time regardless (a forged stored `auto` or a whitelisted out-of-ceiling tool still resolves to the capped level). **THE CLAMP PROOF:** the tests prove a forged higher config / a whitelisted out-of-ceiling tool does NOT raise effective autonomy (returns the min/capped). **The agent sits UNDER AutonomyPolicy + the role ceiling + the fence — none weakened; the per-tool SETTINGS.P2 cap is unchanged and is one of the min() terms.** **NO UI this gate** (P2+ builds the surfaces; RBAC admin.manage is applied by the P2 config controller — no route yet). **NO existing behavior test modified; the electric-fence eval (37/398) + AutonomyPolicy AgentAutonomyTest + the code-class agent suites (unchanged null-agent runtime path) + ApprovalQueue + all suites stay green.** Added `tests/Feature/AiCore/AgentEntityTest.php` (7 tests / 29 assertions: creating a tenant seeds the 6 canonical agents tenant-scoped [whitelist = real tool keys]; the resolver = MIN(configured, tool ceiling, role ceiling) [clinical auto→suggest, operational auto→approve, role OFF→OFF]; a FORGED stored auto is clamped at call time [defense in depth]; a whitelisted out-of-ceiling tool is still capped + a non-whitelisted tool is off + a paused agent is off [narrows only]; AgentRuntime uses the capped resolver level when an agent entity is supplied [suggest→pending, not-whitelisted→off]; the per-tool AutonomyPolicy cap unchanged + the non-agent runtime path unchanged; configure clamps-on-write [forged tool key dropped, bogus level ignored] + audited). VERIFIED locally: `composer check` FULLY green — Pint `passed`, PHPStan L5 `[OK]`, **Pest 1056 passed**; `composer test:smoke` green. (No FE touched → no npm build.) composer check + CI verified after push. Remaining AGENT parts: P2+ the agent-shaped surfaces (master-detail, autonomy ladder, whitelist UI, ceiling panel, fence vault, metrics/ledger) over this capped foundation; rate/timing limits (backend gap); new-agent wizard. See [[AiCore]].

- `d0199e3` AGENT.P6: AiCore/governance — rate/timing limits (runtime-read) + always-on uncertainty escalation (no disable) + new-agent wizard (capped from birth) — **AGENT & TOOL CONFIG wireframe-parity COMPLETE**. FINAL fix part of the AGENT-TOOL-CONFIG chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/AGENT-TOOL-CONFIG-DIFF.md` §9 items 5/5b/6 (now ALL **RESOLVED**). **THE RULES: limits are REAL settings the runtime reads (or honestly deferred — never phantom); the uncertainty-escalation is ALWAYS-ON (no disable path, floor un-removable); a new agent is a P1 governed container capped from birth.** **Opening:** top commit `4239ca9` (AGENT.P5), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder. **(1) RATE & TIMING LIMITS (REAL — the runtime CONSULTS them):** additive migration `2026_08_26_000001` adds `max_drafts_per_hour` (nullable) + `quiet_hours_start`/`quiet_hours_end` (nullable, hour-of-day 0-23) to `agents`. `AgentRuntime::runTool` (on the Agent-entity path, after the OFF check, before AUTO/pending) now CONSULTS them: quiet hours (`Agent::isQuietHour(now-hour)`, handles overnight wrap) → records + returns `quiet_hours` (deferred to a human); max drafts/hour → counts the agent-KIND's AgentActions in the rolling hour (`whereIn agent`, `AgentRegistry::ledgerNames($agent->kind())`, `created_at>=now-1h`) and at the cap → records + returns `rate_limited`. A limit can only STOP the agent, never widen it. Stored per-agent via `AgentConfigService::configure` (new `applyLimit` — validated bounds [max 1..1000, hours 0..23], nullable clears, non-numeric ignored; audited). The floor is un-removable; the (deferred) threshold could only tune WHEN, never remove it. **(3) NEW-AGENT WIZARD (a P1 governed container, capped from birth):** added a `kind` column (the canonical capability an agent is an INSTANCE of — an `AgentRegistry::AGENTS` key — distinct from the per-agent unique `key`; migration backfills `kind=key` so every seeded agent is unchanged; `Agent::kind()` falls back to key). `POST /governance/agents` (`AgentConfigController::store`, admin.manage, tenant-scoped): validates `kind` ∈ real AGENTS keys (you CANNOT create a non-existent capability → 422) + `name`; creates the Agent whitelisted to the kind's REAL remit (registered, callable tools), autonomy default suggest, status active, a unique key slug; a requested `autonomy_level` is CLAMPED to the new agent's effective ceiling (`clampToCeiling(agentCeiling)`) — capped from birth; the resolver caps it at runtime too. - `b8994ef` BILLAR.P1: Billing — model write-offs + contractual adjustments as operator-gated reconciling ledger movements (reconcile-to-the-unit extended, not weakened). First fix part of the BILLING-AR chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/BILLING-AR-DIFF.md` §4 gap #6 (now **RESOLVED** — the money-integrity foundation; unblocks the P2 roll-forward). **The roll-forward couldn't tie out because write-offs/adjustments were unmodeled; this gate models them as REAL append-only ledger movements that reconcile-to-the-unit, operator-gated, audited. NO reporting/visual this gate — the ledger model + the reconcile proof only.** **Opening:** top commit `419a380` (the billing-ar diff), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder. **How money posts + reconciles today (confirmed):** money is integer minor units everywhere; the "ledger" is append-only `payments` + `payment_allocations` (signed; a reversal is the exact negative row) + `refunds`, projected into the mutable `invoice_balances.open_balance_minor`; the frozen `invoices` row is never touched; `PaymentService::openBalance` = `total − net allocations`; `allocate` has the over-allocation guard (FOR UPDATE, can't allocate > open); the credit note (`IssueService::creditNote`, series CN) was the de-facto write-off; the `ReconciliationEngine` checks **I1–I6** in exact integer arithmetic (I2 = `invoice_balances.open == total − net allocations`, bounds [0,total]; I6 = no orphan money), δ_minor === 0 on a clean month (`SimulatedMonthTest` asserts count 6 + every δ=0). **(1) WRITE-OFF + (2) CONTRACTUAL ADJUSTMENT** modeled as ONE append-only table `invoice_adjustments` (migration `2026_08_28_000001`) with `type` (write_off|contractual), signed `amount_minor` (POSITIVE reduces the balance; a correction is a REVERSAL row = exact negative), required `reason`, optional `reference` (insurer agreement ref), `created_by`, `adjusted_at`, `reverses_adjustment_id` (unique). Append-only: ORM guard (updating/deleting throw) + DB triggers (SIGNAL 45000 no_update/no_delete) + a non-zero CHECK — mirroring `payment_allocations`. Model `Modules\Billing\Models\InvoiceAdjustment` (BelongsToTenant, isReversal(), assertTenantReferences). **OPERATOR-GATED service** `Modules\Billing\Services\AdjustmentService::writeOff/contractualAdjustment/reverse` — all gate `billing.manage` (the money-commit gate; the AGENT has NO path — no AI tool calls it, so it can never write one, the fence), assert actor+invoice tenant, reason required, positive amount, `DB::transaction` locking the `invoice_balances` row (FOR UPDATE), an **over-adjustment guard** (`amountMinor > openBalance` → refused — can't reduce below zero, mirroring over-allocation), create the row, refresh the projection, audit (`billing.written_off`/`billing.adjusted`/`billing.adjustment_reversed`). A correction is a reversal row (re-reads the canonical persisted amount, never a caller-mutated instance; not-already-reversed + not-a-reversal guards). **(3) THE RECONCILE PROOF:** `PaymentService::openBalance` now = `total − net allocations − net adjustments` (added `netAllocatedMinor`/`netAdjustedMinor`); `refreshInvoiceBalance` (made public for the AdjustmentService) writes `open_balance_minor` = that, with the PAID/PARTIALLY_PAID status kept **payment-driven** (a write-off reduces what's owed without labelling it "paid"; identical to before for payment-only invoices). The **ReconciliationEngine extended, count unchanged at 6, tie-out NOT weakened**: **I2** derivation subtracts net adjustments (still 0≤open≤total); **I6** gains orphan-adjustment + adjustment-reversal-integrity checks (mirroring allocations). Because aging/outstanding read the reconciled `invoice_balances` projection, write-offs/adjustments flow into them automatically (no reporting change). **HONESTY/FENCE:** real integer-minor ledger movements; balance = charges − payments − credits − adjustments − write-offs; δ_minor === 0 WITH them; operator-gated (agent-never); append-only; NO page-side money math (no reporting this gate). **NOTHING weakened:** the reconcile-to-the-unit invariant, the allocation guard, the append-only ledger, the frozen invoices — all intact; the existing billing/reconcile/hammer suites pass untouched. **NO existing behavior test modified.** Added `tests/Feature/Billing/WriteOffAdjustmentTest.php` (7 tests / 85 assertions): a write-off posts an append-only movement (integer minor, reason, operator) + reduces the balance + reconciles (I1-I6, count 6, every δ=0) + audited; a contractual adjustment posts (with agreement ref) + reconciles; **THE RECONCILE PROOF — balance = charges − payments − adjustments − write-offs (20000−4000−3000−5000=8000) + reconciles δ=0**; operator-gated (a reception/non-billing.manage user refused on both; nothing posted); the over-adjustment guard (can't write off > open); append-only (the row can't update/delete → LogicException; a correction is a reversal row restoring the balance + reconciling; reversal-of-reversal + double-reversal refused); the base case unweakened (a payment-only invoice still δ=0). VERIFIED locally: full **billing suite green (93 tests / 765 assertions** incl. SimulatedMonth count-6/δ=0 + Reconciliation I1-I6 + the 7 new); `composer check` FULLY green — Pint `passed`, PHPStan L5 `[OK]`, **Pest 1100 passed**; `composer test:smoke` green. (No FE touched → no npm build.) composer check + CI verified after push. Remaining BILLING-AR parts: P2 the AR roll-forward reconciliation (opening→closing tie-out, now unblocked); P3+ DSO / net collection rate / by-payer / charged-vs-collected / the management-report grid / account-level overdue rollup + AR Account Detail. - `89304af` BILLAR.P2: Billing/Reporting — the AR roll-forward (engine-computed reconcile-to-the-unit bridge; opening→closing ties out δ=0 over P1 movements). Second fix part of the BILLING-AR chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/BILLING-AR-DIFF.md` §4 item #1 (now **RESOLVED**; unblocked by P1's write-off/adjustment ledger). **The roll-forward is a MetricsService (engine) method that TIES OUT to the unit; the page (P6) will DISPLAY the six figures, never compute them. NO visual grid this gate.** **Opening:** top commit `b8994ef` (BILLAR.P1), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder. **Studied (confirmed):** `MetricsService` financial aggregates run over the reconciled `invoice_balances` projection (integer minor units, billing.view, tenant-scoped, definitions reused from the ReconciliationEngine so reporting agrees with billing); `invoicedTotalMinor` = Σ issued INV totals in range; `paymentsReceivedTotalMinor` = Σ gross payments (cash) — NOT what reduces AR; `outstandingBalanceMinor` = Σ open_balance_minor (point-in-time projection); P1 `invoice_adjustments` (signed minor, type write_off|contractual). Critical model detail: a full credit note fully cancels an unpaid ISSUED invoice, but the cancellation status (CANCELLED_BY_CREDIT_NOTE, open 0) lives on the `invoice_balances` PROJECTION — the frozen `invoices.status` stays ISSUED; partial credit notes do NOT reduce the invoice balance. **(1) The method** `MetricsService::arRollForward($actor, $from, $to): array` returns six integer-minor figures + the tie fields: **OPENING** = `outstandingAsOfMinor(from−1)` — AR derived from the append-only ledger AS-OF a date (Σ over issued INV of `total − allocations(allocated_at≤D) − adjustments(adjusted_at≤D)`, an invoice fully cancelled by a credit note issued ≤D counting 0); **CHARGES** = new issued INV totals in [from,to] **NET of credit-note cancellations in the period** (the fold that makes the bridge exhaustive — a cancelling CN removes the invoice's full total from AR; identified via the `invoice_balances` status, not the frozen invoice status, so partial CNs are correctly excluded); **COLLECTIONS** = net `PaymentAllocation`s (allocated_at in period — the movement that reduces an invoice's open balance, not gross cash); **ADJUSTMENTS** = P1 InvoiceAdjustment type=contractual in period; **WRITE-OFFS** = P1 InvoiceAdjustment type=write_off in period; **CLOSING** = `outstandingBalanceMinor()` (the reconciled projection, outstanding as of now/`to`), computed INDEPENDENTLY of the bridge (the current-period management view, "as of today"). **(2) THE TIE-OUT:** `bridge_closing_minor` = opening + charges − collections − adjustments − write_offs; `discrepancy_minor` = bridge − closing; `ties` = (discrepancy===0). The bridge (sum of period movements) MUST equal the independent reconciled projection, EXACTLY — δ=0 (telescoping: opening[as-of from−1] + period movements = as-of-`to` = the projection by I2; the credit-note fold covers the CN movement). **A non-tie is SURFACED, never papered over** (`ties`/`discrepancy_minor` exposed) — a non-tie means an unmodeled movement changed AR or the projection drifted (a reconcile failure to expose). Every AR movement in the period is one of the five modeled terms (charges [incl. credit notes], payments, P1 adjustments, P1 write-offs). **FENCE:** engine-computed + tying; the page (P6) will DISPLAY the six figures; NO page-side arithmetic (no JS bridge, no client sum); NO visual grid this gate. **NOTHING weakened:** the ReconciliationEngine + P1 + the allocation guard + the append-only ledger + I2 all intact; the roll-forward READS the reconciled projection + the append-only movements, writes nothing. billing.view + tenant-scoped (fail-closed via BelongsToTenant). **NO existing behavior test modified.** Added `tests/Feature/Billing/ArRollForwardTest.php` (7 tests / 43 assertions): the roll-forward returns the six integer-minor figures + ties (a fresh charge: opening 0/charges 20000/closing 20000/δ=0); **THE TIE-OUT — a period with a payment + a P1 contractual adjustment + a P1 write-off still ties** (0+20000−4000−3000−5000=8000, closing=8000, δ=0, and closing === the independent `outstandingBalanceMinor` projection); a payments-only period ties (base case); **OPENING AR carries a prior-period invoice** (issued before `from`) and the period ties (opening 15000, collections 5000, closing 10000); **a credit note in the period folds into charges (net) and still ties** (gross 10000 − credit 10000 = 0 charges, closing 0); **an unmodeled movement is SURFACED as a non-tie** (a raw `invoice_balances` projection mutation → bridge 20000 vs closing 15000 → δ=5000, ties=false — never hidden); billing.view-gated (reception refused) + tenant-scoped (a second tenant sees all zeros). VERIFIED locally: reporting + billing suites green (110 tests / 899 assertions); `composer check` FULLY green — Pint `passed`, PHPStan L5 `[OK]`, **Pest 1107 passed**; `composer test:smoke` green. (No FE this gate → no npm build.) composer check + CI verified after push. Remaining BILLING-AR parts: P3+ DSO / net collection rate / by-payer / charged-vs-collected series / the management-report grid (visual, P6 displays the roll-forward) / account-level overdue rollup + AR Account Detail. See [[Billing]] / [[Reporting]].
- `a39d13b` BILLAR.P3: Billing/Reporting — DSO + net collection rate + honest collectible (engine methods; real figures, honest definitions, no page math). Third fix part of the BILLING-AR chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/BILLING-AR-DIFF.md` §4 items #2/#3 (now **RESOLVED**). **Two engine metrics computed IN THE ENGINE (MetricsService) from real figures; the page (P6) DISPLAYS them, never page-computes. NO visual grid this gate.** **Opening:** top commit `89304af` (BILLAR.P2), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder. **Studied (confirmed the real inputs):** `outstandingBalanceMinor` = current reconciled AR; `invoicedTotalMinor`/P2 `chargesBilledMinor` = charges billed (P2's net-of-credit-notes definition); P2 `netCollectionsMinor` = net allocations in period (what reduces AR — NOT gross cash `paymentsReceivedTotalMinor`); P1 `InvoiceAdjustment` type=contractual (insurer write-downs) / write_off. **Refactor (DRY, no behavior change):** extracted the P2 arRollForward inline term computations into shared private helpers `chargesBilledMinor`/`netCollectionsMinor`/`contractualAdjustmentsMinor`/`writeOffsMinor` — arRollForward now calls them (values UNCHANGED; the P2 test still passes 7/7), so every "charges billed"/"collections" figure agrees across the roll-forward, DSO + the rate. **(1) DSO** `MetricsService::daysSalesOutstanding($actor,$from,$to): array` — stated standard definition **DSO = (AR outstanding ÷ credit sales in period) × days-in-period**: AR = `outstandingBalanceMinor` (reconciled projection as of now); credit sales = `chargesBilledMinor` (P2 net charges); days = inclusive days in [from,to]. Returns the real components (`ar_minor`, `credit_sales_minor`, `days`) + `dso` (float rounded to **1 decimal** — money stays exact integer minor units; only the ratio rounds, documented). **Zero-sales period → `dso: null` ("—", NO divide-by-zero, not 0/∞).** **(2) NET COLLECTION RATE** `MetricsService::netCollectionRate($actor,$from,$to): array` — **rate = collections ÷ collectible**; **collectible = charges billed − contractual adjustments** (honestly DERIVED from real figures: the P1 contractual adjustments are insurer-agreed reductions that were never collectible; **write-offs are NOT subtracted** — uncollected-but-was-collectible, the standard "net" definition, stated). collections = `netCollectionsMinor` (P2 net allocations). Returns the real components (`collections_minor`, `charges_minor`, `contractual_adjustments_minor`, `collectible_minor`) + `rate` (fraction rounded to **4 decimals**; the page formats as %). **Zero-collectible period → `rate: null` ("—", NO divide-by-zero).** A rate > 1.0 (collecting prior-period balances) is reported honestly, never capped. **FENCE/HONESTY:** engine-computed from real figures (the page P6 displays, computes nothing); honest STATED definitions; collectible DERIVED (charges − contractual adjustments), NEVER fabricated; zero/edge cases → honest null ("—"), never a fabricated or divide-by-zero value; NO page-side arithmetic; NO visual this gate. **NOTHING weakened:** P1/P2/the ReconciliationEngine/the reconcile invariant intact; the methods READ real figures + write nothing. billing.view + tenant-scoped (fail-closed via BelongsToTenant). **NO existing behavior test modified.** Added `tests/Feature/Billing/DsoCollectionRateTest.php` (6 tests / 22 assertions, clock frozen so the period includes now): DSO = (AR/creditSales)×days from real figures (AR 30000 / sales 40000 × 30 days = **22.5**, exact components); a zero-sales period → **dso null** (components shown honestly, no divide-by-zero); net collection rate = collections/collectible (20000 / (30000−5000) = **0.8**); **collectible is DERIVED — a contractual adjustment reduces it by exactly its amount (30000→22000), a write-off does NOT (stays 22000)** — proving the honest definition + no fabrication; a zero-collectible period → **rate null**; billing.view-gated (reception refused on both) + tenant-scoped (a second tenant sees 0/null). - `4ad078a` BILLAR.P4: Billing/Reporting — by-payer AR aggregate (honest grouping over a real payer dimension; groups tie to total δ=0). Fourth fix part of the BILLING-AR chain, per `docs/wireframe-parity/BILLING-AR-DIFF.md` §4 item #4 (now **RESOLVED** for the real dimension; the finer taxonomy stays a flagged gap). **Group by a REAL payer attribute; the groups TIE to the overall totals (δ=0, a real partition); engine-computed, page P6 displays, no page-side math. NO visual grid this gate.** **Opening:** top commit `a39d13b` (BILLAR.P3), CI green; DemoClinicSeeder. **THE PAYER DIMENSION (the key question — answered):** payer IS a real modeled attribute — `Invoice::payer_type` (string, REQUIRED, validated enum `assertPayerType`: `self_pay` | `private_insurance`) + nullable `payer_name` (insurer name); credit notes copy the source invoice's payer_type/payer_name. So the REAL dimension = `payer_type` with 2 categories. The wireframe's 4-way Swiss split (patient self-pay / supplementary insurance / accident SUVA-UVG / social municipal) is FINER than the model — NOT modeled — so I group by the real 2 + FLAG the finer taxonomy as a gap; I do NOT fabricate the 4 categories. **(1) The method** `MetricsService::arByPayer($actor,$from,$to): array` — groups AR + collections + charges by `payer_type` (billing.view, tenant-scoped, integer minor): the exhaustive partition key = the DISTINCT payer_types present on issued invoices (tenant-scoped); per group: AR = Σ `invoice_balances.open_balance_minor` over issued INV with that payer_type (the reconciled projection, consistent with `outstandingBalanceMinor`); collections = Σ net `PaymentAllocation`s in period whose invoice has that payer_type; charges = gross issued INV in period for that payer_type − credited (CN-cancelled) INV for that payer_type (the P2 net-of-credit-notes definition). Returns `groups[]` + `total_ar/collections/charges_minor` + `ties`. Scope-safe: subquery-filtered sums (Invoice/InvoiceBalance/PaymentAllocation global scopes), NO cross-tenant join. **(2) THE TIE (the crux):** the split is a REAL PARTITION — every issued invoice has exactly one payer_type, so Σ over groups of AR/collections/charges === the totals from the shared helpers (`outstandingBalanceMinor`/`netCollectionsMinor`/`chargesBilledMinor`), δ=0; `ties` confirms all three.
