- `0b8c481` DENTAL-B.P2 (second of the DENTAL-B chain): Dental — **Odontogram visual parity over the EXISTING tooth-chart backend**, P0D.GU (no new clinical computation, no fabricated per-tooth field). **Opening:** top commit `506762a` (DENTAL-B.P1), tree clean, CI green, Redis up. **The demo data had to be re-seeded TWICE** — the Pest suite shares the dev database (`DB_DATABASE=careos`) and `RefreshDatabase` wipes it, so the gate's "verify by querying, not by trusting the seeder exit code" instruction caught what a green exit code would have hidden; browser verification must happen AFTER the last test run. **WHAT THE BACKEND REALLY HAS (the rail can only show this):** `tooth_records` = `tooth` (FDI) · `surface` (null = whole tooth) · `charted_condition` · `note` · `reason` (a correction's justification) · `charted_by` · `charted_at`, append-only at model AND DB-trigger level, current = latest per (tooth, surface), history = every row; plus `performed_procedures` per tooth. **NO** per-tooth findings count, severity, imaging link, probing depth or eruption state exists. **BUILT:** **Read/Chart mode toggle** — a UI MODE, NOT A PERMISSION (read mode hides charting affordances so a reviewing dentist cannot record by accident; the server still authorises every write on `dental.chart` exactly as before, the client sends no mode the server reads, and the toggle is only offered to a user who can already chart); **per-tooth detail rail** showing the selected tooth's REAL rows — current conditions per surface, then the append-only trail newest-first with WHO charted it (the recorded `charted_by` resolved to a name for display only, one query), when, the clinician's note, and for a correction THEIR OWN stated reason — with an honest empty state ("Nothing charted yet." / "No charting history for this tooth."), no placeholder row invented; **US-notation cross-reference** via new `ToothNotation::universal()`/`universalMap()`, a deterministic TOTAL lookup with the ADA definition documented in-code (permanent 1–32 from the upper-right third molar; primary A–T on the same walk), verified a BIJECTION over all 52 teeth, computed in the DOMAIN so the component maps nothing, surfaced in the rail header and each tooth's `title`/`aria-label`; **chart-key polish** labelling the server's two vocabularies in a calmer grid — still UNORDERED within each group, still carrying the §6 note verbatim, with an in-place comment that grouping by SCOPE (where a condition may be charted) is NOT a ranking; **S1 adopted** — the inline header tile became `PatientClinicalHeader`, DOB formatted and age computed BY THE PAGE through the shared `formatDateOnly`/`ageFromDateOnly` helpers (D-091 local midnight) and passed down as STRINGS, so the component still parses and computes nothing. **NO STAT TILE WAS ADDED, deliberately:** the gate allowed S4 "if any factual count is shown", but the only counts available here are counts over clinical findings — precisely the "1 finding" chip §5.1 rules MUST-NOT-BUILD; an empty tile beats a fence breach. **THE FENCE RE-ASSERTED, not reopened:** no DMFT/dmft, finding count, "Flagged / site to watch", severity ramp, trend or AI finding — absent from the payload AND the components; browser-verified that the ONLY occurrence of "flagged" on the rendered page is inside the §6 note itself ("Nothing here is scored, graded, or flagged"). **NO mixed dentition** (B1 stays a backend gap); **no allergy chip** — S1 supports one but sourcing Clinical allergies into the dental payload is a new cross-module read, out of scope, recorded for a later gate. **Tests added:** `tests/Feature/Dental/OdontogramParityTest.php` (6 tests, 356 assertions) — the rail equals the recorded rows (ids, condition, note, reason, charter name) with the correction trail intact; the empty state invents nothing; the notation map is deterministic, total and bijective against 16 known anchors; read mode is not a permission (a forged `mode` parameter changes nothing in EITHER direction — a permitted write still succeeds, reception is still refused 403); and the recursive absence re-assertion over payload + components. **A MUTATION EXPOSED A REAL HOLE IN THE P1 SCAN:** a camelCase `siteToWatch` computed injected into `ToothArch.vue` passed BOTH fence suites, because `\bwatch\b` does not match inside `sitetowatch` and the phrase `site to watch` never matches an identifier. Both suites now ALSO scan a non-alphanumeric-stripped copy of the source for compound §5.1 phrases (`sitetowatch`, `cariesindex`, `findingcount`, `severityramp`, `trendarrow`, …); bare `watch` stays permitted because it is Vue's own reactive primitive and banning a framework API teaches the next author to weaken the scan. Re-running the mutation now turns both suites red. **BROWSER-VERIFIED:** header renders "Anna Vogel · MRN-000001 · 05/22/1979 · 47 yrs · female"; mode buttons Read/Chart with `aria-pressed`; tooth 16 `title="FDI 16 · US 3"`; the rail's two history entries carry the real notes, the correction reason and "charted by Dr. med. dent. Sabine Morgenstern"; read mode drops form count to 0 while the data stays readable; tooth 18 shows the honest empty state and `FDI 18 · US 1`. **VERIFY:** `npm run build` green · Pint passed · PHPStan L5 "No errors" · vitest 29 passed · `composer test:smoke` 4 passed · Dental+Evals+Architecture 121 passed · full `composer check` green. - `3f500d5` DENTAL-B.P3 (third of the DENTAL-B chain): Dental — **Perio grid ergonomics + raw values over time**, layout/entry ergonomics over the EXISTING raw-measurement backend (P0D.GU; nothing computed, nothing fabricated). **Opening:** top commit `0b8c481` (DENTAL-B.P2), tree clean, Redis up; CI for P2 was still `in_progress` at open so implementation proceeded on the locally-green base and the commit was HELD until CI reported `success` — it did. Perio data re-seeded and **verified by query** (2 exams, 24 measurements). **THE REAL RAW MODEL:** `PerioExam` = patient · examined_by · exam_date · note (append-only, model + DB trigger; a re-exam is a NEW exam); `PerioMeasurement` = one row per (tooth, site) with **pocket_depth_mm (0–15) · recession_mm (−15…30) · bleeding_on_probing (bool) · mobility (Miller 0–3, per tooth) · furcation (Glickman 0–4)** over the six sites `mesio_buccal, buccal, disto_buccal, mesio_lingual, lingual, disto_lingual`; the range checks are data-entry plausibility guards, explicitly NOT grades; `siteHistory()` already returns raw numbers "with NO band, flag, or worsening label". **The existing fence assertion** is `pcAssertNoJudgment` in `PerioChartTest.php` (DENTAL.G6/D-104) — **NOT modified**, extended alongside. **BUILT:** `Components/Dental/PerioSiteGrid.vue` — the full-arch 6-point grid (one column per tooth, one row per site, split into the buccal three and the lingual three as ANATOMY not a ranking; arrow keys move left/right between teeth and up/down between sites with Enter as down, so an arch is probed without the mouse; upper/lower arch toggle; per-tooth mobility/furcation row); **prior readings as RAW NUMBERS** — the controller adds `previous` = `{exam_date, pocket_depth_mm}` keyed tooth → site from the most recent exam and the grid prints "prev 6" under the cell, with **no delta, no arrow, no direction word** (the demo data contains a genuinely deepened site, 16 disto-buccal 4 → 6 mm — exactly the mock's "one site to watch · ▲ from 4" — and it renders as two plain numbers); **S1 adopted** for the header. **NO STAT TILE, again** (the P2/D-168 reasoning): every perio figure the mock tiles is an index; the only count on the page is "3 sites entered", the clinician's own data-entry progress, which says nothing about the patient. **THE FENCE — the entire computed rail is ABSENT:** no BOP %, no "sites ≥ 4 mm" count, no mean/average depth, no plaque score, no trend arrow/delta/direction, no "site to watch", no severity colour band, no bitewing finding; browser-verified across the rendered page, where the only "flagged" is the existing fence note itself. **A SEVERITY RAMP NEEDS NO JUDGMENT WORD — the assertion had to be rebuilt around that.** A mutation `function cellTint(mm) { return mm >= 6 ? 'bg-danger' : mm >= 4 ? 'bg-warning' : '' }` **passed every lexical check** (no banned token; a threshold regex keyed to `pocket_depth_mm` misses a parameter named `mm`). The rule now lives where the breach must surface — **in the styling**: no `:class`/`:style` binding may reference a measurement (`depth`, `_mm`, `tint`, `band`, `colour`, `shade`, `heat`) or compare against a number, and inside the grid the tone classes (`bg-/text-/border-/ring-/fill-` × `danger/warning/success/critical/alarm`) are banned outright, since every cell is drawn identically so a tone there could only be a ramp; the page keeps its tones (its flash is legitimately a success tone), hence the grid-scoped ban. **Browser-proof of the same property: all 96 depth cells report exactly ONE distinct computed style.** **Tests added:** `tests/Feature/Dental/PerioParityTest.php` (4 tests, 398 assertions) — recording writes the same raw rows through the same path; the previous exam is offered as raw numbers with its payload keys asserted to be exactly `[exam_date, pocket_depth_mm]`; plus payload and component re-assertions. **Mutation-checked FOUR ways** — a `bop_percent`/`mean` payload key, a `trendArrow`, a tone-class ramp, and a neutral-palette ramp wired through a `:class` binding each turn the suite red. **TWO OF MY OWN BUGS, both caught by verifying rather than assuming:** the helper `ppCtx()` collided with `PerformProcedureTest`'s and was invisible until the whole-directory run (renamed `pgd*`); and `v-for="site in group.value"` rendered ZERO inputs because template refs are auto-unwrapped — **the suite was green while the grid was empty on screen, and only the browser check caught it**. **BROWSER-VERIFIED:** 96 depth cells + 96 BoP boxes render; arrow keys step 18→17 across teeth and mesio-buccal→buccal down sites and back; 12 prior values print as "prev 3"/"prev 6"; entering three readings on tooth 17 and recording writes exactly those three rows (pd 3/5/4, BoP on the buccal) — the 93 untouched cells are NOT recorded as zeros. **VERIFY:** `npm run build` green · Pint passed · PHPStan L5 "No errors" · vitest 29 passed · `composer test:smoke` 4 passed · Dental+Evals+Architecture 125 passed · full `composer check` green. - `bc24b5f` DENTAL-B.P4 (fourth of the DENTAL-B chain): Dental/Billing — **Treatment Plan visual parity** over the EXISTING plan + billing backend, with TWO fences (money, agent). **Opening:** top commit `3f500d5` (DENTAL-B.P3), tree clean, Redis up, CI `completed / success` confirmed BEFORE starting. Plan/billing data re-seeded and **verified by query** (2 plans, 7 charges, tenant currency CHF). **THE REAL MONEY SOURCE:** `TreatmentPlanService::itemEstimate()` returns the SNAPSHOT (`estimated_fee_minor`, frozen at propose) or the live `TariffItem.unit_price_minor` while draft — it READS the fee, never recomputes it; phase/plan totals are sums of those engine integers **server-side** (the service docblock calls this "the only arithmetic here"; no VAT/discount math, since VAT is applied by the engine when a procedure is actually charged at G4). **"N of M billed" NOW HAS A REAL ENGINE SOURCE, added this gate:** performing a planned item captures a charge through `ChargeCaptureService` and stores `charge_id` on the `PerformedProcedure`, so billed = sum of those charges' `line_total_minor` excluding cancelled — **actual money in the ledger, never the estimate re-labelled**; an un-performed item carries NO billed figure rather than a fabricated zero. **THE PAGE NOW DOES NO MONEY ARITHMETIC AT ALL:** the controller emits formatted strings (`total`, `billed`, per-phase `total`, per-item `estimate`/`billed`) alongside the `*_minor` integers and `TreatmentPlans.vue`'s own `money()` helper was DELETED — not even a divide-by-100 remains — satisfying S5's contract that a card receives an already-formatted ENGINE-SUPPLIED string. **Deliberate trade-off:** the PHP formatter duplicates `resources/js/lib/money.ts` (`formatSwissMoney`, ARDETAIL.P3), same Swiss apostrophe grouping, verified identical (`CHF 1'000.00`); two implementations in two languages can drift, so the tests pin the exact strings; other Billing pages keep the client helper (out of scope). **PAYMENT PLAN — OMITTED + FLAGGED:** the mock's "4 × CHF 310" does NOT map onto the existing `PaymentPlan` (ARDETAIL.P5), whose own invariant covers "an account's REAL outstanding balance" and forbids a total exceeding actual outstanding — a treatment plan is an ESTIMATE, nothing is outstanding until performed and invoiced, so a payment plan against it would require inventing outstanding money; **no parallel dental money model was built**. **AGENT — OMITTED + FLAGGED, absent BY CONSTRUCTION:** the repo has TEN agent tools (`app/AiCore/Tools/`) and **none** touches dental or treatment plans, and `Modules/Dental` has NO ApprovalQueue coupling whatsoever; no tool was invented, and a test pins the absence at both ends (no tool file may mention dental/treatment_plan; the page may offer no agent affordance) so a later gate cannot slip in one that auto-applies. **THE CLINICAL FENCE:** no recommended pathway, prognosis, auto-selected procedure code or urgency/priority score — absent from payload AND components, including the **D-169 styling rule** (no `:class`/`:style` keyed to a clinical or money value or a numeric comparison); `done` stays permitted as a LIFECYCLE fact the caller passes, not a clinical grade. **S1 + S5 adopted** (S3 already present); the item table became `ProcedureCard`s. **Tests added:** `tests/Feature/Dental/TreatmentPlanParityTest.php` (5 tests, 230 assertions) — figures are engine-supplied and TIE δ=0; billed is the real charge; the adversarial grep proves no page-side money arithmetic (`/ 100`, `.toFixed`, `.reduce(`, `_minor` arithmetic, sums, or a billed/total ratio); no agent tool touches plans; plus the re-assertion incl. the styling rule. **Mutation-checked FOUR ways** — a page-side `reduce(...)/100`, `billed_minor` fed from the estimate, a `pathway`/`urgency` payload key, and a `DraftTreatmentPlanTool` file each turn the suite red. **BROWSER-VERIFIED δ=0 ON SCREEN:** Beat Suter's plan renders items CHF 150.00 + CHF 100.00 = phase CHF 250.00 = plan total CHF 250.00, with "CHF 0.00 of CHF 250.00 billed"; no %, no ratio, no pathway/prognosis/urgency, and the only "auto-suggested" text is the existing fence note ("You author this plan — nothing is auto-suggested"). **Own slips caught by verifying:** the test asserted `CHF 1,000.00` (comma) when the format is the Swiss apostrophe, and set no tenant `currency` so the page rendered EUR — the DISPLAY currency is a tenant setting, independent of the tariff item's own currency; Pint then caught a fully-qualified inline class reference alongside its import plus an unused `TreatmentPlan` import. **VERIFY:** `npm run build` green · Pint passed · PHPStan L5 "No errors" · vitest 29 passed · `composer test:smoke` 4 passed · Dental+Billing+Evals+Architecture 296 passed · full `composer check` green. Docs: `DENTAL-BATCH-DIFF.md` §7 P4 marked DONE.
- `ff6ad80` DENTAL-B.P5 (fifth of the DENTAL-B chain): Dental — **Fee Schedule visual parity** over the EXISTING tenant-authored catalog; the line here is **LICENSING, not clinical**. **Opening:** top commit `bc24b5f` (DENTAL-B.P4), tree clean, Redis up, CI `completed / success` confirmed BEFORE starting; catalog re-seeded and **verified by query** (7 positions, CHF). **WHAT THE CATALOG REALLY HOLDS:** a dental procedure IS a `TariffItem` in the tenant's own `dental` `TariffCatalog` + a thin `DentalProcedure` overlay carrying `tooth_scoped`; the row is `code` · `description` · `unit_price_minor` · `vat_rate_bp` · `active`. `DentalCatalogService` AUTHORS it (data entry) and **computes no prices**; charging resolves + snapshots the fee through `ChargeCaptureService`. **Who may edit: `billing.manage` ONLY** — not `patient.view`, not `dental.chart`, which is exactly why the fee schedule is correctly absent from the patient-scoped `DentalSectionNav` (D-167). There is **no category field, no status beyond `active`, no versioning and no point-value concept** anywhere. **BUILT (visual half):** search over the tenant's own codes/names (plain text match — no ranking, no score); grouping by a **REAL attribute** (`tooth_scoped` → "Charged per tooth" / "Charged once") because the wireframe's own category taxonomy has NO backend field and was NOT invented; active/retired status pills; three **S4 tiles** carrying plain factual counts of the tenant's own rows (positions · active · per-tooth), counted SERVER-side so the page computes nothing; and an "About this schedule" note stating on screen what the schedule deliberately does not carry. **MONEY:** the controller now emits `fee` (display string), `fee_input` (edit-form value), `vat` and `vat_input`; the page's `money()` helper and its `(vat_rate_bp / 100).toFixed(2)` are GONE and `startEdit` no longer divides — **the rendered template contains no arithmetic at all**. The only arithmetic left on the surface is `toMinor`/`toBp` converting what the DENTIST TYPED on its way to the UNCHANGED endpoint, asserted to appear nowhere but the two form transforms. No average fee, no total catalog value, no price banding. **THE LICENSING LINE (the crux):** no licensed code set is bundled, seeded or hardcoded, and a **repo-wide structural test** now enforces it across `Modules`, `app`, `database`, `config`, `resources/js` — no CDT-shaped code (`D` + exactly four digits, e.g. D0120) and no licensed tariff term (`taxpunkt`, `tarmed`, `uv-go`, `dentaltarif`, `ada cdt`, `cdt code`, `sso tarif`). The shipped `STARTER` template is asserted GENERIC — every code matches `^D-[A-Z]+$` (D-EXAM, D-PROPHY) — so **a real CDT code is distinguishable from ours by SHAPE alone**. The scan reads **comment-stripped** source, because `DentalCatalogService`'s own docblock says "NO licensed code set (ADA CDT / Swiss SSO point values) is bundled" and the sentence DECLARING the policy must not be what fails the test ENFORCING it (the P1/P3 lesson, third time). **FLAGGED NOT BUILT:** the mock's tax-point pricing (licensed data — never bundle) and **B2 effective-dated versioning + version diff** (a real backend gap); both are now stated on the page rather than silently missing. **D-169 carried forward:** nothing is styled by its price. **Tests added:** `tests/Feature/Dental/FeeScheduleParityTest.php` (5 tests, 9332 assertions) — the view renders the tenant's REAL authored rows with server-formatted fees; the repo-wide licensed-code-set scan; no page-side money arithmetic + no sourceless aggregate; the authoring path and its `billing.manage` gate unchanged (a doctor refused at read AND write); and the D-169 styling rule. **Mutation-checked FOUR ways** — `D-EXAM`→`D0120`, a `TAXPUNKTWERT_MINOR` constant, a page-side average fee, and a bare price-keyed tint (`fee_minor > 50000 ? bg-warning`) each turn the suite red. **BROWSER-VERIFIED:** the seven tenant-authored positions render grouped 4 per-tooth / 3 once with server-formatted CHF fees; tiles read 7 · 7 · 4; search narrows to "Showing 1 of 7"; the edit form prefills "900.00" from the server; columns are Code/Name/Fee/VAT/Status with **no point-value column**; all seven fee cells share exactly ONE computed style (no price ramp); and a **doctor (dental.chart, no billing.manage) gets 403** on the page. **Own slips caught before green:** two Pint failures (a fully-qualified class alongside its import, then quote/spacing style) and two PHPStan warnings (`?->` unnecessary on the left of `??`). **VERIFY:** `npm run build` green · Pint passed · PHPStan L5 "No errors" · vitest 29 passed · `composer test:smoke` 4 passed · Dental+Evals+Architecture 135 passed · full `composer check` green. - `7d7f354` DENTAL-B.P6 (final CORE gate of the DENTAL-B chain — **the core chain P1–P6 is now COMPLETE**): Dental — **Scan Library + Upload visual parity over the 2D backend**, P0D.GU. **Opening:** top commit `ff6ad80` (DENTAL-B.P5), tree clean, Redis up, CI `completed / success` confirmed BEFORE starting; imaging data re-seeded and **verified by query** (1 bitewing on tooth 16, 204800 bytes, 1 dentist reading). **WHAT THE 2D BACKEND REALLY HOLDS:** a `DentalImage` is METADATA over a file in the existing clinical document storage — `image_type` (one of five plain labels: bitewing/periapical/panoramic/photo/scan), an optional FDI `tooth`, an optional free-text `region`, `captured_at`, `uploaded_by`; the file carries `original_filename`, `mime_type`, `size_bytes`; interpretation lives in append-only `DentalImageReading` rows carrying **the dentist's own written text**. Both models are IMMUTABLE (model + DB trigger) and **nothing anywhere looks at the pixels**. **WHAT THE MOCK SHOWS WITH NO SOURCE:** 3D/mesh scans, planned setups, tray/ortho series; per-tooth coverage flagging; scan superimposition + measured deviation; AI radiograph findings — the first three are **B5 partner-gated**, the last is CADe/CADx, a **regulated device** and permanent non-goal. **BUILT (2D only):** the library became a filtered tile list over the REAL rows — filters by **modality and tooth (real recorded attributes only)**, newest/oldest ordering on the recorded capture time, a "Showing n of m" count, an honest no-match message, and per tile the real capture date, **who captured it** (the recorded `uploaded_by` resolved to a name for display only), the original filename and its size in kB; the tooth filter offers **only teeth that actually appear on this patient's images**, not the whole FDI universe; readings now show **who wrote them**; the viewer gained **drag-to-pan** alongside zoom, with an on-screen note that zoom/drag change what you see and record nothing; **S1 header adopted**. **THE UPLOAD PATH IS UNTOUCHED** — same endpoint, same validation (jpg/jpeg/png ≤10 MB, a known type, an optional valid FDI tooth); a test posts a forged `ai_finding` field and proves it reaches NOTHING, because "the page has no AI affordance" is not the same claim as "no AI value can enter through the page". **THE FENCE — the sharpest in the batch — ABSENT and RE-ASSERTED:** no AI/auto finding, detection, confidence, analysis, coverage/quality flag, computed measurement verdict or comparison overlay. **The new formulation: on an image surface the breach is DRAWING, not vocabulary** — a finding needs no word, it needs a box or a highlight over the pixels — so the viewer may not draw: `<canvas>`, `getContext`, `fillRect`, `strokeRect`, `drawImage`, `<svg>`, `marker`, `boundingBox`, `heatmap` are all forbidden in `Imaging.vue`. Zoom/pan stay fine (optics). Any annotation must be the CLINICIAN'S OWN authored record — today the free-text reading. **FLAGGED NOT BUILT** (the P5 precedent, stated on the page): no image analysis; no 3D scans/superimposition/ortho overlays (certified scanner partner needed); no live capture from an X-ray sensor or intraoral scanner. **Tests added:** `tests/Feature/Dental/ImagingParityTest.php` (5 tests, 223 assertions), EXTENDING the existing `DentalImagingTest` (NOT modified). **Mutation-checked THREE ways** — an AI `findings` payload key, a camelCase `coverageScore`, and an `<svg>` overlay drawn on the image each turn the suite red; the AI-findings mutation **also turned the EXISTING no-AI test red**, confirming it still bites. **BROWSER-VERIFIED:** the tile reads "Bitewing · 16 · 8/20/2026 · captured by Dr. med. dent. Sabine Morgenstern · bitewing-16.jpg · 200 kB"; the type filter narrows to "Showing 0 of 1" with the honest no-match message; the reading renders with "read by"; the viewer holds the image with **ZERO svg/canvas overlays**; a patient with no images shows "No images uploaded yet." with the upload form still available; and the ONLY mentions of finding/confidence/comparison anywhere on the page are the two disclaimers stating their absence. **DENTAL-B CORE COMPLETE (P1–P6):** nine of thirteen dental screens addressed; the four no-live-page screens (Scan Comparison, Ortho Progress, Chair Scheduling, Inventory & Sterilization) stay DEFERRED as net-new subsystems; two optional gates remain open (B3 structured procedure records; B4 the `Resource` capability field, which also closes APPT.P4). **VERIFY:** `npm run build` green · Pint passed · PHPStan L5 "No errors" · vitest 29 passed · `composer test:smoke` 4 passed · Dental+Evals+Architecture 140 passed · full `composer check` green. - `c5f4772` PC.P1 (first of the PC chain): Patients/app-layer — **shared clinical components + B1, the recorded-allergy wiring**; presentational (P0D.GU) plus ONE narrow read-only payload addition. **Opening:** top commit `e2cb9ba` (the PC audit), tree clean, Redis up, CI `completed / success` confirmed BEFORE starting; data verified BY QUERY (Praxis Lindenhof: 15 patients · 3 allergies — severe/moderate/mild across three patients, 12 with none). **S1 PROMOTED:** `PatientClinicalHeader.vue` moved `Components/Dental/` → `Components/Clinical/` (it is a clinical component dental merely needed first); all four dental callers updated; **behaviour-identical, proven the DENTAL-B.P1 way** — the rendered dental header captured from a real browser before and after is **byte-for-byte identical (381 normalised chars)**; existing dental tests untouched. **B1 — WHERE THE READ HAD TO LIVE:** `Modules\Patients` **may not use** `Modules\Clinical` (an arch test enforces it), so `PatientShowController` **moved to the app layer** (`app/Http/Controllers/`) — exactly why `AppointmentDetailController` already lives there, composing Scheduling + Patients + Clinical + Audit (D-017). The move changed the NAMESPACE and nothing else: same route, same `patient.view` gate, same payload, **the same single `auditRead()` row** (asserted by counting occurrences, so the allergy read cannot grow a second audit path). **THE PAGE WAS ALREADY WAITING:** `Patients/Show.vue` has carried a dormant top-level `allergies` prop and a hidden banner since it was built — *"not part of Patients/Show's payload today; rendered when present, absent silently until the prop lands"* — the same gap the wireframe names; **landing that prop lights the banner with no page rewrite**. The banner now shows the RECORDED substance · reaction · clinician-recorded severity, **every chip styled identically**, plus an **honest empty state** ("No allergies recorded for this patient") because *none recorded* ≠ *we did not look*; only `active` rows appear (matching the composition `AppointmentDetailController` already uses, so the two surfaces cannot disagree); **ordered by SUBSTANCE, never by severity** — ordering by badness would assert a priority the system has no business asserting. **I did NOT swap Patient 360's hero for S1** — the hero carries a status pill, flag chip and dental cross-link S1 has no props for, and replacing it would REGRESS the page; Patient 360 parity is PC.P3. **THE THREE NEW SHELLS** (`Components/Clinical/`): **N1 `ClinicalRailCard`** (titled box + slot + honest empty state; `count` is a caller-supplied STRING so it cannot be handed a number to compute on), **N3 `AccessLogRow`** (who · what · when · surface · basis — **basis is SERVER-DERIVED and merely printed; inferring it here would make an audit worthless**), **N6 `SignOffBar`** (chrome only — it renders the actions the caller passes and **performs NO signing logic**: no router, no form, no fetch; disabling a button hides an affordance, it is not a gate, per D-168). **THE FENCE SCAN NOW FOLLOWS THE HEADER:** moving S1 out of `Components/Dental/` would have silently dropped it from `SharedComponentsTest`'s glob — **a fence weakened not by editing an assertion but by relocating its subject**, invisible in a diff; both `dscPath()` and the recursive scan now resolve EITHER namespace, and the value was immediate (the severity-tint mutation below was caught by that very test). **Tests added:** `tests/Feature/Patients/PatientClinicalSharedTest.php` (7 tests, 276 assertions) — the promotion moved rather than copied and every dental caller follows it; the payload carries the REAL recorded rows with exact keys, substance ordering and inactive rows excluded; the honest empty list; `patient.view`-gated + tenant-scoped + one audit path; `Modules\Patients` still imports no Clinical; the three shells compute nothing; and the carried-forward fence scan. **Mutation-checked THREE ways** — tinting the chip by `allergy.severity`, adding a computed `crossReacts` payload field (caught twice: exact-keys + token scan), and giving N6 a `router.post` + `tone` prop — each turns the suite red. **Three test-side bugs of my own, all caught before green:** `recorded_by` needs a **StaffProfile**, not a User (the model asserts the reference); my arithmetic regex matched Tailwind's `border-line/60` opacity syntax, so it now reads the `<script>` block only (banning a class utility would teach the next author to weaken the scan — the D-166 `baseline` lesson); and `fall` as a squashed substring matched "fallback", so the token is now `fallrisk` (the judgment is a fall-RISK SCORE, not the word). **BROWSER-VERIFIED:** the dental header renders identically post-promotion; Patient 360 for Erika Baumgartner shows "Allergies: Penicillin · Anaphylaxis requiring adrenaline and admission. · severe" with ONE distinct chip style (no severity tint); Viktor Odermatt (no allergies) shows "No allergies recorded for this patient." and no banner. **VERIFY:** `npm run build` green · Pint passed · PHPStan L5 "No errors" · vitest 29 passed · `composer test:smoke` 4 passed · Patients+Clinical+Dental+Architecture 209 passed (incl. the arch test enforcing the Patients boundary) · full `composer check` green. - `de55f43` PC.P2 (second of the PC chain): Clinical — **Patient Chart visual parity** over the EXISTING chart backend, P0D.GU. **Opening:** top commit `c5f4772` (PC.P1), tree clean, Redis up, CI `completed / success` confirmed BEFORE starting; chart data verified BY QUERY (Erika Baumgartner: 6 encounters · 2 problems · 2 medications · 1 open recall · 3 vitals). **WHAT THE PAYLOAD ALREADY CARRIED** (so most of this gate was completion, not construction): allergies, the medication-safety seam's honest "not configured" state, vitals + the unified `vitalsHistory` ("raw values only (no bands/flags/scores/deltas)"), medications, documents, carePlans, referrals, recalls, orders, the AI summary panel over `ClinicalSummaryTool` (`autonomyCeiling: SUGGEST`, "ABSOLUTE CONSTRAINT: EXTRACTIVE") with source chips + explicit insert, and **full note version chains** with `amendment_reason` + per-version `edit_url` so v1 was always reachable; tabs, month grouping and encounter type filters existed too. **THE REAL DEFECT FIXED: the counts were `array.length` in Vue** — not cosmetic, because several lists are DELIBERATELY PARTIAL (`notes` carries head versions only, since superseded ones live in the version chain; `orders` is empty for an actor who may not see them), so counting the loaded array **under-reports the record to a clinician**. Counts are now computed SERVER-side from real rows (`counts.encounters/notes/problems/vitals/medications/documents/orders/referrals/openRecalls`), and `notes`/`orders` deliberately MIRROR their lists so a chip can never disagree with what sits under it; the now-superseded client-side `openRecalls` computed was **DELETED** rather than left beside it — a second divergent source of the same number is exactly the bug being removed. **BUILT:** find-in-chart (a plain case-insensitive substring match over content ALREADY LOADED — fetches nothing, ranks nothing, scores no relevance, reorders nothing, and says so on screen); recall proximity as **a plain calendar interval** (`due_in_days`, computed server-side on whole days, negative when past due) rendered "due in N days" / "N days past due" / "due today". **THE FENCE, unchanged and re-asserted:** vitals stay RAW (no band/flag/score/delta/trend/arrow/sparkline; nothing styled from a vital value or numeric threshold — D-169); the summary stays EXTRACTIVE at SUGGEST with per-line source chips and ONE explicit human insert (no new tool, no raised ceiling, no auto-insert); no risk score, acuity, EWS, prognosis, auto-problem-list or cross-reactivity; nothing draws (D-172). **Tests added:** `tests/Feature/Clinical/ChartParityTest.php` (5 tests, 181 assertions) — counts === real row counts with a note AMENDMENT proving the head-version case and v1 still reachable in the chain; recall proximity is a signed day interval carrying no urgency/priority/level and tinting nothing; the agent panel wraps the existing tool with exactly two `insertSummary` occurrences (definition + the human `@click`); and the vitals/fence re-assertions. **Mutation-checked FOUR ways** — a recall row tinted by proximity, a `band` key in the vitals payload, the tool's ceiling raised to APPROVE, and an auto-insert call site. **I SHIPPED A FALSE GREEN AND CAUGHT IT:** the vitals re-assertion passed the `band` mutation because my fixture recorded **NO VITALS** — an absence assertion over an EMPTY collection is vacuously true. The test now records two real vitals (including a frankly abnormal 176/104, SpO2 91), asserts `toHaveCount(2)` BEFORE scanning, and asserts the abnormal row's keys are exactly the recorded fields — so the fence is proven against precisely the data that would tempt an annotation; re-run against the mutation it now fails as it should. **Four fixture bugs from reading signatures rather than guessing:** `RecallRule` needs `criteria` and uses `interval_months` (not `interval_days`); `Encounter` fails closed without a `practitioner_id`; `Medication` requires `substance_key`; the note service is `saveDraft(encounter, author, sections, actor)` + `sign(note, user)` + `amend(note, changes, reason, author, actor)`, not `create(...)`. **BROWSER-VERIFIED:** the band reads "6 encounters · 2 problems · 2 medications" + "1 open recall" matching the queried counts exactly; tabs read Notes 6 · Problems 2 · Vitals 3 · Medications 2 · Documents 1 · Orders 0; find-in-chart narrows the timeline (the "AUGUST 2026" month group disappears when filtering to a type this patient has none of); the recall row reads "Jahreskontrolle 2026-08-21 · due today" with ONE distinct row style (no proximity tint); note states render and version history is present; and **no sparkline, no canvas/chart svg, no risk/acuity/EWS/prognosis anywhere**. **VERIFY:** `npm run build` green · Pint passed · PHPStan L5 "No errors" · vitest 29 passed · `composer test:smoke` 4 passed · full `composer check` green. - `1282f18` FENCE-AUDIT (no feature work — test-side hardening only, cashing in D-174): **every absence assertion in the suite inventoried, measured EMPIRICALLY, and the weak ones hardened**. **Opening:** top commit `de55f43` (PC.P2), tree clean, Redis up, CI `completed / success`. **INVENTORY: 85 absence/structural assertions** across 57 files — 47 inline `$forbidden` lists, 15 recursive payload scans, 9 directory-recursion source scans, 6 glob scans, 8 route-table scans. **METHOD — measured, not guessed:** static reading cannot tell you whether a scan ever inspected anything, so the suite was TEMPORARILY instrumented (every recursive helper logged the size of what it was handed; every glob logged its resolved file count), the Dental+Clinical+Hospital+Pharmacy+Billing suites run (410 passed), and the log read. **A first pass looked damning — three helpers appeared to have empty calls — but the probe was counting RECURSIVE DESCENTS into empty sub-arrays, not top-level invocations; a second depth-aware pass corrected it and TWO OF THE THREE FINDINGS EVAPORATED.** Measure the right thing before reporting a problem. **RESULT — 6 weak guards found, none of them hiding a real breach:** (1) of 15 recursive payload scans, **14 bite**; ONE had a vacuous call site — `DiagnosisTest`'s scan of the diagnosis term pick-list ran over an EMPTY list because the fixture never authored a term; (2) **four `*SourceContains` helpers returned `false` for a MISSING DIRECTORY**, so `expect(...)->toBeFalse()` passed having scanned nothing — the guard goes silent the moment its target moves (the D-173 shape); they protect **ALLERGY.P1** (no homemade drug-conflict logic) and three **ARDETAIL** money fences (record-payment, payment-plan, debt-enforcement agent-exclusion); (3) `Components/Clinical/*.vue` had **no positive control at all**, and the merged dental+clinical glob asserted only "not empty" — which still passed if ONE namespace stopped resolving, precisely the move it was written to survive. **THE KEY QUESTION — DID ANYTHING FORBIDDEN SLIP IN BEHIND A DEAD GUARD? NO.** Checked directly: `new SafetyAlert(` appears nowhere in `Modules/Pharmacy/src` or `Modules/Clinical/src` (ALLERGY.P1 holds in reality), and the terms payload emits only `id` + `label` with no suggestion/ranking key. The guards could not PROVE the fence; the fence itself was never breached. **FIXES (all strictly stronger, none weakened):** the four source-scan helpers now **THROW** when their target is missing instead of reporting "not found"; the term pick-list fixture now authors a REAL term — deliberately `Caries profunda`, naming the very finding just charted, i.e. exactly the case where a system that WANTED to suggest would surface it; the glob scans now assert their subjects resolved AND name the components that must be in the scan (`ToothArch`, `ClinicalStatTile`, `ProcedureCard`, `PatientClinicalHeader`, `ClinicalRailCard`, `AccessLogRow`, `SignOffBar`). **MUTATION-CHECKED FOUR WAYS, all previously silent, now loud:** a `suggested` key on the terms list; a simulated move of `Modules/Pharmacy/src`; a simulated move of `Components/Clinical/`; and one of the two component namespaces ceasing to resolve. **NO app behaviour changed** (the diff is 7 test files + AGENTS.md), **no assertion weakened**, no guard deleted. **The rule is now in AGENTS.md** so future gates write positive controls by default: prove the subject is non-empty, make the fixture representative of what would tempt the breach, mutation-check it — **a guard that has never been seen to fail is not yet a guard**. **VERIFY:** Pint passed · full `composer check` green.
- `<pending>` PC.P3 (third of the PC chain): Patients/app-layer — **Patient 360 visual parity, carried by an EXTENDED S1 rather than a forked one**, P0D.GU. **Opening:** top commit `1282f18` (FENCE-AUDIT), tree clean, Redis up, CI `completed / success` confirmed BEFORE starting; representative data verified BY QUERY (Erika Baumgartner `01m0jphz7rrqg2547z57ztwx77`, status active — 1 active allergy at severity **severe**, 3 contacts, 1 coverage, 2 consents, 6 encounters, 1 open recall). **S1 EXTENDED, NOT FORKED — one component, two surfaces:** the four new props (`status`, `links`, `variant`, `initials`) are all **OPTIONAL** and `compact` is the DEFAULT, so the four dental callers pass nothing and render exactly as before. **The hero is ADDITIVE decoration** — an absolutely-positioned initials avatar and watermark plus class BINDINGS — because my first attempt wrapped the band in an avatar row, which would have changed the COMPACT DOM and broken the byte-identity PC.P1 established; **caught before it was built** and rewritten. Proven twice: the dental header markup is **381 chars IDENTICAL** before/after, and in a live browser the dental band's root class string (`euca-tile-dark rounded-2xl p-5 text-white`), its `text-2xl` name and the ABSENCE of avatar/watermark are unchanged. **THE ONE FAKED THING ON THIS SCREEN, FOUND AND REMOVED (D-176):** the hero carried `⚑ {{ t('patients.show.headerFlag') }}` as a **hardcoded, unbound span rendered for EVERY patient** — `Patient` has **no flag column, no fillable attribute and no migration** anywhere in CareOS, so the chip asserted a documented fact that does not exist. Unlike every other omission in this programme (an absence the user can SEE), this was **a presence the user cannot distinguish from a real one**. It is **omitted, not backfilled**: deriving a flag would be a computed risk marker (the fence); defaulting a boolean column would be a clinical fact with no author, reason or timestamp. The i18n key was **deleted with it** (a live string invites re-rendering) and the header deliberately grew **NO `flag` prop** — a prop is an affordance and the next author would fill it. **The gap is RECORDED, not closed**; closing it needs its own gate (a recorded flag with author + reason + timestamp). **COUNTING MOVED SERVER-SIDE AHEAD OF THE DEFECT, not behind it:** `PatientAccessReport::forPatient()` was verified **uncapped**, so today's Vue lengths were accurate — but that is a property of today's payload, not a guarantee, and it is exactly the assumption that broke on the chart at P2; `counts.{contacts,coverages,consents,identifiers,allergies,accessLog}` are now counted from real rows, with allergies counting **active only**. **ONE FIELD THE HERO NO LONGER SHOWS, STATED NOT GLOSSED:** the old bespoke band carried a `preferred_language` chip the shared header has no prop for; it is **not lost** (the Demographics tab on the same page still renders it, untouched) and the wireframe hero does not draw language either — but the band shows one fewer fact than before, and widening S1 for a single caller was the wrong reflex. **THE FENCE:** no risk score, acuity, EWS, prognosis, priority or severity-keyed styling on any file this gate moved (D-173: the extended header, `Show.vue`, `PatientShowController`); the allergy chips keep **one constant class string** for mild and severe alike and stay **ordered by substance, never severity** (D-169). **Tests added:** `tests/Feature/Patients/Patient360ParityTest.php` (7 tests, 194 assertions), with **positive controls throughout** (D-174) — the fixture is representative and non-empty (every tab has rows; a SEVERE allergy and an INACTIVE one are both present, i.e. precisely the data that would tempt a tint or a rank), each scan asserts its subject resolved before scanning, and the dental-caller check first proves the page actually renders the header. **Mutation-checked FIVE ways** — a reintroduced flag chip, severity-keyed chip styling, a page-side `.length` count, a dental caller made `variant="hero"`, and a `severityTone` token in the header — **each turns exactly one test red**, and the tree reverts clean. **A SECOND GUARD BIT, and it was a real regression: the a11y `<h1>` check.** `a11y-markup.test.ts` asserted `Patients/Show.vue` contains `<h1`; the hero moved the heading INTO the shared header, so the page source no longer has one — the RENDERED outline is unchanged, but the assertion had lost its subject (the D-173 shape again, this time in vitest). **Followed rather than relaxed:** the page must render the shared header AND must not add a competing `<h1>`, and the header must carry **exactly one** — strictly stronger than the original `toContain('<h1')`, which a second heading would have passed. Mutation-checked twice, and the FIRST version of the fix was too loose (`toContain('<PatientClinicalHeader')` still matched a renamed `<PatientClinicalHeaderX`), so it is now a tag-boundary regex — caught by running the mutation rather than by reading the assertion. **A guard bit me unprompted:** the raw-source glyph scan caught the `⚑` inside my own explanatory COMMENT, so the prose was reworded rather than the assertion relaxed — the check stays at full strictness on raw source. **`composer check` exit code LIED AGAIN (exit 0, fatal inside):** `p3Ctx()` collided with `CrossTenantIsolationTest` — invisible in a single-file run, fatal across the suite (the `ppCtx` lesson, second time); helpers renamed `p360*` and **every declared helper verified globally unique**, then Patients+Security run TOGETHER (72 passed) to prove it. **BROWSER-VERIFIED both surfaces:** 360 renders "EB · Erika Baumgartner · MRN-000001 · active · 1954-03-12 · 72 y · female · Penicillin · Anaphylaxis… · severe · Dental chart →" with ONE distinct chip style, tab chips reading the server figures (Contacts 3 · Coverages 1 · Consents 2 · Access log 1), **no flag glyph anywhere in the DOM** and no risk/acuity/priority language; the dental chart's compact band is unchanged. **VERIFY:** `npm run build` green · Pint passed · PHPStan L5 "No errors" · Patients+Clinical+Dental+Security 197 passed · full `composer check` green (on the re-run, after the log TEXT was read). Docs: `PATIENTS-CLINICAL-BATCH-DIFF.md` §7 PC.P3 marked DONE + §7c outcome + the flag chip recorded in §5.1. **Also backfilled the 9 stale `<pending>` markers** in this log (DENTAL-B.P1–P6, PC.P1, PC.P2, FENCE-AUDIT) with their real commit hashes, each verified against `git log`.
- `<pending>` PC.P4 (fourth of the PC chain): Clinical — **Note Editor visual parity over the EXISTING note backend**, P0D.GU. **Opening:** top commit `b3a0961` (PC.P3), tree clean, Redis PONG, CI `completed / success` confirmed BEFORE starting. Fixture re-seeded and **verified BY QUERY**, deliberately representative (D-174): a DRAFT (one required section empty — the "2 of 3 filled" case), a **SIGNED** note (`signed_by=2`, real timestamp), its **AMENDMENT** (`v2 draft supersedes v1`, reason recorded) with the chain reading `v1:signed -> v2:draft`, plus a **SEVERE** recorded allergy (Penicillin) — i.e. exactly the data that would tempt an auto-sign, a silent in-place edit or a computed judgment. **THE REAL LIFECYCLE:** `saveDraft` (`note.write`, drafts only) → `sign` (`note.sign`, records `signed_at`/`signed_by`, re-validates the template's required sections, dispatches `ClinicalNoteSigned`) → `amend` (`note.write`, signed notes only, requires a reason, creates a **NEW note at version+1 with `supersedes_id`, itself a DRAFT**); the model blocks any update whose original status was signed and blocks deleting a signed note, with DB triggers behind it; `versionsFor` walks to the root and forward, so **v1 is always reachable**. **THE AUDIT ROW WAS WRONG AND THE REPO SAID SO:** it listed the type-to-confirm modal, the required-section counter and the autosave chip as deltas, but all three were **already live** — `CLINIC.W5` (`1e4b7c0`) predates the audit commit `e2cb9ba`. Checking reality rather than the audit turned "build all of it" into four narrow real deltas; §2 of the diff doc is corrected. **THE ASSIST PANEL IS OMITTED — TWO INDEPENDENT REASONS (D-177).** (1) **No rephrase/authoring capability exists**: ten AiCore tools, NONE touching note prose; the only clinical one is `ClinicalSummaryTool`, EXTRACTIVE at a **SUGGEST** ceiling, and it lives on the CHART where PC.P2 wired it. (2) **The decoded wireframe draws no assist panel at all** — zero occurrences of assist/rephrase/AI/agent/suggest/summarize (the only "transcri"/"scribe" hits are "transcription error" and "describe"). So building one would not be parity; it would be **inventing an agent surface beside a legal clinical record that neither the mock nor the backend asks for**. The extractive summary was **deliberately not duplicated** onto the authoring page — a content-producing affordance next to the note body is precisely what this screen must not have. **NO new tool, NO ceiling raised, NOTHING auto-inserted, NOTHING auto-signed, NO clinical content authored by the system**; the page states this on screen. **BUILT (the four real deltas):** per-section `required · filled` / `required · empty` / `optional` markers + S/O/A/P letters, with **every marker carrying identical classes** so the WORD states the state and nothing is tinted by it (D-169 — and emptiness of a text box is an administrative fact, not a clinical value); the **superseded-version banner** ("Viewing v1 — a newer version exists" + a link back to the current version), computed server-side from the existing chain, **display-only, changing no path**; the **shared N6 sign-off bar** adopted — it performs no signing logic and receives the readiness line as a PRE-COMPOSED STRING (it counts nothing); and the **dormant allergy banner lit** — the editor has carried a declared-but-unsent `allergies` prop since it was built, the same gap PC.P1 closed on Patient 360, with **no boundary question** since `Allergy` is Clinical's own model (active only, ordered by SUBSTANCE never severity, constant chip styling). **FLAGGED NOT CHANGED:** the mock says template prefill "renders placeholder-style until edited; it never autosaves as content", while live applies template defaults as real content at creation — a behaviour change to an existing path with existing tests, so it is recorded rather than made. **Tests added:** `tests/Feature/Clinical/NoteEditorParityTest.php` (7 tests, 385 assertions) — `ClinicalNoteTest` covers the backend invariants and was **NOT modified**, so these assert the parity surface instead: a signed note refused **through the ROUTE** with every field byte-identical afterwards; the amend path superseding with v1 reachable and `chain.is_superseded` true on v1 / false on v2; signing gated (`reception` refused, clinician records who + when); the **D-170 absence** of any authoring tool with a positive control over the real ten-tool fleet; no assist affordance and no auto-insert/auto-sign; the fence over a NON-EMPTY payload; and allergies as recorded facts. **MUTATION-CHECKED SEVEN WAYS — AND ONE PASSED, WHICH WAS THE POINT.** Auto-sign on a timer, a signed note made editable in place, a `generatedAssessment` payload key, a severity-tinted allergy banner, a suppressed superseded banner and an invented `RephraseNoteTool` all turned the suite red — but **auto-insert via a NEW WATCHER writing straight into a SOAP section passed green**, because my guard counted `insertSnippet` CALL SITES rather than bounding the WRITE SURFACE. That is exactly how auto-authored text would arrive. The guard now pins **one watcher and exactly one assignment into a SOAP section anywhere on the page**; re-run, the mutation fails. **A harness bug of my own, caught immediately:** a mutation that replaced a block with a bare newline reverted by searching for that newline and matched the first one in the file, corrupting the controller — restored from git, edits re-applied, `php -l` clean, and the remaining mutations reverted by file copy instead. **A SELF-INFLICTED RED HERRING WORTH REMEMBERING:** I ran a foreground `pest` while `composer check` was running in the background, and BOTH went red with `QueryException` across dozens of unrelated auth tests. Nothing was broken — the dev MySQL is shared with the test suite, and two `RefreshDatabase` processes migrating concurrently wipe each other's schema mid-run. Neither result was evidence of anything. **Never run a second DB-touching suite while `composer check` is in flight**; re-run sequentially and the same code is green. **BROWSER-VERIFIED all three states:** the template draft shows `Required · filled / Required · filled / Required · empty / optional` with **ONE distinct marker style**, S/O/A/P letters, the lit Penicillin banner, the N6 bar reading "Sign this note — 2 of 3 required sections filled — still to fill: Assessment", and the ONLY sentence mentioning rephrase/suggest anywhere on the page is the disclaimer stating their absence; the signed superseded v1 shows the banner + "Return to the current version (v2)", **ZERO editable SOAP boxes** (the single textarea is the amend reason), no delete affordance, and both versions in the rail. **VERIFY:** `npm run build` green · vitest 29 passed · Clinical note suites 17 passed (7 new + the 10 untouched) · Pint · PHPStan L5 · full `composer check` green · `composer test:smoke` green. - `<pending>` PC.P5 (fifth of the PC chain): Patients/app-layer — **the dedicated patient access log + the nDSG Art. 25 / GDPR Art. 15 subject-access export**. **Opening:** top commit `4105177` (PC.P4), tree clean, Redis PONG, CI `completed / success` confirmed BEFORE starting. Fixture seeded and **verified BY QUERY**, deliberately MULTI-ACTOR (D-174): 5 patient-scoped reads spanning `user` (staff), `user` + an agent surface, `patient` (portal), `service` (no actor id) and `operator` — plus a tenant-scoped `operator.access` row with NO patient_id, i.e. the exact shape of the gap. Chain verified `ok=true` over 339 events. **THE READ-AUDIT STUDY — every writer enumerated.** Disclosure reads all run through `LogsReads::auditRead()` → `AuditService::recordRead()`, writing `action = 'read'` with the patient id, from **~65 call sites** across Clinical, Billing, Portal, Dental, Nursing, Comms, Lab, Radiology, Surgery, Pharmacy, Hospital, ED and the ten AI tools. `PlatformAuditContext::actor()` yields `user` / `patient` / `service`; agent reads carry the acting user's identity with a SURFACE that names the tool (`clinical_summary_agent`), so the agent marker is read off the recorded surface, never inferred. **THE GAP FOUND AND REPORTED (not faked):** platform-support events are written to the target tenant's ledger with `actor_type = 'operator'` but under action `operator.access` and **no `patient_id`** — they record that the platform touched the CLINIC, not that anyone read a given patient, so no query can attribute them to a patient without inventing a link. Second, subtler half: if an operator user read patient data through a normal controller, the audit context would label it `user`, indistinguishable from clinic staff. **Mitigating:** Operator Mode is deliberately inert (no HTTP route, no UI — D-164), so neither path is reachable today; closing it needs an OPMODE gate, not a parity gate. The screen **states the limitation in words** rather than implying exhaustiveness. **BUILT:** `GET /patients/{patient}/access-log` + `/export`, gated **`audit.view` + `patient.view`** — the REAL permission is `audit.view` (the audit doc's `patient.audit.view` does not exist; corrected in §2), held by `org_admin` and `him_records`, and requiring both is **strictly tighter than the Patient 360 tab**, which any `patient.view` user still sees. Newest-first, day-grouped rows via the shared **N3 `AccessLogRow`** (PC.P1) showing who (resolved to a name), the recorded actor type, the surface and the time; filters over **real recorded values only** — a calendar range and actor-type chips built from the types ACTUALLY PRESENT, never a hardcoded taxonomy. **Deliberately NOT built:** the mock's "only notable" filter (deciding which reads deserve suspicion is a judgment) and its out-of-branch facet (needs the actor's branch AT THE TIME, which the row does not record); and the mock's "care team vs reception" basis split, which needs the actor's ROLE AT THE TIME — deriving it from today's role would be a fabrication about a past event, so the RECORDED actor type is shown instead. **COMPLETENESS IS STRUCTURAL:** the report filters on patient + action ONLY, with no actor-type/surface/recency whitelist, and there is **exactly ONE query** in the class — `forPatient()` (the 360 tab, oldest-first, unchanged) and `forPatientNewestFirst()` (screen + export) differ only in order and filters. **THE EXPORT is CSV** (the format a patient can open and a regulator expects), calls the SAME method with the SAME filters as the screen, and **audits itself** — its row is written before the query runs, so the export documents its own disclosure and appears in its own file. **A REAL BUG THE MULTI-ACTOR FIXTURE CAUGHT:** the distinct-actor headline used `COUNT(DISTINCT actor_type, actor_id)`, and MySQL drops a row from that when EITHER value is NULL — so the SYSTEM read (legitimately no actor id) was **silently uncounted**: precisely the reader a patient is least likely to know about, missing from the headline. Fixed with a COALESCE'd concat, 3 → 4. A single-actor fixture could never have shown it. **Tests added:** `tests/Feature/Patients/PatientAccessLogTest.php` (7 tests, 173 assertions) — the COMPLETENESS proof (every actor type appears), the structural no-whitelist proof, export ⊇ screen with all four actor types, both surfaces self-audited through ONE path, `audit.view` gating with a doctor refused while 360 still works (positive control) and cross-tenant 404, chain integrity + no write/update/delete affordance in either new file, and the fence over a non-empty multi-actor payload. **MUTATION-CHECKED SEVEN WAYS, all red:** operator reads excluded (4 tests red), system reads excluded (4 red), the export silently truncated, the export not auditing itself, the `audit.view` gate dropped, a computed `suspicious` flag, and operator rows tinted. **D-169 in the browser:** all four actor types render with **exactly ONE basis-chip style** — an operator read looks identical to a receptionist read. **AN EXISTING GUARD FIRED AND WAS RIGHT TO:** `OperatorGrantAccessTest` pins the complete list of files that may reference an operator grant, and my new **docblock prose** named `OperatorGrantService`. The file has no operator capability at all, so the fix was to **reword the comment — NOT to add the file to the expected list**, which would have normalised new entries into a deliberately closed set. **BROWSER-VERIFIED:** 6 accesses by 4 distinct actors, chips reading Operator mode 1 · Patient (self) 1 · System 2 · Staff user 2, newest-first with the page's own render already at the top; the CSV downloaded with all four actor types plus its own `patient_access_log_export` row; a doctor got **403** on both log and export while Patient 360 still returned **200**; a bogus/cross-tenant id **404**ed on both; no suspicious/anomaly/risk language anywhere. **Pint exit-code lied a THIRD time** (wrapper exit 0, `pint --test` failed on the new controller) — caught by reading the log text, fixed, plus one PHPStan `notIdentical.alwaysTrue` on a non-nullable `display_name`. **VERIFY:** `npm run build` green · vitest 29 passed · Patients+Audit 66 passed · Security+Platform 116 passed (incl. the operator suite) · Pint passed · PHPStan L5 "No errors" · full `composer check` green · `composer test:smoke` green. Fixture seeded and **verified BY QUERY**, representative (D-174): a referral in **every real state** (`{"draft":1,"sent":1,"accepted":1,"completed":1,"declined":1}`) plus a **SEVERE** recorded allergy — the data that tempts a rank or a tint. **THE REAL BACKEND:** `Referral` records `direction`, `to_provider_name` (**FREE TEXT**), an optional internal `to_branch_id`, `specialty`, the clinician's `reason`, `status`, `sent_at`, `responded_at`, `notes`. `ReferralService` owns the state machine — **draft → sent → accepted|declined → completed** — gates EVERY write on `note.write` and audits each transition via `ClinicalRecordChanged`. Reads are audited via `LogsReads::auditRead()`. **There was no route and no Vue page**, so the audit was right that this is net-new UI. **THE BIGGEST FINDING — `send()` TRANSMITS NOTHING.** It sets `status` and `sent_at`, full stop: no channel, no message, no document, no integration anywhere (`ReferralService` is the ONLY file in the repo that touches referrals). So "sent" means **the clinician recorded that they sent it**, and the screen says exactly that (D-179): the button reads **"Mark as sent"**, the N6 bar reads "Recording that you sent it. CareOS does not transmit the referral", and the rail states it again. A button labelled "Send" would have asserted an action CareOS never took — the D-176 defect shape applied to a VERB. **FOUR MORE DRAWN-BUT-UNBACKED THINGS, OMITTED + STATED ON SCREEN (D-170):** **urgency** ("soon · within 1 week") — no column, nothing records one, and inventing it would have handed the UI a value to rank and tint by, which is exactly the fence; the **shared/withheld document packet** — referrals have NO attachment relation; a **provider directory** with known partners and triage times — `to_provider_name` is free text and was KEPT free text; and the **agent that "assembles, sends, tracks and chases"** — no agent tool touches referrals (the single grep hit is `ClassifyDocumentTool` mapping the word "referral" in a FILENAME to the letter document category — unrelated to the workflow). Also omitted: the mock's **tracking states** (Received · Appt booking · Report back), which have no backing; the five real states are rendered instead. **BUILT:** `clinical/patients/{patient}/referrals` — compose (recipient, specialty, internal branch, the clinician's own reason, notes) plus every REAL transition, each posting to a route that calls the existing service. **The controller writes no status**: a test proves it contains no `save`/`update`/`forceFill`/`DB::table` at all, and the service's rules still bite through the new routes (re-sending a sent referral and responding to a draft both fail). S1 header + N1 rail + N6 sign-off bar reused. Ordered **newest-first by a recorded timestamp**, never by importance, and **every status pill carries identical classes** — a declined referral is not painted alarming, a completed one not reassuring (D-169). **THE DISCLOSURE IS AUDITED ON THE EXISTING PATH:** opening the screen writes exactly **ONE** read row (`surface: referrals`) — not one per referral — so it appears in the PC.P5 access log; verified end to end in the browser (the CSV export shows the `referrals` surface row). No second audit path. **Tests added:** `tests/Feature/Clinical/ReferralOutTest.php` (8 tests, 178 assertions) — `ReferralRecallTest` covers the backend invariants and was **NOT modified**. They pin: real referrals in real states + honest empty state; transitions through the service only; the single-row disclosure; **the screen and the CHART RAIL agreeing** (same ids, same statuses — one backend, two views); gating + tenancy fail-closed; the D-170 absences with schema-level positive controls (no urgency/priority/triage column, no provider-directory or attachment table, no agent tool reaching referrals); and the fence over a non-empty payload. **MUTATION-CHECKED SEVEN WAYS, all red:** a page-side state write (2 tests), a computed urgency, a suggested specialist, status tinting, the disclosure un-audited, a SECOND audit path, and drafts quietly hidden from the screen (3 tests). **TWO CORRECTIONS FROM CHECKING RATHER THAN ASSUMING:** the service's `note.write` refusal surfaces as **403, not 500** (`AuthorizationException`), and reception **does** hold `patient.view` — so it reaches the screen read-only and is refused at the write, which is the sharper proof and is what the test now asserts (plus `can_write: false` in the payload). And the fence scan initially failed on the i18n key **`scopeUrgency`** — the on-screen sentence stating that urgency does NOT exist; those disclaimer references are now stripped for the same reason comments are (the P1/P3/P5 lesson, fourth time), while their TEXT is asserted verbatim by the D-170 test so nothing is hidden. **One doc-only change to an existing model:** `Referral`'s PHPDoc lacked `created_at`/`updated_at` (the migration has `timestamps()`), which PHPStan flagged; documented, no behaviour change. **BROWSER-VERIFIED:** all 5 referrals render in their 5 real states with **ONE distinct status-pill style**; the draft transitioned **Draft → Sent** through the real service and "Marked sent" appeared; the allergy chip is lit; the transmit disclaimer is on screen; the access-log CSV contains the `referrals` disclosure row (one per render); and there is no urgency, priority, triage or ranked specialist anywhere outside the disclaimers. **VERIFY:** `npm run build` green · vitest 29 passed · Clinical+Patients 140 passed · Pint passed · PHPStan L5 "No errors" · full `composer check` green · `composer test:smoke` green. - `<pending>` PC.P7 (seventh and FINAL CORE gate of the PC chain — **the PATIENTS & CLINICAL batch core is now COMPLETE**): Clinical — **the recall due list, a worklist over the EXISTING recall backend**, P0D.GU. **Opening:** top commit `caa8023` (PC.P6), tree clean, Redis PONG, CI `completed / success` confirmed BEFORE starting. Fixture seeded and **verified BY QUERY** with a deliberate SPREAD (D-174): due dates from **-200 to +120 days** across all five real statuses (`{"completed":1,"dismissed":1,"due":7,"contacted":1,"booked":1}`) — precisely the spread that tempts a severity band. **THE REAL BACKEND:** `Recall` holds `patient_id`, `rule_id`, `due_on` (a DATE) and `status`, and **has NO priority column** — the schema-level fact the fence rests on. `RecallService::transition()` owns the legal graph (due → contacted|booked|completed|dismissed; contacted → booked|completed|dismissed; booked → completed|dismissed), gates on `note.write` and audits each move via `ClinicalRecordChanged`. **Completing a recall closes the recall — it books NOTHING**, so this gate touches no scheduling path (asserted: the controller mentions no appointment/slot-finder/overlap guard). There was no route and no page. **THE ORDERING LINE (D-180):** rows are `due_on` ASC, so the longest overdue leads **as a consequence of sorting a recorded date, not of scoring anything** — stated in the controller docblock, stated on the page ("it is a date sort, not a priority ranking"), and PROVEN by a test showing the sequence is reproduced exactly by sorting the payload on `due_on` alone. `due_in_days` reuses PC.P2's formulation unchanged, so the worklist and the chart rail agree. **THE MOCK'S CENTRAL CLAIM IS REFUSED — "sends routine ones automatically at Level 1".** The existing `clinical.draft_recall_message` tool is capped at **SUGGEST**, so `AgentRuntime::runTool()` can only reach its `propose()` branch: **auto-send is structurally unreachable, not merely switched off** — and that ceiling is exactly what made wiring the tool safe in a parity gate (the ceiling, not the UI, is the guarantee). The tool WAS wired (it genuinely exists — D-170 satisfied): the clinician supplies the wording, the tool substitutes recorded name/rule/due-date, **refuses medical advice**, returns `blocked_no_comms_consent` without consent, always sets `sent: false` + `human_handoff: true`, and the draft waits in the capped approval queue for a human to send. **Nothing is sent from this screen; no ceiling raised.** **ALSO REFUSED:** the mock's "hands clinical cases to a person" triage (perio maintenance, phone-only) — an agent deciding which recalls are clinically sensitive is a computed judgment about patients; every recall here is handled by a human, and the page says so. Its "needs review" column and per-row "Sent · L1" status go with it. **A REAL FINDING: `FollowUpAgent::draftRecallMessage()` HAD NO CALLER** — the tool, the agent wrapper and the AgentRegistry entry all existed but nothing invoked them; the capability was real and unreachable. This gate gave it its first, human-initiated, draft-only entry point. **BUILT:** `/clinical/recalls` — the tenant's recalls with patient, rule name, `due_on`, the plain interval, status and recorded comms consent; filters over REAL attributes only (status, the tenant's OWN rules, a plain calendar window) with **no priority filter, because there is no priority**; every transition posting to a route that calls the service (the controller contains no `save`/`update`/`forceFill`); N1 rail stating what the screen does not do. **AUDIT — a DELIBERATE DEPARTURE, ARGUED:** PC.P1/P5/P6 write ONE read row per render because those screens show ONE patient; a worklist shows many, so a single row would leave most of those patients' access logs SILENT about a real disclosure. This screen writes **one row per patient shown**, through the same `auditRead()` path — one mechanism, correct granularity. Verified in the DB: 7 patients × 2 renders = 14 rows, exactly one each. **Tests added:** `tests/Feature/Clinical/RecallWorklistTest.php` (8 tests, 323 assertions) — `ReferralRecallTest` covers the engine/lifecycle and was **NOT modified**. **MUTATION-CHECKED SIX WAYS, all red:** an overdue tint (the fence this screen exists to pass), a computed priority + urgency band (2 tests), ordering by status instead of the date, a page-side state write (2 tests), the disclosure un-audited, and a no-show risk key. **One test-side bug of my own:** `AutonomyPolicy::SUGGEST`/`AUTO` are STRINGS, so comparing them directly compares alphabetically and proves nothing — the assertion now uses `AutonomyPolicy::LEVELS`, the rank map the cap itself uses. **BROWSER-VERIFIED:** 11 rows ordered -200 → +120; **every row resolved to ONE class string and ONE background**, every status pill to ONE style; a live Due → Contacted transition through the real service; the order/no-auto-send statements on screen; and the only judgment words anywhere are the two disclaimers stating their own absence. **VERIFY:** `npm run build` green · vitest 29 passed · Clinical+Patients+AiCore 204 passed · Pint passed · PHPStan L5 "No errors" · full `composer check` green · `composer test:smoke` green.
